\displaystyle\quad{}=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k_{r^{\prime}}}_{r},d}}\frac{\prod_{i=1}^{r^{\prime}-1}\prod_{j=1}^{r^{\prime\prime}}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(i+j-1)\right)_{k_{i}+l_{j}}}{\prod_{i=1}^{r^{\prime}}\prod_{j=1}^{r^{\prime\prime}+1}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(i+j-2)\right)_{k_{i}+l_{j}}}\frac{\prod_{i=1}^{r^{\prime}}\prod_{j=1}^{r^{\prime\prime}+1}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(i+j-2)\right)_{k_{r^{\prime}}}}{\prod_{i=2}^{r^{\prime}}\prod_{j=1}^{r^{\prime\prime}}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(i+j-2)\right)_{k_{r^{\prime}}}}

\displaystyle\quad{}\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\frac{\prod_{j=1}^{r^{\prime\prime}}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(r^{\prime}+j-1)\right)_{k_{r^{\prime}}+l_{j}}}{\prod_{j=1}^{r^{\prime\prime}+1}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}((r^{\prime}+1)+j-2)\right)_{k_{r^{\prime}+1}+l_{j}}}\Delta^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}_{\mathbf{k}}(z_{11})\Delta^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}_{\mathbf{l}}(z_{22})

\displaystyle\quad{}=\frac{1}{\prod_{j=1}^{r}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(j-1)\right)_{k_{r^{\prime}}}}\frac{\prod_{i=1}^{r^{\prime}}\prod_{j=1}^{r^{\prime\prime}}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(i+j-1)\right)_{k_{i}+l_{j}}}{\prod_{i=1}^{r^{\prime}+1}\prod_{j=1}^{r^{\prime\prime}+1}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(i+j-2)\right)_{k_{i}+l_{j}}}

\displaystyle\quad{}\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\prod_{j=1}^{r^{\prime\prime}}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(j-1)\right)_{k_{r^{\prime}}}\prod_{i=1}^{r^{\prime}}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(i+r^{\prime\prime}-1)\right)_{k_{r^{\prime}}}\Delta^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}_{\mathbf{k}}(z_{11})\Delta^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}_{\mathbf{l}}(z_{22})

\displaystyle\quad{}=\frac{\prod_{i=1}^{r^{\prime}}\prod_{j=1}^{r^{\prime\prime}}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(i+j-1)\right)_{k_{i}+l_{j}}}{\prod_{i=1}^{r^{\prime}+1}\prod_{j=1}^{r^{\prime\prime}+1}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(i+j-2)\right)_{k_{i}+l_{j}}}\Delta^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}_{\mathbf{k}}(z_{11})\Delta^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}_{\mathbf{l}}(z_{22}),

where we have used the induction hypothesis and Proposition
3.1
at the 2nd equality, and (
2.6
) at the 3rd equality.
Therefore, the theorem holds for all
r^{\prime}
.
∎

Proof of Theorem
5.2
.

Again, we may assume
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}
are of tube type, and
f(x_{11})=\Delta^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}_{\mathbf{k}}(x_{11})
,
g(x_{22})=\Delta^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}_{\mathbf{l}}(x_{22})
.
We prove the theorem by induction on
(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime})
. If
r^{\prime}=0
or
r^{\prime\prime}=0
, then this is true by Corollary
2.4
.
Next we assume that the theorem holds for
(r^{\prime}-1,r^{\prime\prime})
and
(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime}-1)
, and prove for
(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime})
.
First, by (
3.4
), we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{22}}\right)^{k_{r^{\prime}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{\lambda+k_{r^{\prime}}-\frac{n}{r}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times(\lambda+k_{r^{\prime}})_{\phi_{0}\bigl(\mathbf{k}-\underline{k_{r^{\prime}}}_{r^{\prime}},\mathbf{l}\bigr),d}\,\bigl\langle\Delta^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}_{\mathbf{k}-\underline{k_{r^{\prime}}}_{r^{\prime}}}(x_{11})\Delta^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}_{\mathbf{l}}(x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda+k_{r^{\prime}},x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=(\lambda)_{\underline{k_{r^{\prime}}}_{r},d}(\lambda+k_{r^{\prime}})_{\phi_{0}\bigl(\mathbf{k}-\underline{k_{r^{\prime}}}_{r^{\prime}},\mathbf{l}\bigr),d}\,\bigl\langle\Delta^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}_{\mathbf{k}}(x_{11})\Delta^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}_{\mathbf{l}}(x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=(\lambda)_{\phi_{0}\bigl(\mathbf{k}-\underline{k_{r^{\prime}}}_{r^{\prime}},\mathbf{l}\bigr)+\underline{k_{r^{\prime}}}_{r},d}\,\bigl\langle\Delta^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}_{\mathbf{k}}(x_{11})\Delta^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}_{\mathbf{l}}(x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x},

and by Proposition
3.1
and the induction hypothesis for
(r^{\prime}-1,r^{\prime\prime})
,
this is holomorphically continued for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
. Similarly, by (
3.5
), we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{11}}\right)^{l_{r^{\prime\prime}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{\lambda+l_{r^{\prime\prime}}-\frac{n}{r}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times(\lambda+l_{r^{\prime\prime}})_{\phi_{0}\bigl(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}-\underline{l_{r^{\prime\prime}}}_{r^{\prime\prime}}\bigr),d}\,\bigl\langle\Delta^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}_{\mathbf{k}}(x_{11})\Delta^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}_{\mathbf{l}-\underline{l_{r^{\prime\prime}}}_{r^{\prime\prime}}}(x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda+l_{r^{\prime\prime}},x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=(\lambda)_{\underline{l_{r^{\prime\prime}}}_{r},d}(\lambda+l_{r^{\prime\prime}})_{\phi_{0}\bigl(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}-\underline{l_{r^{\prime\prime}}}_{r^{\prime\prime}}\bigr),d}\,\bigl\langle\Delta^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}_{\mathbf{k}}(x_{11})\Delta^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}_{\mathbf{l}}(x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=(\lambda)_{\phi_{0}\bigl(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}-\underline{l_{r^{\prime\prime}}}_{r^{\prime\prime}}\bigr)+\underline{l_{r^{\prime\prime}}}_{r},d}\,\bigl\langle\Delta^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}_{\mathbf{k}}(x_{11})\Delta^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}_{\mathbf{l}}(x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x},

and again by Proposition
3.1
and the induction hypothesis for
(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime}-1)
,
this is holomorphically continued for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
. Now, the greatest common divisor of

\displaystyle(\lambda)_{\phi_{0}\bigl(\mathbf{k}-\underline{k_{r^{\prime}}}_{r^{\prime}},\mathbf{l}\bigr)+\underline{k_{r^{\prime}}}_{r},d}

\displaystyle=\prod_{a=1}^{r}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{\min\{k_{i}+l_{j}\mid\,1\leq i\leq r^{\prime},\,1\leq j\leq r^{\prime\prime}+1,\,i+j=a+1\}},

\displaystyle(\lambda)_{\phi_{0}\bigl(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}-\underline{l_{r^{\prime\prime}}}_{r^{\prime\prime}}\bigr)+\underline{l_{r^{\prime\prime}}}_{r},d}

\displaystyle=\prod_{a=1}^{r}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{\min\{k_{i}+l_{j}\mid\,1\leq i\leq r^{\prime}+1,\,1\leq j\leq r^{\prime\prime},\,i+j=a+1\}}

is

(\lambda)_{\phi_{0}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}),d}=\prod_{a=1}^{r}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{\min\{k_{i}+l_{j}\mid\,1\leq i\leq r^{\prime}+1,\,1\leq j\leq r^{\prime\prime}+1,\,i+j=a+1\}},

and hence
(\lambda)_{\phi_{0}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}),d}\,\bigl\langle\Delta^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}_{\mathbf{k}}(x_{11})\Delta^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}_{\mathbf{l}}(x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x}
is
holomorphically continued for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
. This completes the proof of Theorem
5.2
.
∎

By Theorem
5.1
and Proposition
2.6
(1), we get the following.

Corollary 5.4
.

Let
\operatorname{Re}\lambda>p-1
,
\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime}}
,
\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}
, and let
f(x_{11})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\bigr)
,
g(x_{22})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\bigr)
. Then we have

\|f(x_{11})g(x_{22})\|_{\lambda,x}^{2}=C_{0}^{d}(\lambda,\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})\|f(z_{11})g(z_{22})\|_{F,z}^{2},

where
C_{0}^{d}(\lambda,\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})
is as in (
5.1
).

Next we give a rough estimate of zeroes of
(\lambda)_{\phi_{0}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}),d}C_{0}^{d}(\lambda,\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})
.

Proposition 5.5
.

For
\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime}}
,
\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}
, let
C_{0}^{d}(\lambda,\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})
,
\phi_{0}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})
be as in (
5.1
), (
5.2
).
For
{1\leq m_{1}^{\prime}\leq m_{2}^{\prime}\leq r^{\prime}}
,
1\leq m_{1}^{\prime\prime}\leq m_{2}^{\prime\prime}\leq r^{\prime\prime}
, if
k_{1}=\cdots=k_{m_{1}^{\prime}}
,
l_{1}=\cdots=l_{m_{1}^{\prime\prime}}
and
{k_{m_{2}^{\prime}+1}\!=l_{m_{2}^{\prime\prime}+1}\!=0}
, then we have

\displaystyle\big\{\lambda\in\mathbb{C}\mid(\lambda)_{\phi_{0}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}),d}C_{0}^{d}(\lambda,\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})=0\big\}

\displaystyle\subset\begin{cases}\big[\frac{d}{2}\max\{m_{1}^{\prime},m_{1}^{\prime\prime}\}-(k_{1}+l_{1})+1,\,\frac{d}{2}(m_{2}^{\prime}+m_{2}^{\prime\prime}-1)-\max\{k_{m_{2}^{\prime}},l_{m_{2}^{\prime\prime}}\}\big],&k_{1}l_{1}\geq 1,\\
\varnothing,&k_{1}l_{1}=0.\end{cases}

Especially, for
m=1,2,\dots,r^{\prime}+r^{\prime\prime}-1
, if
\phi_{0}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})_{m+1}=0
and
\phi_{0}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})_{m}\neq 0
, then
C_{0}^{d}(\lambda,\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})
is non-zero holomorphic at
\lambda=\frac{d}{2}m
,
and has a pole at
\lambda=\frac{d}{2}(m-1)
.

Proof.

By direct computation, we have

\displaystyle\phi_{0}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})_{a}=\begin{cases}k_{1}+l_{a},&1\leq a\leq\min\{m_{1}^{\prime},m_{2}^{\prime\prime}\},\\
k_{1},&m_{2}^{\prime\prime}<a\leq m_{1}^{\prime}\end{cases}=\begin{cases}k_{a}+l_{1},&1\leq a\leq\min\{m_{1}^{\prime\prime},m_{2}^{\prime}\},\\
l_{1},&m_{2}^{\prime}<a\leq m_{1}^{\prime\prime},\end{cases}

\displaystyle\phi_{0}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})_{m_{2}^{\prime}+m_{2}^{\prime\prime}+1}=0,

and

\displaystyle C_{0}^{d}(\lambda,\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})

\displaystyle=C_{0}^{d}\bigl(\lambda,\bigl(k_{1},\dots,k_{m_{2}^{\prime}}\bigr),\bigl(l_{1},\dots,l_{m_{2}^{\prime\prime}}\bigr)\bigr)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\prod_{a=1}^{\max\{m_{1}^{\prime},m_{1}^{\prime\prime}\}}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{\phi_{0}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})_{a}}}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\prod_{a=\max\{m_{1}^{\prime},m_{1}^{\prime\prime}\}+1}^{m_{2}^{\prime}+m_{2}^{\prime\prime}}\frac{\prod_{i=\max\{1,a-m_{2}^{\prime\prime}\}}^{\min\{a-1,m_{2}^{\prime}\}}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{k_{i}+l_{a-i}}}{\prod_{i=\max\{1,a-m_{2}^{\prime\prime}\}}^{\min\{a,m_{2}^{\prime}+1\}}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{k_{i}+l_{a+1-i}}}.

For
2\leq a\leq m_{2}^{\prime}+m_{2}^{\prime\prime}
, let

\displaystyle\phi(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})_{a}

\displaystyle:={\min}_{2}\{k_{i}+l_{j}\mid 1\leq i\leq m_{2}^{\prime}+1,\ 1\leq j\leq m_{2}^{\prime\prime}+1,\ i+j=a+1\},

\displaystyle\psi(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})_{a}

\displaystyle:=\max\{k_{i}+l_{j}\mid 1\leq i\leq m_{2}^{\prime},\ 1\leq j\leq m_{2}^{\prime\prime},\ i+j=a\},

where
{\min}_{2}
denotes the second smallest element, so that

\max\big\{k_{m_{2}^{\prime}},l_{m_{2}^{\prime\prime}}\big\}\leq\phi(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})_{a}\leq\psi(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})_{a}\leq k_{1}+l_{1}

holds for every
a
. Then we have

\displaystyle\big\{\lambda\in\mathbb{C}\mid(\lambda)_{\phi_{0}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}),d}C_{0}^{d}(\lambda,\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})=0\big\}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\bigcup_{a=\max\{m_{1}^{\prime},m_{1}^{\prime\prime}\}+1}^{m_{2}^{\prime}+m_{2}^{\prime\prime}}\left\{\lambda\in\mathbb{C}\ \middle|\ \begin{array}[]{l}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{\phi_{0}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})_{a}}\\
\displaystyle{}\times\frac{\prod_{i=\max\{1,a-m_{2}^{\prime\prime}\}}^{\min\{a-1,m_{2}^{\prime}\}}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{k_{i}+l_{a-i}}}{\prod_{i=\max\{1,a-m_{2}^{\prime\prime}\}}^{\min\{a,m_{2}^{\prime}+1\}}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{k_{i}+l_{a+1-i}}}=0\end{array}\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad{}\subset\bigcup_{a=\max\{m_{1}^{\prime},m_{1}^{\prime\prime}\}+1}^{m_{2}^{\prime}+m_{2}^{\prime\prime}}\left\{\lambda\in\mathbb{C}\ \middle|\ \frac{\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{\psi(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})_{a}}}{\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{\phi(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})_{a}}}=0\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\bigcup_{a=\max\{m_{1}^{\prime},m_{1}^{\prime\prime}\}+1}^{m_{2}^{\prime}+m_{2}^{\prime\prime}}\left\{\frac{d}{2}(a-1)-j\ \middle|\ j\in\mathbb{Z},\,\phi(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})_{a}\leq j\leq\psi(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})_{a}-1\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad{}\subset\left[\frac{d}{2}\max\{m_{1}^{\prime},m_{1}^{\prime\prime}\}-(k_{1}+l_{1})+1,\,\frac{d}{2}(m_{2}^{\prime}+m_{2}^{\prime\prime}-1)-\max\{k_{m_{2}^{\prime}},l_{m_{2}^{\prime\prime}}\}\right].

When
k_{1}=0
, i.e.,
\mathbf{k}=(0,\dots,0)
, by direct computation, we have

(\lambda)_{\phi_{0}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}),d}C_{0}^{d}(\lambda,\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})=\frac{(\lambda)_{\mathbf{l},d}}{(\lambda)_{\mathbf{l},d}}=1,

and this is non-zero everywhere. The
l_{1}=0
, i.e.,
\mathbf{l}=(0,\dots,0)
case is similar.

If
\phi_{0}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})_{m+1}=0
and
\phi_{0}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})_{m}\neq 0
, then
k_{m_{2}^{\prime}+1}=l_{m_{2}^{\prime\prime}+1}=0
and
\max\big\{k_{m^{\prime}_{2}},l_{m^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\big\}\neq 0
hold for some
m_{2}^{\prime}
,
m_{2}^{\prime\prime}
with
m_{2}^{\prime}+m_{2}^{\prime\prime}=m
,
and by the above formula
(\lambda)_{\phi_{0}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}),d}C_{0}^{d}(\lambda,\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})
is non-zero at
\lambda=\frac{d}{2}m
,
\frac{d}{2}(m-1)
.
Hence, we get the last claim.
∎

Especially, if
\phi_{0}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})_{a+1}=0
and
\phi_{0}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})_{a}\neq 0
, then for non-zero polynomials
f(x_{11})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\bigr)
,
g(x_{22})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\bigr)
,

\bigl\langle f(x_{11})g(x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x}
has a pole at
\lambda=\frac{d}{2}(a-1)
,
and combining with Corollary
2.4
and (
5.4
) we have

\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\bigr)\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\bigr)\subset\bigoplus_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime}+r^{\prime\prime}}\\
m_{a+1}=0\end{subarray}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}),\qquad\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\bigr)\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\bigr)\not\subset\bigoplus_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime}+r^{\prime\prime}}\\
m_{a}=0\end{subarray}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}),

that is, for
a=0,1,\dots,r-1
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\bigr)\cap\bigoplus_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}\\
m_{a+1}=0\end{subarray}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})

\displaystyle=\bigoplus_{\begin{subarray}{c}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime}}\times\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}\\
\phi_{0}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})_{a+1}=0\end{subarray}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\bigr)\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\bigr)

(5.5)

\displaystyle=\bigoplus_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leq a^{\prime}\leq r^{\prime}\\
0\leq a^{\prime\prime}\leq r^{\prime\prime}\\
a^{\prime}+a^{\prime\prime}\leq a\end{subarray}}\bigoplus_{\begin{subarray}{c}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{a^{\prime}}\times\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{a^{\prime\prime}}\\
k_{a^{\prime}},l_{a^{\prime\prime}}\geq 1\end{subarray}}\mathcal{P}_{(\mathbf{k},\underline{0}_{r^{\prime}-a^{\prime}})}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\bigr)\mathcal{P}_{(\mathbf{l},\underline{0}_{r^{\prime\prime}-a^{\prime\prime}})}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\bigr),

where we set
\phi_{0}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})_{r^{\prime}+r^{\prime\prime}+1}=\cdots=\phi_{0}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})_{r}:=0
and
k_{0}=l_{0}:=+\infty
. This equality is earlier given in
[
14
, Section 6]
.

Next we consider general
(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime}}\times\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}
. Then again by the above proposition,
\frac{1}{C_{0}^{d}(\lambda,\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})}\bigl\langle f(x_{11})g(x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x}
is holomorphic
for
\operatorname{Re}\lambda>\frac{d}{2}(r^{\prime}+r^{\prime\prime}-1)-\max\{k_{r^{\prime}},l_{r^{\prime\prime}}\}
.
Then by Theorem
3.5
, comparing the top terms, we get the following.

Theorem 5.6
.

Suppose
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}
are of tube type, and consider the meromorphic continuation of
\langle\cdot,\cdot\rangle_{\lambda}
.
Then for
\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime}}
,
\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}
,
a=1,2,\dots,\min\{k_{r^{\prime}},l_{r^{\prime\prime}}\}
and for
f(x_{11})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\bigr)
,
g(x_{22})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\bigr)
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{C_{0}^{d}(\lambda,\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})}\bigl\langle f(x_{11})g(x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x}\big|_{\lambda=\frac{n}{r}-a}

\displaystyle=\frac{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{a}}{C_{0}^{d}\left(\frac{n}{r}+a,\mathbf{k}-\underline{a}_{r^{\prime}},\mathbf{l}-\underline{a}_{r^{\prime\prime}}\right)}\bigl\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(x_{11})^{-a}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(x_{22})^{-a}f(x_{11})g(x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\frac{n}{r}+a,x}.

5.2
Results on restriction of
\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D)}
to subgroups

Next we consider the decomposition of the holomorphic discrete series representation of scalar type
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D)
under the subgroup
\widetilde{G}_{1}\subset\widetilde{G}
.
By Theorem
2.5
, we have

\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D)|_{\widetilde{G}_{1}}\simeq\sideset{}{{}^{\oplus}}{\sum}_{\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime}}}\,\sideset{}{{}^{\oplus}}{\sum}_{\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}}\mathcal{H}_{\varepsilon_{1}\lambda}\bigl(D_{1},\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\bigr)\boxtimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\bigr)\bigr),

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{\varepsilon_{1}\lambda}\bigl(D_{1},\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\bigr)\boxtimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\bigr)\bigr)

\displaystyle\simeq\begin{cases}\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+k+l}\bigl(D_{{\rm SO}_{0}(2,d)}\bigr)\boxtimes\chi_{{\rm SO}(2)}^{-k+l}&(\text{Case }1),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+\lambda}\bigl(D_{{\rm U}(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime})},V_{2\mathbf{k}}^{(r^{\prime})\vee}\boxtimes V_{2\mathbf{l}}^{(r^{\prime\prime})}\bigr)&(\text{Case }2),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda_{1}+\lambda_{2}}\bigl(D_{{\rm U}(q^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime})},V_{\mathbf{k}}^{(q^{\prime})\vee}\boxtimes V_{\mathbf{l}}^{(s^{\prime\prime})}\bigr)\mathbin{\hat{\boxtimes}}\mathcal{H}_{\lambda_{1}+\lambda_{2}}\bigl(D_{{\rm U}(q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime})},V_{\mathbf{l}}^{(q^{\prime\prime})\vee}\boxtimes V_{\mathbf{k}}^{(s^{\prime})}\bigr)&(\text{Case }3),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\frac{\lambda}{2}+\frac{\lambda}{2}}\bigl(D_{{\rm U}(s^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime})},V_{\mathbf{k}^{2}}^{(s^{\prime})\vee}\boxtimes V_{\mathbf{l}^{2}}^{(s^{\prime\prime})}\bigr)&(\text{Case }4),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+k+\frac{|\mathbf{l}|}{2}}\bigl(D_{E_{6(-14)}},V_{(l_{1}-l_{2},0,0,0,0)}^{[10]\vee}\bigr)\boxtimes\chi_{{\rm U}(1)}^{-\lambda+2k-2|\mathbf{l}|}&(\text{Case }5),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+k}\bigl(D_{{\rm SO}^{*}(10)},V_{\left(\frac{l}{2},\frac{l}{2},\frac{l}{2},\frac{l}{2},-\frac{l}{2}\right)}^{(5)\vee}\bigr)\boxtimes\chi_{{\rm U}(1)}^{-\lambda+3k-3l}&(\text{Case }6),\end{cases}

where we set
\lambda=\lambda_{1}+\lambda_{2}
for Case 3. For each
\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime}}
,
\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}
,
let
V_{\mathbf{k}}\boxtimes W_{\mathbf{l}}
be an abstract
K_{1}
-module isomorphic to
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\bigr)\boxtimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\bigr)
,
let
\|\cdot\|_{\varepsilon_{1}\lambda,\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}
be the
\widetilde{G}_{1}
-invariant norm on
{\mathcal{H}_{\varepsilon_{1}\lambda}(D_{1},\allowbreak V_{\mathbf{k}}\boxtimes W_{\mathbf{l}})}

normalized such that
\|v\|_{\varepsilon_{1}\lambda,\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}=|v|_{V_{\mathbf{k}}\boxtimes W_{\mathbf{l}}}
holds for all constant functions
v\in V_{\mathbf{k}}\boxtimes W_{\mathbf{l}}
,
and for
\lambda>p-1
let

\mathcal{F}_{\lambda,\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}^{\downarrow}\colon\ \mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D)|_{\widetilde{G}_{1}}\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\varepsilon_{1}\lambda}(D_{1},V_{\mathbf{k}}\boxtimes W_{\mathbf{l}})

be the symmetry breaking operator given in (
2.18
) and (
2.19
), using a vector-valued polynomial

\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}(x_{11},x_{22})\in\bigl(\bigl(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\bigr)\boxtimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\bigr)\bigr)\otimes\bigl(\overline{V_{\mathbf{k}}}\boxtimes\overline{W_{\mathbf{l}}}\bigr)\bigr)^{K_{1}}
satisfying

\displaystyle\big|\langle f(x_{11})g(x_{22}),\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}(x_{11},x_{22})\rangle_{F,x}\big|_{V_{\mathbf{k}}\boxtimes W_{\mathbf{l}}}=\|f(z_{11})g(z_{22})\|_{F,z}

for
f(x_{11})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\bigr)
,
g(x_{22})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\bigr)
,
so that

\big\|\mathcal{F}_{\lambda,\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}^{\downarrow}(f(x_{11})g(x_{22}))\big\|_{\varepsilon_{1}\lambda,\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}=\|f(z_{11})g(z_{22})\|_{F,z},\qquad f(x_{11})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\bigr),\ g(x_{22})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\bigr)

holds. Also, when
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}
are of tube type, we fix
[K_{1},K_{1}]
-isomorphisms

V_{\mathbf{k}+\underline{a}_{r^{\prime}}}\boxtimes W_{\mathbf{l}+\underline{a}_{r^{\prime\prime}}}\simeq V_{\mathbf{k}}\boxtimes W_{\mathbf{l}}
for each
a\in\mathbb{Z}_{>0}
.
Then by Proposition
2.6
(3) and Theorem
5.1
, the following Parseval–Plancherel-type formula holds.

Corollary 5.7
.

For
\lambda>p-1
and for
f\in\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D)
we have

\|f\|_{\lambda}^{2}=\sum_{\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime}}}\sum_{\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}}C_{0}^{d}(\lambda,\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})\big\|\mathcal{F}_{\lambda,\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}^{\downarrow}f\big\|_{\varepsilon_{1}\lambda,\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}^{2},

where
C_{0}^{d}(\lambda,\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})
is as in (
5.1
).

Next we consider the meromorphic continuation for smaller
\lambda
.
Then by Propositions
2.7
,
5.5
, Theorems
5.2
,
5.6
and the formula (
5.5
),
we have the following.

Corollary 5.8
.

For
\mathbf{k}\hskip-0.5pt\in\hskip-0.5pt\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime}}
,
\mathbf{l}\hskip-0.5pt\in\hskip-0.5pt\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}
, let
\phi_{0}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})\hskip-0.5pt\in\hskip-0.5pt\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime}+r^{\prime\prime}}
be as in (
5.2
),
and set
\phi_{0}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})_{r^{\prime}+r^{\prime\prime}+1}\allowbreak=\cdots=\phi_{0}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})_{r}:=0
.

(1)

For
a=1,2,\dots,r
,

{\rm d}\tau_{\lambda}(\mathcal{U}(\mathfrak{g}_{1}))\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\bigr)\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\bigr)\subset M_{a}^{\mathfrak{g}}(\lambda)

holds if

\lambda\in\frac{d}{2}(a-1)-\phi_{0}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})_{a}-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},

where
M_{a}^{\mathfrak{g}}(\lambda)\subset\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)_{\widetilde{K}}
is the
\bigl(\mathfrak{g},\widetilde{K}\bigr)
-submodule given in (
2.12
).

(2)

For
a=0,1,\dots,r-1
we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{\frac{d}{2}a}(D)|_{\widetilde{G}_{1}}

\displaystyle\simeq\sideset{}{{}^{\oplus}}{\sum}_{\begin{subarray}{c}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime}}\times\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}\\
\phi_{0}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})_{a+1}=0\end{subarray}}\mathcal{H}_{\varepsilon_{1}\frac{d}{2}a}\bigl(D_{1},\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\bigr)\boxtimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\bigr)\bigr)

\displaystyle=\sideset{}{{}^{\oplus}}{\sum}_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leq a^{\prime}\leq r^{\prime}\\
0\leq a^{\prime\prime}\leq r^{\prime\prime}\\
a^{\prime}+a^{\prime\prime}\leq a\end{subarray}}\hskip 6.0pt\sideset{}{{}^{\oplus}}{\sum}_{\begin{subarray}{c}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{a^{\prime}}\times\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{a^{\prime\prime}}\\
k_{a^{\prime}},l_{a^{\prime\prime}}\geq 1\end{subarray}}\mathcal{H}_{\varepsilon_{1}\frac{d}{2}a}\bigl(D_{1},\mathcal{P}_{(\mathbf{k},\underline{0}_{r^{\prime}-a^{\prime}})}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\bigr)\boxtimes\mathcal{P}_{(\mathbf{l},\underline{0}_{r^{\prime\prime}-a^{\prime\prime}})}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\bigr)\bigr),

where we set
k_{0}=l_{0}:=+\infty
.

(3)

For
a=0,1,\dots,r-1
, if
\phi_{0}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l})_{a+1}=0
, then
\mathcal{F}_{\lambda,\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}^{\downarrow}
is holomorphic at
\lambda=\frac{d}{2}a
,
and its restriction gives the symmetry breaking operator

\mathcal{F}_{\frac{d}{2}a,\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}^{\downarrow}\colon\ \mathcal{H}_{\frac{d}{2}a}(D)|_{\widetilde{G}_{1}}\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\varepsilon_{1}\frac{d}{2}a}(D_{1},V_{\mathbf{k}}\boxtimes W_{\mathbf{l}}).

(4)

Suppose
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}
are of tube type. For
a=1,2,\dots,\min\{k_{r^{\prime}},l_{r^{\prime\prime}}\}
, if

\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}(x_{11},x_{22})=c\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(x_{11})^{a}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(x_{22})^{a}\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{k}-\underline{a}_{r^{\prime}},\mathbf{l}-\underline{a}_{r^{\prime\prime}}}(x_{11},x_{22}),

then we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{\frac{n}{r}-a,\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}^{\downarrow}=c\mathcal{F}_{\frac{n}{r}+a,\mathbf{k}-\underline{a}_{r^{\prime}},\mathbf{l}-\underline{a}_{r^{\prime\prime}}}^{\downarrow}\circ\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\right)^{a}\colon

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\frac{n}{r}-a}(D)|_{\widetilde{G}_{1}}\longrightarrow\mathcal{O}_{\varepsilon_{1}\left(\frac{n}{r}-a\right)}(D_{1},V_{\mathbf{k}}\boxtimes W_{\mathbf{l}})\simeq\mathcal{O}_{\varepsilon_{1}\left(\frac{n}{r}+a\right)}(D_{1},V_{\mathbf{k}-\underline{a}_{r^{\prime}}}\boxtimes W_{\mathbf{l}-\underline{a}_{r^{\prime\prime}}}).

If
\mathbf{k}
,
\mathbf{l}
satisfy the condition in Remark
5.3
(1), then “only if” in Corollary
5.8
(1) also holds.
Also, Corollary
5.8
(2) for
\lambda=\frac{d}{2}
(
a=1
case) is earlier given in
[
41
]
.
The parameter set in Corollary
5.8
(2) also appears in Howe’s correspondence for the dual pair
({\rm U}(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime}),{\rm U}(a))
(see, e.g.,
[
1
,
24
,
52
]
),
and especially, we can prove (2) for Cases 2–4 by using the seesaw dual pair theory (see, e.g.,
[
18
, Section 3]
,
[
37
]
) as in
[
41
]
.

6
Case
\boldsymbol{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}}
is simple

In this section we treat
\mathfrak{p}^{+}=(\mathfrak{p}^{+})^{\sigma}\oplus(\mathfrak{p}^{+})^{-\sigma}=\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
such that
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
is simple,
and
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}(e)_{2}\subsetneq\mathfrak{p}^{+}(e)_{2}
holds for some (or equivalently any) maximal tripotent
e\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
, so that

(\mathfrak{p}^{+},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2})=\begin{cases}(\mathbb{C}^{n},\mathbb{C}^{n^{\prime}},\mathbb{C}^{n^{\prime\prime}})&(\text{Case }1),\\
(\operatorname{Sym}(r,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Sym}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{C})\oplus\operatorname{Sym}(r^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{C}),{\rm M}(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C}))&(\text{Case }2),\\
(\operatorname{Alt}(s,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Alt}(s^{\prime},\mathbb{C})\oplus\operatorname{Alt}(s^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{C}),{\rm M}(s^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C}))&(\text{Case }3),\\
({\rm M}(r,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Alt}(r,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Sym}(r,\mathbb{C}))&(\text{Case }4),\\
({\rm M}(r,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Sym}(r,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Alt}(r,\mathbb{C}))&(\text{Case }5),\\
\bigl(\operatorname{Herm}(3,\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}},{\rm M}(2,6;\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Alt}(6,\mathbb{C})\bigr)&(\text{Case }6),\\
\bigl(\operatorname{Herm}(3,\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}},\operatorname{Alt}(6,\mathbb{C}),{\rm M}(2,6;\mathbb{C})\bigr)&(\text{Case }7),\\
\bigl(\operatorname{Herm}(3,\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}},\mathbb{C}\oplus\operatorname{Herm}(2,\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}},{\rm M}(1,2;\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}}\bigr)&(\text{Case }8),\\
\bigl({\rm M}(1,2;\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}},{\rm M}(2,4;\mathbb{C}),{\rm M}(4,2;\mathbb{C})\bigr)&(\text{Case }9),\\
\bigl({\rm M}(1,2;\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}},\mathbb{C}\oplus{\rm M}(1,5;\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Alt}(5,\mathbb{C})\bigr)&(\text{Case }10)\end{cases}

(
n=n^{\prime}+n^{\prime\prime}
,
n\geq 3
,
n^{\prime\prime}\neq 2
for Case 1,
r=r^{\prime}+r^{\prime\prime}
for Case 2, and
s=s^{\prime}+s^{\prime\prime}
,
s^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime}\geq 2
for Case 3).
Then the corresponding symmetric pairs are

(G,G_{1})=\begin{cases}({\rm SO}_{0}(2,n),{\rm SO}_{0}(2,n^{\prime})\times{\rm SO}(n^{\prime\prime}))&(\text{Case }1),\\
({\rm Sp}(r,\mathbb{R}),{\rm Sp}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{R})\times{\rm Sp}(r^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{R}))&(\text{Case }2),\\
({\rm SO}^{*}(2s),{\rm SO}^{*}(2s^{\prime})\times{\rm SO}^{*}(2s^{\prime\prime}))&(\text{Case }3),\\
({\rm SU}(r,r),{\rm SO}^{*}(2r))&(\text{Case }4),\\
({\rm SU}(r,r),{\rm Sp}(r,\mathbb{R}))&(\text{Case }5),\\
(E_{7(-25)},{\rm SU}(2,6))&(\text{Case }6),\\
(E_{7(-25)},{\rm SU}(2)\times{\rm SO}^{*}(12))&(\text{Case }7),\\
(E_{7(-25)},{\rm SL}(2,\mathbb{R})\times{\rm Spin}_{0}(2,10))&(\text{Case }8),\\
(E_{6(-14)},{\rm SU}(2,4)\times{\rm SU}(2))&(\text{Case }9),\\
(E_{6(-14)},{\rm SL}(2,\mathbb{R})\times{\rm SU}(1,5))&(\text{Case }10)\end{cases}

(up to covering). Let
\dim\mathfrak{p}^{+}=:n
,
\dim\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}=:n_{2}
,
\operatorname{rank}\mathfrak{p}^{+}=:r
,
\operatorname{rank}\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}=:r_{2}
,
let
d
,
d_{2}
be the numbers defined in (
2.4
) for
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
respectively, and let
\varepsilon_{2}\in\{1,2\}
be as in (
2.15
).
Then the numbers
(r,r_{2},d,d_{2},\varepsilon_{2})
are given by

(r,r_{2},d,d_{2},\varepsilon_{2})=\begin{cases}(2,2,n-2,n^{\prime\prime}-2,1)&(\text{Case }1,\,n^{\prime\prime}\geq 3),\\
(2,1,n-2,-,2)&(\text{Case }1,\,n^{\prime\prime}=1),\\
(r,\min\{r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime}\},1,2,2)&(\text{Case }2),\\
(\lfloor s/2\rfloor,\min\{s^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime}\},4,2,1)&(\text{Case }3),\\
(r,r,2,1,1)&(\text{Case }4),\\
(r,\lfloor r/2\rfloor,2,4,2)&(\text{Case }5),\\
(3,3,8,4,1)&(\text{Case }6),\\
(3,2,8,2,1)&(\text{Case }7),\\
(3,2,8,6,1)&(\text{Case }8),\\
(2,2,6,2,1)&(\text{Case }9),\\
(2,2,6,4,1)&(\text{Case }10).\end{cases}

When
\varepsilon_{2}=1
, we have
d_{2}=d/2
or
r_{2}=2
. Similarly, when
\varepsilon_{2}=2
, we have
d_{2}=2d
or
r_{2}=1
.
For
r_{2}=1
case, since
d_{2}
is not determined uniquely and any number is allowed, we may assume
d_{2}=2d
.

6.1
Results on weighted Bergman inner products

First, for
f(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)
, we want to compute the top terms and the poles of
\bigl\langle f(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x}
,
where
\langle\cdot,\cdot\rangle_{\lambda}=\langle\cdot,\cdot\rangle_{\lambda,x}
is the weighted Bergman inner product given in (
2.9
),
with the variable of integration
x=x_{1}+x_{2}
.

Theorem 6.1
.

Let
\operatorname{Re}\lambda>p-1
,
\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r_{2}}
, and let
f(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)
. Then we have

\bigl\langle f(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x}\big|_{z_{1}=0}=C_{\varepsilon_{2}}^{d,d_{2}}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})f(z_{2}),

where, for
\varepsilon_{2}=1
,

\displaystyle C_{1}^{d,d_{2}}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})

\displaystyle=\frac{\bigl(\lambda+k_{1}-\frac{d-d_{2}}{2}\bigr)_{k_{2}}}{(\lambda)_{k_{1}+k_{2}}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}\right)_{k_{2}}},

\displaystyle r_{2}=2,

\displaystyle C_{1}^{d,d_{2}}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})

\displaystyle=\frac{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(i+j-2)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{j}}}{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}+1}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(i+j-3)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{j}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\prod_{a=2}^{2r_{2}-2}\prod_{i=\max\{1,a+1-r_{2}\}}^{\lfloor a/2\rfloor}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(a-1)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{a+1-i}}}{\prod_{a=1}^{2r_{2}-1}\prod_{i=\max\{1,a+1-r_{2}\}}^{\lceil a/2\rceil}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(a-1)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{a+2-i}}},

\displaystyle d_{2}=d/2,

(6.1)

and for
\varepsilon_{2}=2
,

\displaystyle C_{2}^{d,d_{2}}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})=\frac{2^{|\mathbf{k}|}\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}}(2\lambda-1-d(i+j-1))_{k_{i}+k_{j}}}{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}+1}(2\lambda-1-d(i+j-2))_{k_{i}+k_{j}}}\frac{\prod_{i=1}^{r_{2}}\left(\lambda-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{d}{2}(2i-1)\right)_{k_{i}}}{\prod_{i=1}^{r_{2}}(\lambda-d(i-1))_{k_{i}}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(i+j-1)\right)_{\left\lfloor\frac{k_{i}+k_{j}}{2}\right\rfloor}\prod_{1\leq i\leq j\leq r_{2}}\left(\lambda-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{d}{2}(i+j-1)\right)_{\left\lceil\frac{k_{i}+k_{j}}{2}\right\rceil}}{\prod_{1\leq i\leq j\leq r_{2}+1}\!\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(i+j-2)\right)_{\left\lfloor\frac{k_{i}+k_{j}}{2}\right\rfloor}\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}+1}\!\left(\lambda-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{d}{2}(i+j-2)\right)_{\left\lceil\frac{k_{i}+k_{j}}{2}\right\rceil}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\prod_{a=3}^{2r_{2}-1}\prod_{i=\max\{1,a-r_{2}\}}^{\lceil a/2\rceil-1}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{\left\lfloor\frac{k_{i}+k_{a-i}}{2}\right\rfloor}}{\prod_{a=1}^{2r_{2}}\prod_{i=\max\{1,a-r_{2}\}}^{\lceil a/2\rceil}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{\left\lfloor\frac{k_{i}+k_{a+1-i}}{2}\right\rfloor}}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\ {}\times\frac{\prod_{a=2}^{2r_{2}}\prod_{i=\max\{1,a-r_{2}\}}^{\lfloor a/2\rfloor}\left(\lambda-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{\left\lceil\frac{k_{i}+k_{a-i}}{2}\right\rceil}}{\prod_{a=2}^{2r_{2}}\prod_{i=\max\{1,a-r_{2}\}}^{\lfloor a/2\rfloor}\left(\lambda-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{\left\lceil\frac{k_{i}+k_{a+1-i}}{2}\right\rceil}}.

(6.2)

Here we put
k_{r_{2}+1}:=0
.

Theorem 6.2
.

For
\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r_{2}}
,
\varepsilon_{2}=1,2
, we define
\phi_{\varepsilon_{2}}(\mathbf{k})\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{\varepsilon_{2}r_{2}}
by

\displaystyle\phi_{1}(\mathbf{k})_{a}:=\min\{k_{i}+k_{j}\mid 1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}+1,\,i+j=2a+1\},

\displaystyle 1\leq a\leq r_{2},

(6.3)

\displaystyle\phi_{2}(\mathbf{k})_{a}:=\min\left\{\left\lfloor\frac{k_{i}+k_{j}}{2}\right\rfloor\ \middle|\ 1\leq i\leq j\leq r_{2}+1,\ i+j=a+1\right\},

\displaystyle 1\leq a\leq 2r_{2},

(6.4)

where
k_{r_{2}+1}:=0
. Then for
f(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)
, as a function of
\lambda
,

(\lambda)_{\phi_{\varepsilon_{2}}(\mathbf{k}),d}\bigl\langle f(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x}

(6.5)

is holomorphically continued for all
\mathbb{C}
.

Remark 6.3
.

(1)

By
[
44
, Corollary 6.6]
, if
f(x_{2})\neq 0
and if
\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r_{2}}
satisfies “
\varepsilon_{2}=1
,
\mathbf{k}=(\underline{k}_{a},\underline{0}_{r_{2}-a})
,
1\leq a\leq r_{2}
”
or “
\varepsilon_{2}=1
,
\mathbf{k}=(k_{1},\underline{k_{2}}_{a-1},\underline{0}_{r_{2}-a})
,
2\leq a\leq r_{2},\ a\colon\text{even}
”
or “
\varepsilon_{2}=2
,
\mathbf{k}=(\underline{k+1}_{l},\underline{k}_{a-l},\underline{0}_{r_{2}-a})
,
0\leq l<a\leq r_{2}
”,
then (
6.5
) gives a non-zero polynomial in
z\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}
for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
.

(2)

The restriction of Theorem
6.2
to
z_{1}=0
, the holomorphy of

(\lambda)_{\phi_{\varepsilon_{2}}(\mathbf{k}),d}\bigl\langle f(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x}\big|_{z_{1}=0}

follows from Theorem
6.1
, since it is easily proved that
(\lambda)_{\phi_{\varepsilon_{2}}(\mathbf{k}),d}C_{\varepsilon_{2}}^{d,d_{2}}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})
is holomorphic for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
.

(3)

Under Corollary
2.4
, Theorem
6.2
is equivalent to the inclusion

\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)\subset\bigoplus_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{\varepsilon_{2}r_{2}}\\
m_{a}\leq\phi_{\varepsilon_{2}}(\mathbf{k})_{a}\end{subarray}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}).

(6.6)

Here
(\lambda)_{\mathbf{m},d}
is as in (
2.11
), and
\underline{k}_{a}:=(\underbrace{k,\dots,k}_{a})
.

Proof of Theorem
6.1
.

By the last paragraph of Section
3.1
, we may assume
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
are of tube type, so that
r=\varepsilon_{2}r_{2}
holds,
and by the
K_{1}
-equivariance, may assume
f(x_{2})=\Delta_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})
.

First suppose
\varepsilon_{2}=1
,
r_{2}=2
. Then the theorem follows from
[
44
, Theorems 6.3 (1), Corollary 6.5 (2)]
,

\displaystyle\bigl\langle\Delta_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x}=\frac{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{d}{2}+1}}{(\lambda)_{(k_{1}+k_{2},2k_{2}),d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{2}}\right)^{k_{2}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{\lambda+2k_{2}-\frac{d}{2}-1}\Delta_{(k_{1}-k_{2},0)}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{(k_{1}+k_{2},2k_{2}),d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{d}{2}+1}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{2}}\right)^{k_{2}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{\lambda+k_{2}-\frac{d}{2}-1}

\displaystyle\qquad{}\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times{}_{1}F_{0}\,\biggl(-\lambda-2k_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+1;-\frac{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z_{1})}{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})}\biggr)\,\Delta_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\bigl(\lambda+k_{1}-\frac{d-d_{2}}{2}\bigr)_{k_{2}}}{(\lambda)_{(k_{1}+k_{2},k_{2}),d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{d}{2}+1}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{\lambda-\frac{d}{2}-1}

\displaystyle\qquad{}\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times{}_{2}F_{1}\,\biggl(\begin{matrix}-\lambda-k_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+1,\,-\lambda-k_{1}+\frac{d-d_{2}}{2}+1\\
-\lambda-k_{1}-k_{2}+\frac{d-d_{2}}{2}+1\end{matrix};-\frac{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z_{1})}{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})}\biggr)\,\Delta_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\bigl(\lambda+k_{1}-\frac{d-d_{2}}{2}\bigr)_{k_{2}}}{(\lambda)_{(k_{1}+k_{2},k_{2}),d}}{}_{2}F_{1}\,\biggl(\begin{matrix}-k_{2},\,-k_{1}-\frac{d_{2}}{2}\\
-\lambda-k_{1}-k_{2}+\frac{d-d_{2}}{2}+1\end{matrix};-\frac{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z_{1})}{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})}\biggr)\,\Delta_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2}),

(6.7)

where
{}_{1}F_{0}(\alpha;t):=\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\frac{(\alpha)_{m}}{m!}t^{m}
,

{}_{2}F_{1}\left(\begin{smallmatrix}\alpha,\beta\\
\gamma\end{smallmatrix};t\right):=\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\frac{(\alpha)_{m}(\beta)_{m}}{(\gamma)_{m}m!}t^{m}
.

Next suppose
\varepsilon_{2}=1
,
d_{2}=d/2
. The 2nd equality of (
6.1
) is easy. We prove the 1st equality by induction on
r_{2}
.
If
r_{2}=1
, then we have
\mathfrak{p}^{+}=\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
under the tube type assumption, and this follows from Corollary
2.4
.
Next we assume the theorem for
r_{2}-1
, and prove it for
r_{2}
. Then since
r=r_{2}
holds, by (
3.3
) we have

\displaystyle\bigl\langle\Delta_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x}\big|_{z_{1}=0}

\displaystyle=\frac{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z_{2})^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{2k_{r_{2}}}_{r_{2}},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\!\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{2}}\right)^{k_{r_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z_{2})^{\lambda+2k_{r_{2}}-\frac{n}{r}}\Bigl\langle\Delta_{\mathbf{k}-\underline{k_{r_{2}}}_{r_{2}}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\Bigr\rangle_{\lambda+2k_{r_{2}},x}\Bigr|_{z_{1}=0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{\underline{2k_{r_{2}}}_{r_{2}},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{-\lambda+\frac{d}{2}(r_{2}-1)+1}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{2}}\right)^{k_{r_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{\lambda+2k_{r_{2}}-\frac{d}{2}(r_{2}-1)-1}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\frac{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}-1}\left(\lambda+2k_{r_{2}}-\frac{d}{4}(i+j-2)\right)_{(k_{i}-k_{r_{2}})+(k_{j}-k_{r_{2}})}}{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}}\left(\lambda+2k_{r_{2}}-\frac{d}{4}(i+j-3)\right)_{(k_{i}-k_{r_{2}})+(k_{j}-k_{r_{2}})}}\Delta_{\mathbf{k}-\underline{k_{r_{2}}}_{r_{2}}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{\underline{2k_{r_{2}}}_{r_{2}},d}}\prod_{i=1}^{r_{2}}\left(\lambda+k_{i}-\frac{d}{2}(r_{2}-1)+\frac{d}{4}(r_{2}-i)\right)_{k_{r_{2}}}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\frac{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}-1}\left(\lambda+2k_{r_{2}}-\frac{d}{4}(i+j-2)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{j}-2k_{r_{2}}}}{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}}\left(\lambda+2k_{r_{2}}-\frac{d}{4}(i+j-3)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{j}-2k_{r_{2}}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{k_{r_{2}}}\Delta_{\mathbf{k}-\underline{k_{r_{2}}}_{r_{2}}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{\underline{2k_{r_{2}}}_{r_{2}},d}}\frac{\prod_{i=1}^{r_{2}}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(i+r_{2}-2)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{r_{2}}}}{\prod_{i=1}^{r_{2}}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(i+(r_{2}+1)-3)\right)_{k_{i}}}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\frac{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}-1}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(i+j-2)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{j}}}{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(i+j-3)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{j}}}\frac{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(i+j-3)\right)_{2k_{r_{2}}}}{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}-1}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(i+j-2)\right)_{2k_{r_{2}}}}\Delta_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(2r_{2}-2)\right)_{2k_{r_{2}}}}{\prod_{i=1}^{r_{2}}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(i-1)\right)_{2k_{r_{2}}}}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\frac{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(i+j-2)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{j}}}{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}+1}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(i+j-3)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{j}}}\frac{\prod_{1\leq i\leq j\leq r_{2}-1}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(i+j-2)\right)_{2k_{r_{2}}}}{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}-1}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(i+j-2)\right)_{2k_{r_{2}}}}\Delta_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(i+j-2)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{j}}}{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}+1}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(i+j-3)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{j}}}\Delta_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2}),

where we have used the induction hypothesis and Proposition
3.1
at the 2rd equality, and (
2.6
) at the 3rd equality. Hence the theorem follows.

Next suppose
\varepsilon_{2}=2
. The 2nd equality of (
6.2
) follows from the formula
(2\mu-1)_{k}=2^{k}(\mu)_{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}\left(\mu-\frac{1}{2}\right)_{\lceil k/2\rceil}
,
and the 3rd equality is easy.
Next we prove the 1st equality by induction on
r_{2}
. When
r_{2}=1
, the theorem follows from
[
44
, Theorem 6.3 (2), Corollary 6.5 (3)]
,

\displaystyle\bigl\langle\Delta_{k_{1}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x}=\frac{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{d}{2}+1}}{(\lambda)_{(k_{1},k_{1}),d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\left(\frac{1}{2}\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{2}}\right)^{k_{1}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{\lambda+k_{1}-\frac{d}{2}-1}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{(k_{1},k_{1}),d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{d}{2}+1}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\left(\frac{1}{2}\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{2}}\right)^{k_{1}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{2\left(\lambda+k_{1}-\frac{d}{2}-1\right)}

\displaystyle\qquad{}\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times{}_{1}F_{0}\,\biggl(-\lambda-k_{1}+\frac{d}{2}+1;-\frac{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z_{1})}{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{2}}\biggr)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\left(\lambda+\left\lceil\frac{k_{1}}{2}\right\rceil-\frac{d+1}{2}\right)_{\lfloor k_{1}/2\rfloor}}{(\lambda)_{(k_{1},\lfloor k_{1}/2\rfloor),d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{d}{2}+1}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{2\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}-1\right)+k_{1}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times{}_{2}F_{1}\,\biggl(\begin{matrix}-\lambda-\frac{k_{1}}{2}+\frac{d}{2}+1,\,-\lambda-\frac{k_{1}}{2}+\frac{d}{2}+\frac{3}{2}\\[1.0pt]
-\lambda-k_{1}+\frac{d}{2}+\frac{3}{2}\end{matrix};-\frac{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z_{1})}{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{2}}\biggr)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\left(\lambda+\left\lceil\frac{k_{1}}{2}\right\rceil-\frac{d+1}{2}\right)_{\lfloor k_{1}/2\rfloor}}{(\lambda)_{(k_{1},\lfloor k_{1}/2\rfloor),d}}{}_{2}F_{1}\,\biggl(\begin{matrix}-\frac{k_{1}}{2},\,-\frac{k_{1}}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\\
-\lambda-k_{1}+\frac{d}{2}+\frac{3}{2}\end{matrix};-\frac{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z_{1})}{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{2}}\biggr){}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{k_{1}}.

(6.8)

Next we assume the theorem for
r_{2}-1
, and prove it for
r_{2}
. Then since
r=2r_{2}
,
d_{2}=2d
hold, by (
3.3
) we have

\displaystyle\bigl\langle\Delta_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x}\big|_{z_{1}=0}

\displaystyle=\frac{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z_{2})^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k_{r_{2}}}_{2r_{2}},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\!\left(\frac{1}{2}\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{2}}\right)^{k_{r_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z_{2})^{\lambda+k_{r_{2}}-\frac{n}{r}}\Bigl\langle\Delta_{\mathbf{k}-\underline{k_{r_{2}}}_{r_{2}}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\Bigr\rangle_{\lambda+k_{r_{2}},x}\Bigr|_{z_{1}=0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k_{r_{2}}}_{2r_{2}},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{2\left(-\lambda+\frac{d}{2}(2r_{2}-1)+1\right)}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\left(\frac{1}{2}\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{2}}\right)^{k_{r_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{2\left(\lambda+k_{r_{2}}-\frac{d}{2}(2r_{2}-1)-1\right)}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\frac{2^{|\mathbf{k}|-k_{r_{2}}r_{2}}\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}-1}(2(\lambda+k_{r_{2}})-1-d(i+j-1))_{(k_{i}-k_{r_{2}})+(k_{j}-k_{r_{2}})}}{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}}(2(\lambda+k_{r_{2}})-1-d(i+j-2))_{(k_{i}-k_{r_{2}})+(k_{j}-k_{r_{2}})}}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\frac{\prod_{i=1}^{r_{2}-1}\left(\lambda+k_{r_{2}}-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{d}{2}(2i-1)\right)_{k_{i}-k_{r_{2}}}}{\prod_{i=1}^{r_{2}-1}\left(\lambda+k_{r_{2}}-d(i-1)\right)_{k_{i}-k_{r_{2}}}}\Delta_{\mathbf{k}-\underline{k_{r_{2}}}_{r_{2}}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{2^{|\mathbf{k}|-2k_{r_{2}}r_{2}}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k_{r_{2}}}_{2r_{2}},d}}\prod_{i=1}^{r_{2}}(2\lambda+k_{i}-d(2r_{2}-1)-2+d(r_{2}-i)+1)_{k_{r_{2}}}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\frac{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}-1}(2\lambda+2k_{r_{2}}-1-d(i+j-1))_{k_{i}+k_{j}-2k_{r_{2}}}}{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}}(2\lambda+2k_{r_{2}}-1-d(i+j-2))_{k_{i}+k_{j}-2k_{r_{2}}}}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\frac{\prod_{i=1}^{r_{2}-1}\left(\lambda+k_{r_{2}}-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{d}{2}(2i-1)\right)_{k_{i}-k_{r_{2}}}}{\prod_{i=1}^{r_{2}-1}(\lambda+k_{r_{2}}-d(i-1))_{k_{i}-k_{r_{2}}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{k_{r_{2}}}\Delta_{\mathbf{k}-\underline{k_{r_{2}}}_{r_{2}}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{2^{|\mathbf{k}|-2k_{r_{2}}r_{2}}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k_{r_{2}}}_{2r_{2}},d}}\frac{\prod_{i=1}^{r_{2}}(2\lambda-1-d(i+r_{2}-1))_{k_{i}+k_{r_{2}}}}{\prod_{i=1}^{r_{2}}(2\lambda-1-d(i+(r_{2}+1)-2))_{k_{i}}}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\frac{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}-1}(2\lambda-1-d(i+j-1))_{k_{i}+k_{j}}}{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}}(2\lambda-1-d(i+j-2))_{k_{i}+k_{j}}}\frac{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}}(2\lambda-1-d(i+j-2))_{2k_{r_{2}}}}{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}-1}(2\lambda-1-d(i+j-1))_{2k_{r_{2}}}}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\frac{\prod_{i=1}^{r_{2}}\left(\lambda-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{d}{2}(2i-1)\right)_{k_{i}}}{\prod_{i=1}^{r_{2}}(\lambda-d(i-1))_{k_{i}}}\frac{\prod_{i=1}^{r_{2}}(\lambda-d(i-1))_{k_{r_{2}}}}{\prod_{i=1}^{r_{2}}\left(\lambda-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{d}{2}(2i-1)\right)_{k_{r_{2}}}}\Delta_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{2^{|\mathbf{k}|-2k_{r_{2}}r_{2}}(2\lambda-1-d(2r_{2}-1))_{2k_{r_{2}}}}{\prod_{i=1}^{r_{2}}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(2i-2)\right)_{k_{r_{2}}}\prod_{i=1}^{r_{2}}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(2i-1)\right)_{k_{r_{2}}}}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\frac{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}}(2\lambda-1-d(i+j-1))_{k_{i}+k_{j}}}{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}+1}(2\lambda-1-d(i+j-2))_{k_{i}+k_{j}}}\frac{\prod_{1\leq i\leq j\leq r_{2}-1}(2\lambda-1-d(i+j-1))_{2k_{r_{2}}}}{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}-1}(2\lambda-1-d(i+j-1))_{2k_{r_{2}}}}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\frac{\prod_{i=1}^{r_{2}}\left(\lambda-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{d}{2}(2i-1)\right)_{k_{i}}}{\prod_{i=1}^{r_{2}}(\lambda-d(i-1))_{k_{i}}}\frac{\prod_{i=1}^{r_{2}}(\lambda-d(i-1))_{k_{r_{2}}}}{\prod_{i=1}^{r_{2}}\left(\lambda-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{d}{2}(2i-1)\right)_{k_{r_{2}}}}\Delta_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{2^{|\mathbf{k}|-2k_{r_{2}}r_{2}}\prod_{i=1}^{r_{2}}(2\lambda-1-d(2i-1))_{2k_{r_{2}}}}{\prod_{i=1}^{r_{2}}\left(\lambda-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{d}{2}(2i-1)\right)_{k_{r_{2}}}\prod_{i=1}^{r_{2}}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(2i-1)\right)_{k_{r_{2}}}}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\frac{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}}(2\lambda-1-d(i+j-1))_{k_{i}+k_{j}}}{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}+1}(2\lambda-1-d(i+j-2))_{k_{i}+k_{j}}}\frac{\prod_{i=1}^{r_{2}}\left(\lambda-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{d}{2}(2i-1)\right)_{k_{i}}}{\prod_{i=1}^{r_{2}}(\lambda-d(i-1))_{k_{i}}}\Delta_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{2^{|\mathbf{k}|}\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}}(2\lambda-1-d(i+j-1))_{k_{i}+k_{j}}}{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}+1}(2\lambda-1-d(i+j-2))_{k_{i}+k_{j}}}\frac{\prod_{i=1}^{r_{2}}\left(\lambda-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{d}{2}(2i-1)\right)_{k_{i}}}{\prod_{i=1}^{r_{2}}(\lambda-d(i-1))_{k_{i}}}\Delta_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2}),

where we have used the induction hypothesis and Proposition
3.1
at the 2nd equality, and (
2.6
) at the 3rd equality. Hence the theorem follows.
∎

Proof of Theorem
6.2
.

Again it is enough to prove when both
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
and
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
are of tube type
(i.e.,
\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)=\bigl(\mathbb{C}^{n},\mathbb{C}^{n^{\prime\prime}}\bigr)
,
(\operatorname{Sym}(2r_{2},\mathbb{C}),{\rm M}(r_{2},\mathbb{C}))
,
(\operatorname{Alt}(2r_{2},\mathbb{C}),{\rm M}(r_{2},\mathbb{C}))
,
({\rm M}(r_{2},\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Sym}(r_{2},\mathbb{C}))
,
({\rm M}(2r_{2},\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Alt}(2r_{2},\mathbb{C}))
,

(\operatorname{Herm}(3,\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}},\operatorname{Alt}(6,\mathbb{C}))
), and may assume
f(x_{2})=\Delta_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})
.
When
\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)=\bigl(\mathbb{C}^{n},\mathbb{C}^{n^{\prime\prime}}\bigr)
, the theorem follows from (
6.7
) and (
6.8
).
When
\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)=(\operatorname{Sym}(2r_{2},\mathbb{C}),\allowbreak{\rm M}(r_{2},\mathbb{C}))
,
(\operatorname{Alt}(2r_{2},\mathbb{C}),{\rm M}(r_{2},\mathbb{C}))
, (
6.6
) follows from (
3.9
) and (
3.10
), and hence the theorem holds.

Next we consider the cases
\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)=({\rm M}(r_{2},\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Sym}(r_{2},\mathbb{C}))
,
d=2
,
\varepsilon_{2}=1
, and
\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)=({\rm M}(2r_{2},\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Alt}(2r_{2},\mathbb{C}))
,
d=2
,
\varepsilon_{2}=2
.
We prove the theorem by induction on
r_{2}
. When
r_{2}=1
, if
\varepsilon_{2}=1
, then since
\mathfrak{p}^{+}=\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
holds, the theorem follows from Corollary
2.4
.
Similarly, if
\varepsilon_{2}=2
, then the theorem follows from (
6.8
). Next we assume the theorem for
r_{2}-1
, and prove it for
r_{2}
.
By (
3.3
), we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{2}}\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{2}}\right)^{k_{r_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{\lambda+\frac{2k_{r_{2}}}{\varepsilon_{2}}-\frac{n}{r}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\left(\lambda+\frac{2k_{r_{2}}}{\varepsilon_{2}}\right)_{\phi_{\varepsilon_{2}}\bigl(\mathbf{k}-\underline{k_{r_{2}}}_{r_{2}}\bigr),d}\Bigl\langle\Delta_{\mathbf{k}-\underline{k_{r_{2}}}_{r_{2}}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\Bigr\rangle_{\lambda+\frac{2k_{r_{2}}}{\varepsilon_{2}},x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=(\lambda)_{\underline{2k_{r_{2}}/\varepsilon_{2}}_{\varepsilon_{2}r_{2}},d}\left(\lambda+\frac{2k_{r_{2}}}{\varepsilon_{2}}\right)_{\phi_{\varepsilon_{2}}\bigl(\mathbf{k}-\underline{k_{r_{2}}}_{r_{2}}\bigr),d}\bigl\langle\Delta_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=(\lambda)_{\phi_{\varepsilon_{2}}\bigl(\mathbf{k}-\underline{k_{r_{2}}}_{r_{2}}\bigr)+\underline{\frac{2k_{r_{2}}}{\varepsilon_{2}}}_{\varepsilon_{2}r_{2}},d}\,\bigl\langle\Delta_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x},

and by Proposition
3.1
and the induction hypothesis, this is holomorphic for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
. Now we have

\displaystyle\phi_{1}\bigl(\mathbf{k}-\underline{k_{r_{2}}}_{r_{2}}\bigr)_{a}+2k_{r_{2}}=\min\{k_{i}+k_{j}\mid 1\leq i<j\leq r_{2},\,i+j=2a+1\},

\displaystyle 1\leq a\leq r_{2},

\displaystyle\phi_{2}\bigl(\mathbf{k}-\underline{k_{r_{2}}}_{r_{2}}\bigr)_{a}+k_{r_{2}}=\min\left\{\left\lfloor\frac{k_{i}+k_{j}}{2}\right\rfloor\ \middle|\ 1\leq i\leq j\leq r_{2},\,i+j=a+1\right\},\quad

\displaystyle 1\leq a\leq 2r_{2},

and therefore by Corollary
2.4
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)\subset\bigoplus_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r_{2}}\\
m_{a}\leq k_{i}+k_{j},\,1\leq i<j\leq r_{2},\,i+j=2a+1\end{subarray}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}),\qquad

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{2}=1,

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)\subset\bigoplus_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{2r_{2}}\\
m_{a}\leq\big\lfloor\frac{k_{i}+k_{j}}{2}\big\rfloor,\,1\leq i\leq j\leq r_{2},\,i+j=a+1\end{subarray}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}),\qquad

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{2}=2.

On the other hand, by (
3.7
) and (
3.8
),

\displaystyle\operatorname{Hom}_{{\rm U}(r_{2})}(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}(\operatorname{Sym}(r_{2},\mathbb{C})),\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}({\rm M}(r_{2},\mathbb{C})))\neq\{0\}\qquad

implies

\displaystyle m_{r_{2}-i}\leq k_{r_{2}-2i},

\displaystyle\operatorname{Hom}_{{\rm U}(2r_{2})}(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}(\operatorname{Alt}(2r_{2},\mathbb{C})),\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}({\rm M}(2r_{2},\mathbb{C})))\neq\{0\}\qquad

implies

\displaystyle m_{2r_{2}-i}\leq\left\lfloor\frac{k_{r_{2}-i}}{2}\right\rfloor,

and combining with the above formulas, we get

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}(\operatorname{Sym}(r_{2},\mathbb{C}))\subset\bigoplus_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r_{2}}\\
m_{a}\leq k_{i}+k_{j},\,1\leq i<j\leq r_{2}+1,\,i+j=2a+1\end{subarray}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}({\rm M}(r_{2},\mathbb{C})),

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}(\operatorname{Alt}(2r_{2},\mathbb{C}))\subset\bigoplus_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{2r_{2}}\\
m_{a}\leq\big\lfloor\frac{k_{i}+k_{j}}{2}\big\rfloor,\,1\leq i\leq j\leq r_{2}+1,\,i+j=a+1\end{subarray}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}({\rm M}(2r_{2},\mathbb{C})),

with
k_{r_{2}+1}=0
. Hence by Corollary
2.4
,
(\lambda)_{\phi_{\varepsilon_{2}}(\mathbf{k}),d}\,\bigl\langle\Delta_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x}

is holomorphic for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
.

Now the case
\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)=(\operatorname{Herm}(3,\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}},\operatorname{Alt}(6,\mathbb{C}))
is remaining, but we postpone the proof for this case until Section
7
.
∎

By Theorem
6.1
and Proposition
2.6
(1), we get the following.

Corollary 6.4
.

Let
\operatorname{Re}\lambda>p-1
,
\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r_{2}}
, and let
f(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)
. Then we have

\|f(x_{2})\|_{\lambda,x}^{2}=C_{\varepsilon_{2}}^{d,d_{2}}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})\|f(z_{2})\|_{F,z}^{2},

where
C_{\varepsilon_{2}}^{d,d_{2}}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})
is as in (
6.1
) and (
6.2
).

Next we give a rough estimate of zeroes of
(\lambda)_{\phi_{\varepsilon_{2}}(\mathbf{k}),d}C_{\varepsilon_{2}}^{d,d_{2}}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})
.

Proposition 6.5
.

For
\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r_{2}}
, let
C_{\varepsilon_{2}}^{d,d_{2}}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})
,
\phi_{\varepsilon_{2}}(\mathbf{k})
be as in
(
6.1
)–(
6.4
)
.

(1)

Let
\varepsilon_{2}=1
,
r_{2}=2
. Then we have

\big\{\lambda\in\mathbb{C}\mid(\lambda)_{\phi_{1}(\mathbf{k}),d}C_{1}^{d,d_{2}}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})=0\big\}\subset\begin{cases}\big[\frac{d-d_{2}}{2}-(k_{1}+k_{2})+1,\frac{d-d_{2}}{2}-k_{1}\big],&k_{2}>0,\\
\varnothing,&k_{2}=0.\end{cases}

(2)

Let
\varepsilon_{2}=1
,
d_{2}=d/2
. For
2\leq m_{1}\leq m_{2}\leq r_{2}
, if
k_{2}=\cdots=k_{m_{1}}
and
k_{m_{2}+1}=0
, then we have

\displaystyle\big\{\lambda\in\mathbb{C}\mid(\lambda)_{\phi_{1}(\mathbf{k}),d}C_{1}^{d,d_{2}}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})=0\big\}

\displaystyle\quad\qquad{}\subset\begin{cases}\left[\frac{d}{4}(m_{1}-1)-(k_{1}+k_{2})+1,\,\frac{d}{4}(2m_{2}-3)-k_{m_{2}-1}\right],&k_{1}>k_{2}>0,\\
\left[\frac{d}{4}\left(2\left\lceil\frac{m_{1}}{2}\right\rceil-1\right)-(k_{1}+k_{2})+1,\,\frac{d}{4}(2m_{2}-3)-k_{m_{2}-1}\right],&k_{1}=k_{2}>0,\\
\varnothing,&k_{2}=0.\end{cases}

(3)

Let
\varepsilon_{2}=2
. For
1\leq m_{1},m_{2},m_{3}\leq r_{2}
with
m_{1},m_{2}\leq m_{3}
, if
k_{1}-k_{m_{1}}\leq 1
,
k_{a}=1
for
m_{2}+1\leq a\leq m_{3}
and
k_{m_{3}+1}=0
, then we have

\displaystyle\big\{\lambda\in\mathbb{C}\mid(\lambda)_{\phi_{2}(\mathbf{k}),d}C_{2}^{d,d_{2}}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})=0\big\}

\displaystyle\quad\qquad{}\subset\begin{cases}\left[\frac{d}{2}m_{1}-k_{1}+\frac{3}{2},\,\frac{d}{2}(m_{2}+m_{3}-1)-\frac{k_{m_{2}}}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\right],&k_{1}\geq 2,\,m_{2}<m_{3},\\[4.0pt]
\left[\frac{d}{2}m_{1}-k_{1}+\frac{3}{2},\,\frac{d}{2}(2m_{3}-1)-\left\lceil\frac{k_{m_{3}}}{2}\right\rceil+\frac{1}{2}\right],&k_{1}\geq 2,\,m_{2}=m_{3},\\
\varnothing,&k_{1}\leq 1.\end{cases}

Especially, for
m=1,2,\dots,\varepsilon_{2}r_{2}-1
, if
\phi_{\varepsilon_{2}}(\mathbf{k})_{m+1}=0
and
\phi_{\varepsilon_{2}}(\mathbf{k})_{m}\neq 0
,
then
C_{\varepsilon_{2}}^{d,d_{2}}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})
is non-zero holomorphic at
\lambda=\frac{d}{2}m
, and has a pole at
\lambda=\frac{d}{2}(m-1)
.

Proof.

(1) Clear from
(\lambda)_{\phi_{1}(\mathbf{k}),d}C_{1}^{d,d_{2}}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})=\bigl(\lambda+k_{1}-\frac{d-d_{2}}{2}\bigr)_{k_{2}}
.

(2) By direct computation, we have

\phi_{1}(\mathbf{k})_{a}=\begin{cases}k_{1}+k_{2},&a=1,\\
2k_{2},&2\leq a\leq\lfloor m_{1}/2\rfloor,\\
\min\{k_{1}+k_{m_{1}+1},2k_{2}\},&a=\frac{m_{1}+1}{2},\\
0,&a\geq m_{2}+1,\end{cases}

and

\displaystyle C_{1}^{d,d/2}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})=C_{1}^{d,d/2}(\lambda,(k_{1},\dots,k_{m_{2}}))

\displaystyle\quad{}=\frac{1}{\prod_{a=1}^{\lfloor m_{1}/2\rfloor}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{\phi_{1}(\mathbf{k})_{a}}}\frac{\prod_{a=2\lceil m_{1}/2\rceil}^{2m_{2}-2}\prod_{i=\max\{1,a+1-m_{2}\}}^{\lfloor a/2\rfloor}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(a-1)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{a+1-i}}}{\prod_{a=2\lceil m_{1}/2\rceil}^{2m_{2}-1}\prod_{i=\max\{1,a+1-m_{2}\}}^{\lceil a/2\rceil}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(a-1)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{a+2-i}}}

\displaystyle\quad{}\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\begin{cases}\displaystyle\frac{\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(m_{1}-1)\right)_{k_{1}+k_{2}}}{\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(m_{1}-1)\right)_{k_{1}+k_{m_{1}+1}}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(m_{1}-1)\right)_{2k_{2}}},&m_{1}\colon\text{odd},\\
1,&m_{1}\colon\text{even}.\end{cases}

For
2\leq a\leq 2m_{2}-2
, let

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\phi^{\prime}(\mathbf{k})_{a}:=\begin{cases}\min\{k_{i}+k_{j}\mid 1\leq i<j\leq m_{2}+1,\,i+j=a+2\},&a\colon\text{even},\\
{\min}_{2}\{k_{i}+k_{j}\mid 1\leq i<j\leq m_{2}+1,\,i+j=a+2\},&a\colon\text{odd},\end{cases}\\
&\psi^{\prime}(\mathbf{k})_{a}:=\max\{k_{i}+k_{j}\mid 1\leq i<j\leq m_{2},\,i+j=a+1\},\end{split}

where
{\min}_{2}
denotes the second smallest element. Then we have

\displaystyle\big\{\lambda\in\mathbb{C}\mid(\lambda)_{\phi_{1}(\mathbf{k}),d}C_{1}^{d,d/2}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})=0\big\}

\displaystyle\quad{}=\bigcup_{a=2\lceil m_{1}/2\rceil+1,\text{odd}}^{2m_{2}-3}\left\{\lambda\in\mathbb{C}\,\middle|\,\begin{array}[]{@{}l@{}}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(a-1)\right)_{\phi_{1}(\mathbf{k})_{\lceil a/2\rceil}}\\
\displaystyle{}\times\frac{\prod_{i=\max\{1,a+1-r_{2}\}}^{\lfloor a/2\rfloor}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(a-1)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{a+1-i}}}{\prod_{i=\max\{1,a+1-r_{2}\}}^{\lceil a/2\rceil}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(a-1)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{a+2-i}}}=0\end{array}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad{}\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\cup\bigcup_{a=2\lceil m_{1}/2\rceil,\text{even}}^{2m_{2}-2}\left\{\lambda\in\mathbb{C}\,\middle|\,\frac{\prod_{i=\max\{1,a+1-r_{2}\}}^{\lfloor a/2\rfloor}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(a-1)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{a+1-i}}}{\prod_{i=\max\{1,a+1-r_{2}\}}^{\lceil a/2\rceil}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(a-1)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{a+2-i}}}=0\right\}

\displaystyle\quad{}\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\cup\begin{cases}\displaystyle\left\{\lambda\in\mathbb{C}\,\middle|\,\frac{\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(m_{1}-1)\right)_{k_{1}+k_{2}}}{\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(m_{1}-1)\right)_{\max\{k_{1}+k_{m_{1}+1},2k_{2}\}}}=0\right\},&m_{1}\colon\text{odd},\\
\varnothing,&m_{1}\colon\text{even}\end{cases}

\displaystyle\quad{}\subset\bigcup_{a=2\lceil m_{1}/2\rceil}^{2m_{2}-2}\left\{\lambda\in\mathbb{C}\,\middle|\,\frac{\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(a-1)\right)_{\psi^{\prime}(\mathbf{k})_{a}}}{\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(a-1)\right)_{\phi^{\prime}(\mathbf{k})_{a}}}=0\right\}

\displaystyle\quad{}\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\cup\begin{cases}\displaystyle\left\{\lambda\in\mathbb{C}\,\middle|\,\frac{\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(m_{1}-1)\right)_{k_{1}+k_{2}}}{\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(m_{1}-1)\right)_{\max\{k_{1}+k_{m_{1}+1},2k_{2}\}}}=0\right\},&\begin{pmatrix}m_{1}\colon\text{odd},\\
k_{1}>k_{2}\end{pmatrix},\\
\varnothing,&\text{otherwise}\end{cases}

\displaystyle\quad{}\subset\bigcup_{\begin{subarray}{c}a=m_{1}\,(k_{1}>k_{2})\\
a=2\lceil m_{1}/2\rceil\,(k_{1}=k_{2})\end{subarray}}^{2m_{2}-2}\left\{\frac{d}{4}(a-1)-j\,\middle|\,j\in\mathbb{Z},\,\phi^{\prime}(\mathbf{k})_{a}\leq j\leq\psi^{\prime}(\mathbf{k})_{a}-1\right\}

\displaystyle\quad{}\subset\begin{cases}\left[\frac{d}{4}(m_{1}-1)-(k_{1}+k_{2})+1,\frac{d}{4}(2m_{2}-3)-k_{m_{2}-1}\right],&k_{1}>k_{2},\\
\left[\frac{d}{4}\left(2\left\lceil\frac{m_{1}}{2}\right\rceil-1\right)-(k_{1}+k_{2})+1,\frac{d}{4}(2m_{2}-3)-k_{m_{2}-1}\right],&k_{1}=k_{2}.\end{cases}

When
k_{2}=0
, by direct computation we have

(\lambda)_{\phi_{1}(\mathbf{k}),d}C_{1}^{d,d/2}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})=\frac{(\lambda)_{k_{1}}}{(\lambda)_{k_{1}}}=1,

and this is non-zero everywhere.

If
\phi_{1}(\mathbf{k})_{m+1}=0
and
\phi_{1}(\mathbf{k})_{m}\neq 0
, then
k_{m+1}=0
and
k_{m}\neq 0
hold, and by the above formula
(\lambda)_{\phi_{1}(\mathbf{k}),d}C_{1}^{d,d_{2}}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})
is non-zero
at
\lambda=\frac{d}{2}m
,
\frac{d}{2}(m-1)
. Hence we get the last claim.

(3) It is enough to prove for the following three cases:

(i)

1\leq m_{1}\leq m_{2}\leq m_{3}\leq r_{2}
,
k_{m_{2}}\geq 2
.

(ii)

1\leq m_{2}<m_{1}=m_{3}\leq r_{2}
,
k_{m_{2}}\geq 2
, i.e.,
\mathbf{k}=(\underbrace{2,\dots,2}_{m_{2}},\underbrace{1,\dots,1}_{m_{3}-m_{2}},\underbrace{0,\dots,0}_{r_{2}-m_{3}})
.

(iii)

k_{1}\leq 1
, i.e.,
\mathbf{k}=(1,\dots,1,0,\dots,0)
.

First we consider Case (i). We take
1\leq m_{0}\leq m_{1}
such that
k_{a}=k_{1}
for
1\leq a\leq m_{0}
and
k_{a}=k_{1}-1
for
m_{0}+1\leq a\leq m_{1}
.
By direct computation we have

\phi_{2}(\mathbf{k})_{a}=\begin{cases}k_{1},&1\leq a\leq m_{0},\\
k_{1}-1,&m_{0}+1\leq a\leq m_{1},\\
\left\lfloor\frac{k_{1}+k_{m_{1}+1}}{2}\right\rfloor,&a=m_{1}+1,\\
1,&2m_{2}+1\leq a\leq m_{2}+m_{3},\\
0,&m_{2}+m_{3}+1\leq a,\end{cases}

and

\displaystyle C_{2}^{d,2d}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})=C_{2}^{d,2d}(\lambda,(k_{1},\dots,k_{m_{3}}))

\displaystyle\hphantom{C_2^{d,2d}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})}{}=\frac{1}{\prod_{a=1}^{m_{1}+1}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{\phi_{0}(\mathbf{k})_{a}}}\frac{\left(\lambda-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{d}{2}m_{1}\right)_{k_{1}}}{\left(\lambda-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{d}{2}m_{1}\right)_{\left\lceil\frac{k_{1}+k_{m_{1}+1}}{2}\right\rceil}}

\displaystyle\ \hphantom{C_{2}^{d,2d}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})=}{}\times\prod_{a=m_{1}+2}^{\min\{2m_{2},2m_{3}-1\}}\left(\frac{\prod_{i=\max\{1,a-m_{3}\}}^{\lceil a/2\rceil-1}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{\left\lfloor\frac{k_{i}+k_{a-i}}{2}\right\rfloor}}{\prod_{i=\max\{1,a-m_{3}\}}^{\lceil a/2\rceil}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{\left\lfloor\frac{k_{i}+k_{a+1-i}}{2}\right\rfloor}}\right.

\displaystyle\hphantom{C_2^{d,2d}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})=\times\prod_{a=m_1+2}^{\min\lx@text@lbrace 2m_2,2m_3-1\lx@text@rbrace}\Bigg(}{}\times\left.\frac{\prod_{i=\max\{1,a-m_{3}\}}^{\lfloor a/2\rfloor}\left(\lambda-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{\left\lceil\frac{k_{i}+k_{a-i}}{2}\right\rceil}}{\prod_{i=\max\{1,a-m_{3}\}}^{\lfloor a/2\rfloor}\left(\lambda-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{\left\lceil\frac{k_{i}+k_{a+1-i}}{2}\right\rceil}}\right)

\displaystyle\ \hphantom{C_{2}^{d,2d}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})=}{}\times\begin{cases}\displaystyle\frac{\prod_{a=\max\{2m_{2},m_{3}\}+1}^{m_{2}+m_{3}}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)+\frac{k_{a-m_{3}}-1}{2}\right)}{\prod_{a=2m_{2}+1}^{m_{2}+m_{3}}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)},&m_{2}<m_{3},\\
\displaystyle\frac{1}{\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(2m_{3}-1)\right)_{\lfloor k_{m_{3}}/2\rfloor}}\frac{\left(\lambda-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{d}{2}(2m_{3}-1)\right)_{k_{m_{3}}}}{\left(\lambda-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{d}{2}(2m_{3}-1)\right)_{\lceil k_{m_{3}}/2\rceil}},&m_{2}=m_{3}.\end{cases}

Let

\displaystyle\phi(\mathbf{k})_{a}:=\min\{k_{i}+k_{j}\mid 1\leq i\leq j\leq m_{3}+1,\,i+j=a+1\},

\displaystyle 1\leq a\leq 2m_{3},

\displaystyle\phi^{\prime}(\mathbf{k})_{a}:={\min}_{2}\{k_{i}+k_{j}\mid 1\leq i\leq j\leq m_{3}+1,\,i+j=a+1\},\qquad

\displaystyle 3\leq a\leq 2m_{3}-1,

\displaystyle\phi^{\prime\prime}(\mathbf{k})_{a}:=\min\{k_{i}+k_{j}\mid 1\leq i<j\leq m_{3}+1,\,i+j=a+1\},

\displaystyle 2\leq a\leq 2m_{3},

\displaystyle\psi(\mathbf{k})_{a}:=\max\{k_{i}+k_{j}\mid 1\leq i\leq j\leq m_{3},\,i+j=a\},

\displaystyle 2\leq a\leq 2m_{3},

\displaystyle\psi^{\prime\prime}(\mathbf{k})_{a}:=\max\{k_{i}+k_{j}\mid 1\leq i<j\leq m_{3},\,i+j=a\},

\displaystyle 3\leq a\leq 2m_{3}-1,

where
{\min}_{2}
denotes the second smallest element, so that
\phi_{2}(\mathbf{k})_{a}=\lfloor\phi(\mathbf{k})_{a}/2\rfloor
holds for
1\leq a\leq 2m_{3}
. Then since the zeroes of

\displaystyle\frac{\prod_{i=\max\{1,a-m_{3}\}}^{\lceil a/2\rceil-1}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{\left\lfloor\frac{k_{i}+k_{a-i}}{2}\right\rfloor}}{\prod_{i=\max\{1,a-m_{3}\}}^{\lceil a/2\rceil}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{\left\lfloor\frac{k_{i}+k_{a+1-i}}{2}\right\rfloor}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}\times\frac{\prod_{i=\max\{1,a-m_{3}\}}^{\lfloor a/2\rfloor}\left(\lambda-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{\left\lceil\frac{k_{i}+k_{a-i}}{2}\right\rceil}}{\prod_{i=\max\{1,a-m_{3}\}}^{\lfloor a/2\rfloor}\left(\lambda-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{\left\lceil\frac{k_{i}+k_{a+1-i}}{2}\right\rceil}}\times\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{\phi_{2}(\mathbf{k})_{a}}

are contained in

\displaystyle\left\{\lambda\in\mathbb{C}\,\middle|\,\frac{\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{\lfloor\psi^{\prime\prime}(\mathbf{k})_{a}/2\rfloor}}{\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{\lfloor\phi^{\prime}(\mathbf{k})_{a}/2\rfloor}}\frac{\left(\lambda-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{\lceil\psi(\mathbf{k})_{a}/2\rceil}}{\left(\lambda-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{\lceil\phi^{\prime\prime}(\mathbf{k})_{a}/2\rceil}}=0\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad{}\subset\left\{\lambda\in\mathbb{C}\ \middle|\ \frac{(2\lambda-1-d(a-1))_{\psi(\mathbf{k})_{a}}}{(2\lambda-1-d(a-1))_{\phi(\mathbf{k})_{a}}}=0\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad{}=\left\{\frac{d}{2}(a-1)-\frac{j}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\ \middle|\ j\in\mathbb{Z},\,\phi(\mathbf{k})_{a}\leq j\leq\psi(\mathbf{k})_{a}-1\right\}

for
m_{1}+2\leq a\leq\min\{2m_{2},2m_{3}-1\}
, we have

\displaystyle\big\{\lambda\in\mathbb{C}\mid(\lambda)_{\phi_{2}(\mathbf{k}),d}C_{2}^{d,2d}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})=0\big\}

\displaystyle\subset\left\{\frac{d}{2}m_{1}-j+\frac{1}{2}\ \middle|\ j\in\mathbb{Z},\,\left\lceil\frac{k_{1}+k_{m_{1}+1}}{2}\right\rceil\leq j\leq k_{1}-1\right\}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\cup\bigcup_{a=m_{1}+2}^{\min\{2m_{2},2m_{3}-1\}}\left\{\frac{d}{2}(a-1)-\frac{j}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\ \middle|\ j\in\mathbb{Z},\,\phi(\mathbf{k})_{a}\leq j\leq\psi(\mathbf{k})_{a}-1\right\}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\cup\begin{cases}\left\{\frac{d}{2}(a-1)-\frac{k_{a-m_{3}}}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\ \middle|\ a\in\mathbb{Z},\,\max\{2m_{2},m_{3}\}+1\leq a\leq m_{2}+m_{3}\right\},&m_{2}<m_{3},\\[5.0pt]
\left\{\frac{d}{2}(2m_{3}-1)-j+\frac{1}{2}\ \middle|\ j\in\mathbb{Z},\,\left\lceil\frac{k_{m_{3}}}{2}\right\rceil\leq j\leq k_{m_{3}}-1\right\},&m_{2}=m_{3}\end{cases}

\displaystyle\subset\begin{cases}\left[\frac{d}{2}m_{1}-k_{1}+\frac{3}{2},\,\frac{d}{2}(m_{2}+m_{3}-1)-\frac{k_{m_{2}}}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\right],&m_{2}<m_{3},\\[5.0pt]
\left[\frac{d}{2}m_{1}-k_{1}+\frac{3}{2},\,\frac{d}{2}(2m_{3}-1)-\left\lceil\frac{k_{m_{3}}}{2}\right\rceil+\frac{1}{2}\right],&m_{2}=m_{3}.\end{cases}

Next we consider Case (ii). By direct computation we have

\phi_{2}(\mathbf{k})=(\underbrace{2,\dots,2}_{m_{2}},\underbrace{1,\dots,1}_{m_{3}},\underbrace{0,\dots,0}_{2r_{2}-m_{2}-m_{3}}),\qquad C_{1}^{d,2d}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})=\frac{\prod_{a=m_{3}+1}^{m_{2}+m_{3}}\left(\lambda+\frac{1}{2}-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)}{(\lambda)_{\phi_{2}(\mathbf{k}),d}},

and since
m_{1}=m_{3}
, we get

\displaystyle\big\{\lambda\in\mathbb{C}\mid(\lambda)_{\phi_{2}(\mathbf{k}),d}C_{2}^{d,2d}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})=0\big\}

\displaystyle=\left\{\frac{d}{2}(a-1)-\frac{1}{2}\,\middle|\,a\in\mathbb{Z},\,m_{3}+1\leq a\leq m_{2}+m_{3}\right\}

\displaystyle\subset\left[\frac{d}{2}m_{1}-2+\frac{3}{2},\,\frac{d}{2}(m_{2}+m_{3}-1)-\frac{2}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\right].

Similarly, for Case (iii), when
k_{1}=\cdots=k_{m_{3}}=1
and
k_{m_{3}+1}=0
,
0\leq m_{3}\leq r_{2}
, by direct computation we have

(\lambda)_{\phi_{2}(\mathbf{k}),d}C_{1}^{d,2d}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})=\frac{\prod_{a=1}^{m_{3}}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)}{\prod_{a=1}^{m_{3}}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)}=1,

and this is non-zero everywhere.

If
\phi_{2}(\mathbf{k})_{m+1}=0
and
\phi_{2}(\mathbf{k})_{m}\neq 0
, then
k_{m_{2}}\geq 2
,
k_{m_{2}+1}=\cdots=k_{m_{3}}=1
and
k_{m_{3}+1}=0
hold for some
m_{2}\leq m_{3}
with
m_{2}+m_{3}=m
, and by the above formula

(\lambda)_{\phi_{2}(\mathbf{k}),d}C_{2}^{d,2d}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})
is non-zero at
\lambda=\frac{d}{2}m
,
\frac{d}{2}(m-1)
. Hence we get the last claim.
∎

Especially, if
\phi_{\varepsilon_{2}}(\mathbf{k})_{a+1}=0
and
\phi_{\varepsilon_{2}}(\mathbf{k})_{a}\neq 0
, then for a non-zero polynomial
f(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)
,

\bigl\langle f(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x}
has a pole at
\lambda=\frac{d}{2}(a-1)
,
and combining with Corollary
2.4
and (
6.6
), we have

\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)\subset\bigoplus_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{\varepsilon_{2}r_{2}}\\
m_{a+1}=0\end{subarray}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}),\qquad\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)\not\subset\bigoplus_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{\varepsilon_{2}r_{2}}\\
m_{a}=0\end{subarray}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}),

that is, for
a=0,1,\dots,r-1
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)\cap\bigoplus_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}\\
m_{a+1}=0\end{subarray}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})

\displaystyle=\bigoplus_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r_{2}}\\
\phi_{\varepsilon_{2}}(\mathbf{k})_{a+1}=0\end{subarray}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\displaystyle\bigoplus_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r_{2}}\\
k_{a+1}=0\end{subarray}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr),&\varepsilon_{2}=1,\\
\displaystyle\bigoplus_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leq a_{1}\leq a_{2}\leq r_{2}\\
a_{1}+a_{2}\leq a\end{subarray}}\,\bigoplus_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{a_{1}}\\
k_{a_{1}}\geq 2\end{subarray}}\mathcal{P}_{(\mathbf{k},\underline{1}_{a_{2}-a_{1}},\underline{0}_{r_{2}-a_{2}})}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr),&\varepsilon_{2}=2,\end{cases}

(6.9)

where we set
\phi_{\varepsilon_{2}}(\mathbf{k})_{\varepsilon_{2}r_{2}+1}=\cdots=\phi_{\varepsilon_{2}}(\mathbf{k})_{r}:=0
and
k_{0}:=+\infty
.

Next we consider general
\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r_{2}}
. Then again by the above proposition,

\frac{1}{C_{\varepsilon_{2}}^{d,d_{2}}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})}\bigl\langle f(x_{2}),\allowbreak{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x}
is holomorphic
for
\operatorname{Re}\lambda\geq\frac{d}{2}(\varepsilon_{2}r_{2}-1)+1-\big\lfloor\frac{k_{r_{2}}}{\varepsilon_{2}}\big\rfloor
.
Then by Theorem
3.5
, comparing the top terms, we get the following.

Theorem 6.6
.

Suppose
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
are of tube type, and consider the meromorphic continuation of
\langle\cdot,\cdot\rangle_{\lambda}
.
Then for
\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r_{2}}
,
a=1,2,\dots,\lfloor k_{r_{2}}/\varepsilon_{2}\rfloor
and for
f(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{C_{\varepsilon_{2}}^{d,d_{2}}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})}\bigl\langle f(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x}\biggr|_{\lambda=\frac{n}{r}-a}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad{}=\frac{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{a}}{C_{\varepsilon_{2}}^{d,d_{2}}\bigl(\frac{n}{r}+a,\mathbf{k}-\underline{\varepsilon_{2}a}_{r_{2}}\bigr)}\bigl\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{-\varepsilon_{2}a}f(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\frac{n}{r}+a,x}.

6.2
Results on restriction of
\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D)}
to subgroups

Next we consider the decomposition of the holomorphic discrete series representation of scalar type
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D)
under the subgroup
\widetilde{G}_{1}\subset\widetilde{G}
.
By Theorem
2.5
, we have

\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D)|_{\widetilde{G}_{1}}\simeq\sideset{}{{}^{\oplus}}{\sum}_{\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r_{2}}}\mathcal{H}_{\varepsilon_{1}\lambda}\bigl(D_{1},\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)\bigr),

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{\varepsilon_{1}\lambda}\bigl(D_{1},\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)\bigr)

\displaystyle\simeq\begin{cases}\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+|\mathbf{k}|}\bigl(D_{{\rm SO}_{0}(2,n^{\prime})}\bigr)\boxtimes V_{(k_{1}-k_{2},0,\dots,0)}^{[n^{\prime\prime}]\vee}&(\text{Case }1),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}\bigl(D_{{\rm Sp}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{R})},V_{\mathbf{k}}^{(r^{\prime})\vee}\bigr)\mathbin{\hat{\boxtimes}}\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}\bigl(D_{{\rm Sp}(r^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{R})},V_{\mathbf{k}}^{(r^{\prime\prime})\vee}\bigr)&(\text{Case }2),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}\bigl(D_{{\rm SO}^{*}(2s^{\prime})},V_{\mathbf{k}}^{(s^{\prime})\vee}\bigr)\mathbin{\hat{\boxtimes}}\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}\bigl(D_{{\rm SO}^{*}(2s^{\prime\prime})},V_{\mathbf{k}}^{(s^{\prime\prime})\vee}\bigr)&(\text{Case }3),\\
\mathcal{H}_{2\lambda}\bigl(D_{{\rm SO}^{*}(2r)},V_{2\mathbf{k}}^{(r)\vee}\bigr)&(\text{Case }4),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}\bigl(D_{{\rm Sp}(r,\mathbb{R})},V_{\mathbf{k}^{2}}^{(r)\vee}\bigr)&(\text{Case }5),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}\bigl(D_{{\rm SU}(2,6)},\mathbb{C}\boxtimes V_{(k_{1}+k_{2},k_{1}+k_{3},k_{2}+k_{3})^{2}}^{(6)}\bigr)&(\text{Case }6),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}\Bigl(D_{{\rm SO}^{*}(12)},V_{\left(\frac{k_{1}+k_{2}}{2},\frac{k_{1}+k_{2}}{2},\frac{k_{1}+k_{2}}{2},\frac{k_{1}+k_{2}}{2},\frac{k_{1}-k_{2}}{2},\frac{-k_{1}+k_{2}}{2}\right)}^{(6)\vee}\Bigr)\boxtimes V_{(k_{1},k_{2})}^{(2)\vee}&(\text{Case }7),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+|\mathbf{k}|}(D_{{\rm SL}(2,\mathbb{R})})\hskip-1.0pt\mathbin{\hat{\boxtimes}}\hskip-1.0pt\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+\frac{|\mathbf{k}|}{2}}\!\Bigl(D_{{\rm Spin}_{0}(2,10)},V_{\left(\frac{k_{1}+k_{2}}{2},\frac{k_{1}-k_{2}}{2},\frac{k_{1}-k_{2}}{2},\frac{k_{1}-k_{2}}{2},\frac{k_{1}-k_{2}}{2}\right)}^{[10]\vee}\Bigr)\!&(\text{Case }8),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}\bigl(D_{{\rm SU}(2,4)},\mathbb{C}\boxtimes V_{(k_{1}+k_{2},k_{1}+k_{2},k_{1},k_{2})}^{(4)}\bigr)\boxtimes V_{(k_{1},k_{2})}^{(2)\vee}&(\text{Case }9),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+\frac{|\mathbf{k}|}{2}}(D_{{\rm SL}(2,\mathbb{R})})\mathbin{\hat{\boxtimes}}\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}\bigl(D_{{\rm SU}(1,5)},\mathbb{C}\boxtimes V_{(k_{1}+k_{2},k_{1},k_{1},k_{2},k_{2})}^{(5)}\bigr)&(\text{Case }10).\end{cases}

For each
\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r_{2}}
, let
V_{\mathbf{k}}
be an abstract
K_{1}
-module isomorphic to
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)
,
let
\|\cdot\|_{\varepsilon_{1}\lambda,\mathbf{k}}
be the
\widetilde{G}_{1}
-invariant norm on
\mathcal{H}_{\varepsilon_{1}\lambda}(D_{1},V_{\mathbf{k}})

normalized such that
\|v\|_{\varepsilon_{1}\lambda,\mathbf{k}}=|v|_{V_{\mathbf{k}}}
holds for all constant functions
v\in V_{\mathbf{k}}
,
and for
\lambda>p-1
let

\mathcal{F}_{\lambda,\mathbf{k}}^{\downarrow}\colon\ \mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D)|_{\widetilde{G}_{1}}\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\varepsilon_{1}\lambda}(D_{1},V_{\mathbf{k}})

be the symmetry breaking operator given in (
2.18
) and (
2.19
), using a vector-valued polynomial

\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{k}}(x_{2})\in\bigl(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)\otimes\overline{V_{\mathbf{k}}}\bigr)^{K_{1}}
satisfying

\big|\langle f(x_{2}),\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{k}}(x_{2})\rangle_{F,x}\big|_{V_{\mathbf{k}}}=\|f(z_{2})\|_{F,z},\qquad f(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr),

so that

\big\|\mathcal{F}_{\lambda,\mathbf{k}}^{\downarrow}(f(x_{2}))\big\|_{\varepsilon_{1}\lambda,\mathbf{k}}=\|f(z_{2})\|_{F,z},\qquad f(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)

holds. Also, when
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
is of tube type, we fix
[K_{1},K_{1}]
-isomorphisms

V_{\mathbf{k}+\underline{a}_{r_{2}}}\simeq V_{\mathbf{k}}
for each
a\in\mathbb{Z}_{>0}
.
Then by Proposition
2.6
(3) and Theorem
6.1
, the following Parseval–Plancherel-type formula holds.

Corollary 6.7
.

For
\lambda>p-1
and for
f\in\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D)
, we have

\|f\|_{\lambda}^{2}=\sum_{\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r_{2}}}C_{\varepsilon_{2}}^{d,d_{2}}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})\big\|\mathcal{F}_{\lambda,\mathbf{k}}^{\downarrow}f\big\|_{\varepsilon_{1}\lambda,\mathbf{k}}^{2},

where
