C_{\varepsilon_{2}}^{d,d_{2}}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})
is as in (
6.1
) and (
6.2
).

Next we consider the meromorphic continuation for smaller
\lambda
.
Then by Propositions
2.7
,
6.5
, Theorems
6.2
,
6.6
and the formula (
6.9
), we have the following.

Corollary 6.8
.

For
\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r_{2}}
, let
\phi_{\varepsilon_{2}}(\mathbf{k})\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{\varepsilon_{2}r_{2}}
be as in (
6.3
) and (
6.4
),
and set
\phi_{\varepsilon_{2}}(\mathbf{k})_{\varepsilon_{2}r_{2}+1}\allowbreak=\cdots=\phi_{\varepsilon_{2}}(\mathbf{k})_{r}:=0
.

(1)

For
a=1,2,\dots,r
,

{\rm d}\tau_{\lambda}(\mathcal{U}(\mathfrak{g}_{1}))\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)\subset M_{a}^{\mathfrak{g}}(\lambda)

holds if

\lambda\in\frac{d}{2}(a-1)-\phi_{\varepsilon_{2}}(\mathbf{k})_{a}-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},

where
M_{a}^{\mathfrak{g}}(\lambda)\subset\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)_{\widetilde{K}}
is the
\bigl(\mathfrak{g},\widetilde{K}\bigr)
-submodule given in (
2.12
).

(2)

For
a=0,1,\dots,r-1
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{\frac{d}{2}a}(D)|_{\widetilde{G}_{1}}

\displaystyle\simeq\sideset{}{{}^{\oplus}}{\sum}_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r_{2}}\\
\phi_{\varepsilon_{2}}(\mathbf{k})_{a+1}=0\end{subarray}}\mathcal{H}_{\varepsilon_{1}\frac{d}{2}a}\bigl(D_{1},\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)\bigr)

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\displaystyle\hskip 5.0pt\sideset{}{{}^{\oplus}}{\sum}_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r_{2}}\\
k_{a+1}=0\end{subarray}}\mathcal{H}_{\varepsilon_{1}\frac{d}{2}a}\bigl(D_{1},\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)\bigr),&\varepsilon_{2}=1,\\
\displaystyle\hskip 5.0pt\sideset{}{{}^{\oplus}}{\sum}_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leq a_{1}\leq a_{2}\leq r_{2}\\
a_{1}+a_{2}\leq a\end{subarray}}\hskip 6.0pt\sideset{}{{}^{\oplus}}{\sum}_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{a_{1}}\\
k_{a_{1}}\geq 2\end{subarray}}\mathcal{H}_{\varepsilon_{1}\frac{d}{2}a}\bigl(D_{1},\mathcal{P}_{(\mathbf{k},\underline{1}_{a_{2}-a_{1}},\underline{0}_{r_{2}-a_{2}})}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)\bigr),&\varepsilon_{2}=2,\end{cases}

where we set
k_{0}:=+\infty
.

(3)

For
a=0,1,\dots,r-1
, if
\phi_{\varepsilon_{2}}(\mathbf{k})_{a+1}=0
, then
\mathcal{F}_{\lambda,\mathbf{k}}^{\downarrow}
is holomorphic at
\lambda=\frac{d}{2}a
,
and its restriction gives the symmetry breaking operator

\mathcal{F}_{\frac{d}{2}a,\mathbf{k}}^{\downarrow}\colon\ \mathcal{H}_{\frac{d}{2}a}(D)|_{\widetilde{G}_{1}}\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\varepsilon_{1}\frac{d}{2}a}(D_{1},V_{\mathbf{k}}).

(4)

Suppose
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
are of tube type. For
a=1,2,\dots,\lfloor k_{r_{2}}/\varepsilon_{2}\rfloor
, if

\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{k}}(x_{2})=c\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{\varepsilon_{2}a}\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{k}-\underline{\varepsilon_{2}a}_{r_{2}}}(x_{2}),

then we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{\frac{n}{r}-a,\mathbf{k}}^{\downarrow}=c\mathcal{F}_{\frac{n}{r}+a,\mathbf{k}-\underline{\varepsilon_{2}a}_{r_{2}}}^{\downarrow}\circ\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\right)^{a}\colon

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\frac{n}{r}-a}(D)|_{\widetilde{G}_{1}}\longrightarrow\mathcal{O}_{\varepsilon_{1}\left(\frac{n}{r}-a\right)}(D_{1},V_{\mathbf{k}})\simeq\mathcal{O}_{\varepsilon_{1}\left(\frac{n}{r}+a\right)}\bigl(D_{1},V_{\mathbf{k}-\underline{\varepsilon_{2}a}_{r_{2}}}\bigr).

If
\mathbf{k}
satisfies the condition in Remark
6.3
(1), then “only if” in Corollary
6.8
(1) also holds.
Also, Corollary
6.8
(2) for
\lambda=\frac{d}{2}
(
a=1
case) is earlier given in
[
41
]
,
and that for
(G,G_{1})=({\rm SU}(r,r),{\rm SO}^{*}(2r))
is earlier given in
[
55
]
. See also
[
30
]
for
(G,G_{1})=({\rm O}(p,q),{\rm O}(p,q^{\prime})\times{\rm O}(q^{\prime\prime}))
case.
The parameter sets in Corollary
6.8
(2) also appear in Howe’s correspondence for the dual pairs
({\rm SO}^{*}(2r_{2}),{\rm Sp}(a))
,
({\rm Sp}(r_{2},\mathbb{R}),{\rm O}(a))
(see, e.g.,
[
1
,
24
,
52
]
),
and especially, we can prove (2) for Cases 2–5 by using the seesaw dual pair theory (see, e.g.,
[
18
, Section 3]
,
[
37
]
) as in
[
41
]
.

7
Case
\boldsymbol{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}}
is simple of rank 3

In the previous section, we skipped the proof of Theorem
6.2
for
\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)=(\operatorname{Herm}(3,\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}},\allowbreak\operatorname{Alt}(6,\mathbb{C}))
.
In this section we assume that
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
is simple,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
and
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
are both of tube type, and
\operatorname{rank}\mathfrak{p}^{+}=\operatorname{rank}\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}=3
, that is, we treat the cases

\displaystyle\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)=\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+},(\mathfrak{p}^{+})^{\sigma},(\mathfrak{p}^{+})^{-\sigma}\bigr)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad{}=\bigl(\operatorname{Herm}(3,\mathbb{F})^{\mathbb{C}},\operatorname{Alt}(3,\mathbb{F}^{\prime})^{\mathbb{C}},\operatorname{Herm}(3,\mathbb{F}^{\prime})^{\mathbb{C}}\bigr),\qquad(\mathbb{F},\mathbb{F}^{\prime})=(\mathbb{C},\mathbb{R}),(\mathbb{H},\mathbb{C}),(\mathbb{O},\mathbb{H})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad{}\simeq\begin{cases}({\rm M}(3,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Alt}(3,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Sym}(3,\mathbb{C}))&(\text{Case }1,\,(\mathbb{F},\mathbb{F}^{\prime})=(\mathbb{C},\mathbb{R})),\\
(\operatorname{Alt}(6,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Alt}(3,\mathbb{C})\oplus\operatorname{Alt}(3,\mathbb{C}),{\rm M}(3,\mathbb{C}))&(\text{Case }2,\,(\mathbb{F},\mathbb{F}^{\prime})=(\mathbb{H},\mathbb{C})),\\
\bigl(\operatorname{Herm}(3,\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}},{\rm M}(2,6;\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Alt}(6,\mathbb{C})\bigr)&(\text{Case }3,\,(\mathbb{F},\mathbb{F}^{\prime})=(\mathbb{O},\mathbb{H})).\end{cases}

Then the corresponding symmetric pairs are

(G,G_{1})=\begin{cases}({\rm SU}(3,3),{\rm SO}^{*}(6))&(\text{Case }1),\\
({\rm SO}^{*}(12),{\rm SO}^{*}(6)\times{\rm SO}^{*}(6))&(\text{Case }2),\\
\bigl(E_{7(-25)},{\rm SU}(2,6)\bigr)&(\text{Case }3),\end{cases}

and for these cases we have
d=\dim_{\mathbb{R}}\mathbb{F}
,
d_{2}=\dim_{\mathbb{R}}\mathbb{F}^{\prime}=\frac{d}{2}
,
\varepsilon_{2}=1
.
The purpose of this section is to prove Theorem
6.2
for these cases, that is,
for
\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{3}
,
f(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)
, we prove that

(\lambda)_{\phi_{1}(\mathbf{k}),d}\bigl\langle f(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x}

is holomorphically continued for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
, where

\phi_{1}(\mathbf{k}):=(k_{1}+k_{2},\min\{k_{1},k_{2}+k_{3}\},k_{3})\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{3}.

7.1
Preliminaries

First we prepare some notations. We fix a maximal tripotent
e\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}
, and regard
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
as Jordan algebras of rank 3.
Let
\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}\subset\mathfrak{n}^{+}
be the Euclidean real forms, let
\Omega\subset\mathfrak{n}^{+}
be the symmetric cone,
and consider the
\mathbb{C}
-bilinear form
(\cdot|\cdot)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}:=(\cdot|Q(\overline{e})\cdot)_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}\colon\mathfrak{p}^{+}\times\mathfrak{p}^{+}\to\mathbb{C}
,
and the determinant polynomials
\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x)
,
\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})
on
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
. Then
\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}
coincides with the restriction of
\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}
on
\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}
.
Let
K\subset G
,
K_{1}\subset G_{1}
be the maximal compact subgroups, with the complexification
K^{\mathbb{C}}
,
K_{1}^{\mathbb{C}}
,
and let
L_{2}\subset K_{1}^{\mathbb{C}}
be the subgroup with the Lie algebra
\mathfrak{l}_{2}=[\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2},\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{2}]\simeq\mathbb{R}\oplus\mathfrak{sl}(3,\mathbb{F}^{\prime})
, defined as in Section
2.2
.

Next we fix a Jordan frame
\{e_{1},e_{2},e_{3}\}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}
with
\sum_{j=1}^{3}e_{j}=e
,
set
e^{k}:=\sum_{j=1}^{k}e_{j}
,
\check{e}^{k}:=\sum_{j=4-k}^{3}e_{j}
,
k=1,2,3
,
and let
\Delta_{k}(x):=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}(e^{k})}(x)
,
\check{\Delta}_{k}(x):=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}(\check{e}^{k})}(x)
be the determinant polynomials on

\mathfrak{p}^{+}\bigl(e^{k}\bigr)_{2},\mathfrak{p}^{+}\bigl(\check{e}^{k}\bigr)_{2}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}
, regarded as polynomials on
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
, as in Section
2.4
.
Especially we have
\Delta_{3}(x)=\check{\Delta}_{3}(x)=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x)
.
Also, for
\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{+}^{3}
,
x_{2}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
, as in (
2.5
) let

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\Delta^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}_{\mathbf{m}}(x_{2})=\Delta_{1}(x_{2})^{m_{1}-m_{2}}\Delta_{2}(x_{2})^{m_{2}-m_{3}}\Delta_{3}(x_{2})^{m_{3}},\\
&\check{\Delta}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}_{\mathbf{m}}(x_{2})=\check{\Delta}_{1}(x_{2})^{m_{1}-m_{2}}\check{\Delta}_{2}(x_{2})^{m_{2}-m_{3}}\check{\Delta}_{3}(x_{2})^{m_{3}}.\end{split}

Let
M_{L_{2}}A_{L_{2}}N_{L_{2}}^{\top}\subset L_{2}
be the minimal parabolic subgroup defined as in Section
2.2
, so that

\Delta^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}_{\mathbf{m}}(x_{2})
is relatively invariant under the action of
M_{L_{2}}A_{L_{2}}N_{L_{2}}^{\top}
.

Next, for
x\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}
, let
x^{\sharp}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}
be the adjugate element, which is characterized by

x^{\sharp}:=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x)x^{\mathit{-1}}

for invertible
x
, so that

\Delta_{2}(x)=\check{\Delta}_{1}\bigl(x^{\sharp}\bigr),\qquad\check{\Delta}_{2}(x)=\Delta_{1}\bigl(x^{\sharp}\bigr)

hold. Then the following holds.

Lemma 7.1
.

Let
\mathfrak{p}^{+}=\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
be as above.

(1)

For
x,y\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}
, we have
\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x+y)=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x)+\bigl(x^{\sharp}|y\bigr)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}+\bigl(x|y^{\sharp}\bigr)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}+\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(y)
.

(2)

For
x_{1}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}
,
x_{2}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
, we have
(x_{j})^{\sharp}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
, and
(x_{1})^{\sharp}
is at most of rank
1
.

(3)

For
x_{1}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}
,
x_{2}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
, we have
\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x_{1}+x_{2})=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})+\bigl(x_{2}|(x_{1})^{\sharp}\bigr)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}
.

Proof.

(1) Since
\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}
is homogeneous of degree 3,
\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(sx+ty)
,
s,t\in\mathbb{C}
is of the form

\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(sx+ty)=f_{3}(x,y)s^{3}+f_{2}(x,y)s^{2}t+f_{1}(x,y)st^{2}+f_{0}(x,y)t^{3},

with
f_{j}(x,y)\in\mathcal{P}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+}\bigr)
. Then clearly we have

\displaystyle f_{3}(x,y)

\displaystyle=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(sx+ty)\bigr|_{s=1,t=0}=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x),

\displaystyle f_{0}(x,y)

\displaystyle=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(sx+ty)\bigr|_{s=0,t=1}=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(y),

and by
[
13
, Proposition III.4.2 (ii)]
, we have

\displaystyle f_{2}(x,y)

\displaystyle=\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(sx+ty)\biggr|_{s=1,t=0}=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x)\bigl(x^{\mathit{-1}}|y\bigr)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}=\bigl(x^{\sharp}|y\bigr)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}},

\displaystyle f_{1}(x,y)

\displaystyle=\frac{\partial}{\partial s}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(sx+ty)\biggr|_{s=0,t=1}=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(y)\bigl(x|y^{\mathit{-1}}\bigr)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}=\bigl(x|y^{\sharp}\bigr)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}.

Then by putting
s=t=1
, we get the desired formula.

(2) Since
\sigma_{-}:=-\sigma
acts on
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
as a Jordan algebra automorphism, for
x_{j}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{j}
we have

\sigma_{-}\bigl((x_{j})^{\sharp}\bigr)=(\sigma_{-}(x_{j}))^{\sharp}=(\mp x_{j})^{\sharp}=(\mp 1)^{2}(x_{j})^{\sharp}=(x_{j})^{\sharp},

and hence
(x_{j})^{\sharp}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
holds. Also, for
x_{1}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}
we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x_{1})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left(\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x_{1})+\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(\sigma_{-}(x_{1}))\right)=\frac{1}{2}\left(\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x_{1})+\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(-x_{1})\right)=0,

\displaystyle\bigl((x_{1})^{\sharp}\bigr)^{\sharp}

\displaystyle=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x_{1})x_{1}=0,

and hence for any
l\in K_{1}^{\mathbb{C}}
we have

\Delta_{2}\bigl(l(x_{1})^{\sharp}\bigr)=\check{\Delta}_{1}\bigl(\bigl(l(x_{1})^{\sharp}\bigr)^{\sharp}\bigr)=\check{\Delta}_{1}\bigl(l^{\sharp}\bigl((x_{1})^{\sharp}\bigr)^{\sharp}\bigr)=0,

where
l^{\sharp}:=\chi(l)^{2}l^{\top-1}
. Thus
(x_{1})^{\sharp}
is at most of rank 1.

(3) For
x=x_{2}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
,
y=x_{1}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}
, we have
\bigl((x_{2})^{\sharp}|x_{1}\bigr)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}=0
since
(x_{2})^{\sharp}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
is orthogonal to
x_{1}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}
,
and we have
\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x_{1})=0
by the above argument. Hence the desired formula follows from (1).
∎

7.2
Proof of theorem on poles

Now we give the proof of Theorem
6.2
when
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
are of tube type and of rank 3.

Proof of Theorem
6.2
for rank 3 cases.

By the
K_{1}
-equivariance, it is enough to prove the theorem when
f(x_{2})=\Delta^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}_{\mathbf{k}}(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)
,
\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{3}
.
By (
3.3
), for
z=z_{1}+z_{2}\in\Omega\subset\mathfrak{n}^{+}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}
, we have

\displaystyle\bigl\langle\Delta_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle=\frac{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+d+1}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{2k_{3}}_{3},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\!\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{2}}\right)\!\!{\vphantom{\biggr)}}^{k_{3}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{\lambda+2k_{3}-d-1}\bigl\langle\Delta_{(k_{1}-k_{3},k_{2}-k_{3},0)}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda+2k_{3},x}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{\underline{2k_{3}}_{3},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+d+1}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{2}}\right)^{k_{3}}\bigl(\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})+\bigl(z_{2}|z_{1}^{\sharp}\bigr)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\bigr)^{\lambda+2k_{3}-d-1}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\frac{\left(\lambda+k_{1}+k_{3}-\frac{d}{4}\right)_{k_{2}-k_{3}}}{(\lambda+2k_{3})_{(k_{1}+k_{2}-2k_{3},k_{2}-k_{3}),d}}{}_{2}F_{1}\left(\begin{matrix}-k_{2}+k_{3},-k_{1}+k_{3}-\frac{d}{4}\\
-\lambda-k_{1}-k_{2}+\frac{d}{4}+1\end{matrix};-\frac{\Delta_{2}(z_{1})}{\Delta_{2}(z_{2})}\right)\Delta_{\begin{subarray}{c}(k_{1}-k_{3},\hskip 7.0pt\\
\ k_{2}-k_{3},0)\end{subarray}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\!(z_{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{(-1)^{k_{2}-k_{3}}\left(-\lambda-k_{1}-k_{2}+\frac{d}{4}+1\right)_{k_{2}-k_{3}}}{(\lambda)_{(k_{1}+k_{2},k_{2}+k_{3},2k_{3}),d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+d+1}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{2}}\right)^{k_{3}}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{\lambda+k_{3}-d-1}\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\frac{(-\lambda-2k_{3}+d+1)_{m}}{m!}{}\,\biggl(-\frac{\bigl(z_{2}|z_{1}^{\sharp}\bigr)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}}{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})}\biggr)^{m}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\sum_{n=0}^{k_{2}-k_{3}}\frac{(-k_{2}+k_{3})_{n}\left(-k_{1}+k_{3}-\frac{d}{4}\right)_{n}}{\left(-\lambda-k_{1}-k_{2}+\frac{d}{4}+1\right)_{n}n!}\left(-\frac{\Delta_{2}(z_{1})}{\Delta_{2}(z_{2})}\right)^{n}\Delta_{(k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2}),

where we have used Proposition
3.1
, (
6.7
) and Lemma
7.1
(3) at the 2nd equality,
and used the binomial formula and the definition of
{}_{2}F_{1}
at the 3rd equality. Now we have

\displaystyle\biggl(-\frac{\bigl(z_{2}|z_{1}^{\sharp}\bigr)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}}{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})}\biggr)^{m}\in\mathcal{P}_{2m}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}\bigr)\otimes\mathcal{P}_{(0,-m,-m)}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr),

\displaystyle\left(-\frac{\Delta_{2}(z_{1})}{\Delta_{2}(z_{2})}\right)^{n}\Delta_{(k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{2n}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}\bigr)\otimes\mathcal{P}_{(k_{1}-n,k_{2}-n,k_{3})}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr),

where
\mathcal{P}_{m}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}\bigr)
denotes the space of all homogeneous polynomials on
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}
of degree
m
, since
z_{1}^{\sharp}
is at most of rank 1.
For
\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}^{3}
with
l_{1}\geq 0
,
0\leq l_{2}\leq k_{1}-k_{2}
,
n\leq l_{3}\leq k_{2}-k_{3}
,
|\mathbf{l}|=m+n
, we define the polynomials

\displaystyle F^{n}_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}(z_{1},z_{2})\in{}

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{2(m+n)}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}\bigr)\otimes\mathcal{P}_{(k_{1}+l_{1}-m-n,k_{2}+l_{2}-m-n,k_{3}+l_{3}-m-n)}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{P}_{2|\mathbf{l}|}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}\bigr)\otimes\mathcal{P}_{(k_{1}-l_{2}-l_{3},k_{2}-l_{1}-l_{3},k_{3}-l_{1}-l_{2})}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)

such that

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}^{3},\,|\mathbf{l}|=m+n\\
0\leq l_{1}\\
0\leq l_{2}\leq k_{1}-k_{2}\\
n\leq l_{3}\leq k_{2}-k_{3}\end{subarray}}F^{n}_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}(z_{1},z_{2})=\frac{1}{m!n!}\,\biggl(-\frac{\bigl(z_{2}|z_{1}^{\sharp}\bigr)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}}{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})}\biggr)^{m}\left(-\frac{\Delta_{2}(z_{1})}{\Delta_{2}(z_{2})}\right)^{n}\Delta_{(k_{1},k_{2},k_{3})}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})

(7.1)

holds. Then we have

\displaystyle\bigl\langle\Delta_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{(-1)^{k_{2}-k_{3}}}{(\lambda)_{(k_{1}+k_{2},k_{2}+k_{3},2k_{3}),d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+d+1}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{2}}\right)^{k_{3}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{\lambda+k_{3}-d-1}

\displaystyle\qquad{}\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\smash{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}^{3},\,0\leq l_{1}\\
0\leq l_{2}\leq k_{1}-k_{2}\\
0\leq l_{3}\leq k_{2}-k_{3}\end{subarray}}}\sum_{n=0}^{l_{3}}(-\lambda-2k_{3}+d+1)_{|\mathbf{l}|-n}\left(-\lambda-k_{1}-k_{2}+n+\frac{d}{4}+1\right)_{k_{2}-k_{3}-n}

\displaystyle\qquad{}\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\hskip 155.0pt\times(-k_{2}+k_{3})_{n}\left(-k_{1}+k_{3}-\frac{d}{4}\right)_{n}F_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}^{n}(z_{1},z_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{(-1)^{k_{2}-k_{3}}}{(\lambda)_{(k_{1}+k_{2},k_{2}+k_{3},2k_{3}),d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+d+1}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{2}}\right)^{k_{3}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{\lambda+k_{3}-d-1}

\displaystyle\qquad{}\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}^{3},\,0\leq l_{1}\\
0\leq l_{2}\leq k_{1}-k_{2}\\
0\leq l_{3}\leq k_{2}-k_{3}\end{subarray}}(-\lambda-2k_{3}+d+1)_{l_{1}+l_{2}}\left(-\lambda-k_{1}-k_{2}+l_{3}+\frac{d}{4}+1\right)_{k_{2}-k_{3}-l_{3}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\sum_{n=0}^{l_{3}}(-k_{2}+k_{3})_{n}\left(-k_{1}+k_{3}-\frac{d}{4}\right)_{n}\left(-\lambda-k_{1}-k_{2}+n+\frac{d}{4}+1\right)_{l_{3}-n}

\displaystyle\qquad{}\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times(-\lambda-2k_{3}+l_{1}+l_{2}+d+1)_{l_{3}-n}F_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}^{n}(z_{1},z_{2}).

Now we set

\displaystyle\tilde{F}_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}(\lambda;z_{1},z_{2}):={}

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{l_{3}}(-k_{2}+k_{3})_{n}\left(-k_{1}+k_{3}-\frac{d}{4}\right)_{n}\left(-\lambda-k_{1}-k_{2}+n+\frac{d}{4}+1\right)_{l_{3}-n}

\displaystyle\times(-\lambda-2k_{3}+l_{1}+l_{2}+d+1)_{l_{3}-n}F_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}^{n}(z_{1},z_{2})

\displaystyle\in\mathbb{C}[\lambda]_{\leq 2l_{3}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{2|\mathbf{l}|}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}\bigr)\otimes\mathcal{P}_{(k_{1}-l_{2}-l_{3},k_{2}-l_{1}-l_{3},k_{3}-l_{1}-l_{2})}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr),

(7.2)

where
\mathbb{C}[\lambda]_{\leq l}
denotes the space of polynomials in
\lambda
of degree at most
l
. Then since

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{2}}\right)^{k_{3}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{\lambda+k_{3}-d-1}\tilde{F}_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}(\lambda;z_{1},z_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad{}=(-1)^{k_{3}}\left(-\lambda-k_{1}-k_{3}+l_{2}+l_{3}+\frac{d}{2}+1\right)_{k_{3}}\left(-\lambda-k_{2}-k_{3}+l_{1}+l_{3}+\frac{3}{4}d+1\right)_{k_{3}}

\displaystyle\qquad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times(-\lambda-2k_{3}+l_{1}+l_{2}+d+1)_{k_{3}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{\lambda-d-1}\tilde{F}_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}(\lambda;z_{1},z_{2})

holds by (
2.6
), we have

\displaystyle\bigl\langle\Delta_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle=\frac{(-1)^{k_{2}}}{(\lambda)_{(k_{1}+k_{2},k_{2}+k_{3},2k_{3}),d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+d+1}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{\lambda-d-1}

\displaystyle\ \hphantom{=}{}\times\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}^{3},\,0\leq l_{1}\\
0\leq l_{2}\leq k_{1}-k_{2}\\
0\leq l_{3}\leq k_{2}-k_{3}\end{subarray}}(-\lambda-2k_{3}+d+1)_{l_{1}+l_{2}}\left(-\lambda-k_{1}-k_{2}+l_{3}+\frac{d}{4}+1\right)_{k_{2}-k_{3}-l_{3}}

\displaystyle\ \hphantom{=}{}\times\left(-\lambda-k_{1}-k_{3}+l_{2}+l_{3}+\frac{d}{2}+1\right)_{k_{3}}\left(-\lambda-k_{2}-k_{3}+l_{1}+l_{3}+\frac{3}{4}d+1\right)_{k_{3}}

\displaystyle\ \hphantom{=}{}\times(-\lambda-2k_{3}+l_{1}+l_{2}+d+1)_{k_{3}}\tilde{F}_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}(\lambda;z_{1},z_{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{(-1)^{k_{2}}(-\lambda-2k_{3}+d+1)_{k_{3}}}{(\lambda)_{(k_{1}+k_{2},k_{2}+k_{3},2k_{3}),d}}\,\biggl(1+\frac{\bigl(z_{2}|z_{1}^{\sharp}\bigr)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}}{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})}\biggr)^{-\lambda+d+1}

\displaystyle\ \hphantom{=}{}\times\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}^{3},\,0\leq l_{1}\\
0\leq l_{2}\leq k_{1}-k_{2}\\
0\leq l_{3}\leq k_{2}-k_{3}\end{subarray}}(-\lambda-k_{3}+d+1)_{l_{1}+l_{2}}\left(-\lambda-k_{1}-k_{2}+l_{3}+\frac{d}{4}+1\right)_{k_{2}-k_{3}-l_{3}}

\displaystyle\ \hphantom{=}{}\times\left(-\lambda-k_{1}-k_{3}+l_{2}+l_{3}+\frac{d}{2}+1\right)\!\!{\vphantom{\biggr)}}_{k_{3}}\!\!\left(-\lambda-k_{2}-k_{3}+l_{1}+l_{3}+\frac{3}{4}d+1\right)\!\!{\vphantom{\biggr)}}_{k_{3}}\!\!\tilde{F}_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}(\lambda;z_{1},z_{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{(-1)^{k_{2}-k_{3}}}{(\lambda)_{(k_{1}+k_{2},k_{2}+k_{3},k_{3}),d}}\,\biggl(1+\frac{\bigl(z_{2}|z_{1}^{\sharp}\bigr)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}}{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})}\biggr)^{-\lambda+d+1}

\displaystyle\ \hphantom{=}{}\times\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}^{3},\,0\leq l_{1}\\
0\leq l_{2}\leq k_{1}-k_{2}\\
0\leq l_{3}\leq k_{2}-k_{3}\end{subarray}}(-\lambda-k_{3}+d+1)_{l_{1}+l_{2}}\left(-\lambda-k_{1}-k_{2}+l_{3}+\frac{d}{4}+1\right)_{k_{2}-k_{3}-l_{3}}

(7.3)

\displaystyle\ \hphantom{=}{}\times\!\left(\!-\lambda-k_{1}-k_{3}+l_{2}+l_{3}+\frac{d}{2}+1\right)\!\!{\vphantom{\biggr)}}_{k_{3}}\!\!\left(\!-\lambda-k_{2}-k_{3}+l_{1}+l_{3}+\frac{3}{4}d+1\right)\!\!{\vphantom{\biggr)}}_{k_{3}}\!\tilde{F}_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}(\lambda;z_{1},z_{2}),

where we have used Lemma
7.1
(3) at the 2nd equality. Therefore,

(\lambda)_{(k_{1}+k_{2},k_{2}+k_{3},k_{3}),d}\,\bigl\langle\Delta_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x}

is holomorphic for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
, and hence by Corollary
2.4
, we have

\Delta_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})\in\bigoplus_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{3},\,m_{1}\leq k_{1}+k_{2}\\
m_{2}\leq k_{2}+k_{3},\,m_{3}\leq k_{3}\end{subarray}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}).

(7.4)

On the other hand, if
k_{1}=k_{2}=k_{3}
, since

\tilde{F}_{\underline{k_{1}}_{3},(l_{1},0,0)}(\lambda;z_{1},z_{2})=F_{\underline{k_{1}}_{3},(l_{1},0,0)}^{0}(z_{1},z_{2})=\frac{1}{l_{1}!}\,\biggl(-\frac{\bigl(z_{2}|z_{1}^{\sharp}\bigr)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}}{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})}\biggr)^{l_{1}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{k_{1}}

holds, we have

\displaystyle\bigl\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{k_{1}},{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle=\frac{\left(-\lambda-2k_{1}+\frac{d}{2}+1\right)_{k_{1}}}{(\lambda)_{(2k_{1},2k_{1},k_{1}),d}}\,\biggl(1+\frac{\bigl(z_{2}|z_{1}^{\sharp}\bigr)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}}{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})}\biggr)^{-\lambda+d+1}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\sum_{l_{1}=0}^{\infty}(-\lambda-k_{1}+d+1)_{l_{1}}\left(-\lambda-2k_{1}+l_{1}+\frac{3}{4}d+1\right)_{k_{1}}\frac{1}{l_{1}!}\,\biggl(-\frac{\bigl(z_{2}|z_{1}^{\sharp}\bigr)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}}{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})}\biggr)^{l_{1}}\!\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{k_{1}}

\displaystyle=\frac{(-1)^{k_{1}}\left(-\lambda-2k_{1}+\frac{3}{4}d+1\right)_{k_{1}}}{(\lambda)_{(2k_{1},k_{1},k_{1}),d}}\,\biggl(1+\frac{\bigl(z_{2}|z_{1}^{\sharp}\bigr)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}}{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})}\biggr)^{-\lambda+d+1}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times{}_{2}F_{1}\,\biggl(\begin{matrix}-\lambda-k_{1}+d+1,-\lambda-k_{1}+\frac{3}{4}d+1\\
-\lambda-2k_{1}+\frac{3}{4}d+1\end{matrix};-\frac{\bigl(z_{2}|z_{1}^{\sharp}\bigr)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}}{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})}\biggr){}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{k_{1}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\left(\lambda+k_{1}-\frac{3}{4}d\right)_{k_{1}}}{(\lambda)_{(2k_{1},k_{1},k_{1}),d}}{}_{2}F_{1}\,\biggl(\begin{matrix}-k_{1},-k_{1}-\frac{d}{4}\\
-\lambda-2k_{1}+\frac{3}{4}d+1\end{matrix};-\frac{\bigl(z_{2}|z_{1}^{\sharp}\bigr)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}}{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})}\biggr){}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{k_{1}}

as in
[
44
, Corollary 6.5]
, and hence

(\lambda)_{(2k_{1},k_{1},k_{1}),d}\bigl\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{k_{1}},{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x}

is holomorphic for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
, that is, we have

\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{k_{1}}\in\bigoplus_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{3},\,m_{1}\leq 2k_{1}\\
m_{2}\leq k_{1},\,m_{3}\leq k_{1}\end{subarray}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}).

Then for general
\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{3}
, by Lemma
2.1
, we also have

\Delta_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})\in\mathbb{C}\check{\Delta}_{(k_{1}-k_{3},k_{1}-k_{2},0)}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{2}}\right)\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{k_{1}}\subset\bigoplus_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{3},\,m_{1}\leq 2k_{1}\\
m_{2}\leq k_{1},\,m_{3}\leq k_{1}\end{subarray}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}),

(7.5)

and combining (
7.4
) and (
7.5
), we get

\Delta_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})\in\bigoplus_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{3},\,m_{1}\leq k_{1}+k_{2}\\
m_{2}\leq\min\{k_{1},k_{2}+k_{3}\},\,m_{3}\leq k_{3}\end{subarray}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}).

Therefore, by Corollary
2.4
,

(\lambda)_{(k_{1}+k_{2},\min\{k_{1},k_{2}+k_{3}\},k_{3}),d}\bigl\langle\Delta_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x}

is holomorphic for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
.
∎

7.3
Conjecture on weighted Bergman inner products

In the previous subsection we roughly computed the inner product
\bigl\langle\Delta_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x}

for
\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{3}
. By (
7.3
), we have

\displaystyle\bigl\langle\Delta_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\left(-\lambda-k_{1}-k_{2}+\frac{d}{4}+1\right)_{k_{2}-k_{3}}\left(-\lambda-k_{1}-k_{3}+\frac{d}{2}+1\right)_{k_{3}}\left(-\lambda-k_{2}-k_{3}+\frac{3}{4}d+1\right)_{k_{3}}}{(\lambda)_{(k_{1}+k_{2},k_{2}+k_{3},k_{3}),d}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times(-1)^{k_{2}-k_{3}}\,\biggl(1+\frac{\bigl(z_{2}|z_{1}^{\sharp}\bigr)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}}{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})}\biggr)^{-\lambda+d+1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}^{3},\,0\leq l_{1}\\
0\leq l_{2}\leq k_{1}-k_{2}\\
0\leq l_{3}\leq k_{2}-k_{3}\end{subarray}}\frac{(-\lambda-k_{3}+d+1)_{l_{1}+l_{2}}}{\left(-\lambda-k_{1}-k_{2}+\frac{d}{4}+1\right)_{l_{3}}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\frac{\left(-\lambda-k_{1}+\frac{d}{2}+1\right)_{l_{2}+l_{3}}}{\left(-\lambda-k_{1}-k_{3}+\frac{d}{2}+1\right)_{l_{2}+l_{3}}}\frac{\left(-\lambda-k_{2}+\frac{3}{4}d+1\right)_{l_{1}+l_{3}}}{\left(-\lambda-k_{2}-k_{3}+\frac{3}{4}d+1\right)_{l_{1}+l_{3}}}\tilde{F}_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}(\lambda;z_{1},z_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad{}=C_{1}^{d,d/2}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})\,\biggl(1+\frac{\bigl(z_{2}|z_{1}^{\sharp}\bigr)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}}{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})}\biggr)^{-\lambda+d+1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}^{3},\,0\leq l_{1}\\
0\leq l_{2}\leq k_{1}-k_{2}\\
0\leq l_{3}\leq k_{2}-k_{3}\end{subarray}}\frac{(-\lambda-k_{3}+d+1)_{l_{1}+l_{2}}}{\left(-\lambda-k_{1}-k_{2}+\frac{d}{4}+1\right)_{l_{3}}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\frac{\left(-\lambda-k_{1}+\frac{d}{2}+1\right)_{l_{2}+l_{3}}}{\left(-\lambda-k_{1}-k_{3}+\frac{d}{2}+1\right)_{l_{2}+l_{3}}}\frac{\left(-\lambda-k_{2}+\frac{3}{4}d+1\right)_{l_{1}+l_{3}}}{\left(-\lambda-k_{2}-k_{3}+\frac{3}{4}d+1\right)_{l_{1}+l_{3}}}\tilde{F}_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}(\lambda;z_{1},z_{2}),

where

\displaystyle C_{1}^{d,d/2}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})=\frac{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq 3}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(i+j-2)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{j}}}{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq 4}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{4}(i+j-3)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{j}}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\left(\lambda+k_{1}+k_{3}-\frac{d}{4}\right)_{k_{2}-k_{3}}\left(\lambda+k_{1}-\frac{d}{2}\right)_{k_{3}}\left(\lambda+k_{2}-\frac{3}{4}d\right)_{k_{3}}}{(\lambda)_{(k_{1}+k_{2},k_{2}+k_{3},k_{3}),d}}

(7.6)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\left(\lambda+k_{1}+k_{3}-\frac{d}{4}\right)_{k_{2}-k_{3}}\left(\lambda+\max\{k_{1},k_{2}+k_{3}\}-\frac{d}{2}\right)_{\min\{k_{3},k_{1}-k_{2}\}}\left(\lambda+k_{2}-\frac{3}{4}d\right)_{k_{3}}}{(\lambda)_{(k_{1}+k_{2},\min\{k_{1},k_{2}+k_{3}\},k_{3}),d}}

with
k_{4}:=0
. Now by the definition of
\tilde{F}_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}(\lambda;z_{1},z_{2})
(
7.1
) and (
7.2
), we can show that

\tilde{F}_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}(\lambda;z_{1},z_{2})\in\mathbb{C}[\lambda]_{\leq 2l_{3}}\otimes\bigl(\mathcal{P}_{2|\mathbf{l}|}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}\bigr)\otimes\mathcal{P}_{(k_{1}-l_{2}-l_{3},k_{2}-l_{1}-l_{3},k_{3}-l_{1}-l_{2})}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)\bigr)_{\mathbf{k}}^{M_{L_{2}}N_{L_{2}}^{\top}},

holds, where for
m\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
\mathbf{k},\mathbf{n}\in\mathbb{Z}_{+}^{3}
,
\mathcal{P}_{m}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}\bigr)
denotes the space of all homogeneous polynomials on
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}
of degree
m
, and

\displaystyle\bigl(\mathcal{P}_{m}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}\bigr)\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{n}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)\bigr)_{\mathbf{k}}^{M_{L_{2}}N_{L_{2}}^{\top}}

\displaystyle:=\left\{f(x_{1},x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{m}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}\bigr)\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{n}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)\ \middle|\ \begin{matrix}f(man.x_{1},man.x_{2})={\rm e}^{2(t_{1}k_{1}+t_{2}k_{2}+t_{3}k_{3})}f(x_{1},x_{2})\\
\bigl(m\in M_{L_{2}},\,a={\rm e}^{t_{1}h_{1}+t_{2}h_{2}+t_{3}h_{3}}\in A_{L_{2}},\,n\in N_{L_{2}}^{\top}\bigr)\end{matrix}\right\}.

For Cases 1 and 2,
\mathcal{P}_{m}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}\bigr)\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{n}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)
are given by

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{m}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}\bigr)\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{n}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{P}_{m}(\operatorname{Alt}(3,\mathbb{C}))\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{n}}(\operatorname{Sym}(3,\mathbb{C}))

\displaystyle\simeq V_{(m,m,0)}^{(3)\vee}\otimes V_{(2n_{1},2n_{2},2n_{3})}^{(3)\vee}

\displaystyle(\text{Case }1),

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{m}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}\bigr)\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{n}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)

\displaystyle=\bigoplus_{l=0}^{m}\bigl(\mathcal{P}_{l}(\operatorname{Alt}(3,\mathbb{C}))\boxtimes\mathcal{P}_{m-l}(\operatorname{Alt}(3,\mathbb{C}))\bigr)\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{n}}({\rm M}(3,\mathbb{C}))

\displaystyle\simeq\bigoplus_{l=0}^{m}\left(V_{(l,l,0)}^{(3)\vee}\boxtimes V_{(m-l,m-l,0)}^{(3)}\right)\otimes\left(V_{(n_{1},n_{2},n_{3})}^{(3)\vee}\boxtimes V_{(n_{1},n_{2},n_{3})}^{(3)}\right)

\displaystyle(\text{Case }2),

and these decompose multiplicity-freely under
K_{1}^{\mathbb{C}}
by the Pieri rule.
Hence the space
\bigl(\mathcal{P}_{m}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}\bigr)\allowbreak\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{n}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)\bigr)_{\mathbf{k}}^{M_{L_{2}}N_{L_{2}}^{\top}}
is at most 1-dimensional,
and thus there exist polynomials

f_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}(\lambda)\in\mathbb{C}[\lambda]_{\leq 2l_{3}},\qquad F_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}(z_{1},z_{2})\in\left(\mathcal{P}_{2|\mathbf{l}|}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}\bigr)\otimes\mathcal{P}_{(k_{1}-l_{2}-l_{3},k_{2}-l_{1}-l_{3},k_{3}-l_{1}-l_{2})}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)\right)^{M_{L_{2}}N_{L_{2}}^{\top}}_{\mathbf{k}}

such that

\tilde{F}_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}(\lambda;z_{1},z_{2})=f_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}(\lambda)F_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}(z_{1},z_{2})

holds. We expect that this also holds for Case 3. In addition, we conjecture the following.

Conjecture 7.2
.

For each
\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{3}
,
\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}^{3}
with
l_{1}\geq 0
,
0\leq l_{2}\leq k_{1}-k_{2}
,
0\leq l_{3}\leq k_{2}-k_{3}
, there exists a polynomial

F_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}(z_{1},z_{2})\in\left(\mathcal{P}_{2|\mathbf{l}|}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}\bigr)\otimes\mathcal{P}_{(k_{1}-l_{2}-l_{3},k_{2}-l_{1}-l_{3},k_{3}-l_{1}-l_{2})}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)\right)^{M_{L_{2}}N_{L_{2}}^{\top}}_{\mathbf{k}}

such that for
\operatorname{Re}\lambda>p-1
and for
z=z_{1}+z_{2}\in\Omega\subset\mathfrak{n}^{+}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}
, we have

\displaystyle\bigl\langle\Delta_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=C_{1}^{d,d/2}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})\,\biggl(1+\frac{\bigl(z_{2}|z_{1}^{\sharp}\bigr)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}}{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})}\biggr)^{-\lambda+d+1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}^{3},\,0\leq l_{1}\\
0\leq l_{2}\leq k_{1}-k_{2}\\
0\leq l_{3}\leq k_{2}-k_{3}\end{subarray}}\frac{(-\lambda-k_{3}+d+1)_{l_{1}+l_{2}}}{\left(-\lambda-k_{1}-k_{2}+\frac{d}{4}+1\right)_{l_{3}}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\frac{\left(-\lambda-k_{2}+\frac{3}{4}d+1\right)_{l_{1}+l_{3}}}{\left(-\lambda-k_{1}-k_{3}+\frac{d}{2}+1\right)_{l_{2}}}\frac{\left(-\lambda-k_{1}+\frac{d}{2}+1\right)_{l_{2}+l_{3}}}{\left(-\lambda-k_{2}-k_{3}+\frac{3}{4}d+1\right)_{l_{1}}}F_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}(z_{1},z_{2})

(7.7)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=C_{1}^{d,d/2}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}^{3},\,0\leq l_{1}\\
0\leq l_{2}\leq k_{1}-k_{2}\\
0\leq l_{3}\leq k_{2}-k_{3}\end{subarray}}\frac{(-k_{3})_{l_{1}+l_{2}}}{\left(-\lambda-k_{1}-k_{2}+\frac{d}{4}+1\right)_{l_{3}}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\frac{\left(-k_{2}-\frac{d}{4}\right)_{l_{1}+l_{3}}}{\left(-\lambda-k_{1}-k_{3}+\frac{d}{2}+1\right)_{l_{2}}}\frac{\left(-k_{1}-\frac{d}{2}\right)_{l_{2}+l_{3}}}{\left(-\lambda-k_{2}-k_{3}+\frac{3}{4}d+1\right)_{l_{1}}}F_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}(z_{1},z_{2}),

(7.8)

where
C_{1}^{d,d/2}(\lambda,\mathbf{k})
is as in (
7.6
).

By
[
44
, Theorem 6.3 (1)]
, both (
7.7
) and (
7.8
) hold if
k_{2}=k_{3}
. In general, (
7.7
) is equivalent to

f_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}(\lambda)=\left(-\lambda-k_{1}-k_{3}+l_{2}+\frac{d}{2}+1\right)_{l_{3}}\left(-\lambda-k_{2}-k_{3}+l_{1}+\frac{3}{4}d+1\right)_{l_{3}}.

Also, if (
7.8
) is true, then the symmetry breaking operator

\mathcal{F}_{\lambda,\mathbf{k}}^{\downarrow}\colon\ \mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D)|_{\widetilde{G}_{1}}\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\varepsilon_{1}\lambda}(D_{1},V_{\mathbf{k}})

with
V_{\mathbf{k}}:=\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\bigr)
given in (
2.18
) and (
2.19
), where

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{\varepsilon_{1}\lambda}(D_{1},V_{\mathbf{k}})

\displaystyle\simeq\begin{cases}\mathcal{H}_{2\lambda}\bigl(D_{{\rm SO}^{*}(6)},V_{2\mathbf{k}}^{(3)\vee}\bigr)&(\text{Case }1),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}\bigl(D_{{\rm SO}^{*}(6)},V_{\mathbf{k}}^{(3)\vee}\bigr)\mathbin{\hat{\boxtimes}}\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}\bigl(D_{{\rm SO}^{*}(6)},V_{\mathbf{k}}^{(3)\vee}\bigr)&(\text{Case }2),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}\bigl(D_{{\rm SU}(2,6)},\mathbb{C}\boxtimes V_{(k_{1}+k_{2},k_{1}+k_{3},k_{2}+k_{3})^{2}}^{(6)}\bigr)&(\text{Case }3),\end{cases}

is given by the differential operator of the form

\displaystyle\bigl(\mathcal{F}_{\lambda,\mathbf{k}}^{\downarrow}f\bigr)(x_{1})

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}^{3},\,0\leq l_{1}\\
0\leq l_{2}\leq k_{1}-k_{2}\\
0\leq l_{3}\leq k_{2}-k_{3}\end{subarray}}\frac{(-k_{3})_{l_{1}+l_{2}}}{\left(-\lambda-k_{1}-k_{2}+\frac{d}{4}+1\right)_{l_{3}}}\frac{\left(-k_{2}-\frac{d}{4}\right)_{l_{1}+l_{3}}}{\left(-\lambda-k_{1}-k_{3}+\frac{d}{2}+1\right)_{l_{2}}}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\frac{\left(-k_{1}-\frac{d}{2}\right)_{l_{2}+l_{3}}}{\left(-\lambda-k_{2}-k_{3}+\frac{3}{4}d+1\right)_{l_{1}}}\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\right)f(x)\biggr|_{x_{2}=0},

where
\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}(z)=\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}(z_{1},z_{2})\in\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{-},V_{\mathbf{k}})^{K_{1}}
satisfies

\bigl(v,\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}(\overline{z_{1}},\overline{z_{2}})\bigr)_{V_{\mathbf{k}}}=F_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}}(z_{1},z_{2})

for a suitable fixed lowest weight vector
v\in V_{\mathbf{k}}
.
By the analytic continuation, this gives the symmetry breaking operator between the spaces of all holomorphic functions even for
\operatorname{Re}\lambda\leq p-1
except for the poles.
Moreover, the equality (
7.7
)
=
(
7.8
) at
\lambda=\frac{n}{r}-a
,
a=1,2,\dots,k_{3}
corresponds to Theorem
6.6

or Corollary
6.8
(4),

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{\frac{n}{r}-a,\mathbf{k}}^{\downarrow}=c\mathcal{F}_{\frac{n}{r}+a,\mathbf{k}-\underline{a}_{3}}^{\downarrow}\circ\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\right)^{a}\colon

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\frac{n}{r}-a}(D)|_{\widetilde{G}_{1}}\longrightarrow\mathcal{O}_{\varepsilon_{1}\left(\frac{n}{r}-a\right)}(D_{1},V_{\mathbf{k}})\simeq\mathcal{O}_{\varepsilon_{1}\left(\frac{n}{r}+a\right)}(D_{1},V_{\mathbf{k}-\underline{a}_{3}})

for some
c\in\mathbb{C}
.

8
Tensor product case

In this section we consider the direct sum
\mathfrak{p}^{+}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+}
with
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
simple, and the involution
\sigma\colon(x,y)\mapsto(y,x)
,
so that
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}=\{(x,x)\mid x\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}\}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}=\{(x,-x)\mid x\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}\}
. Then the corresponding symmetric pair is of the form
(G\times G,\Delta(G))
.
Let
\dim\mathfrak{p}^{+}=:n
,
\operatorname{rank}\mathfrak{p}^{+}=:r
, and let
d
be the number defined in (
2.4
).
This section contains some overlap with
[
43
, Section 5.2]
. See also
[
51
]
.

8.1
Results on weighted Bergman inner products

We consider the outer tensor product
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D)\mathbin{\hat{\boxtimes}}\mathcal{H}_{\mu}(D)
of holomorphic discrete series representations of scalar type,
and let
\langle\cdot,\cdot\rangle_{\lambda\otimes\mu}
denote its inner product. For
f\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
, we want to compute the inner product

\bigl\langle f(x-y),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}+(y|\overline{w})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda\otimes\mu,(x,y)}
, with the variable of integration
(x,y)
.
To do this, for
f\in\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
,
\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}
, define
\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(x,y)\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{n}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
by

f(x+y)=\sum_{\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}}\sum_{\mathbf{n}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(x,y)\in\bigoplus_{\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}}\bigoplus_{\mathbf{n}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{n}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}).

If
f\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
, then
\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(x,y)\neq 0
holds only if
m_{j}\leq k_{j}
,
n_{j}\leq k_{j}
hold for all
j=1,\dots,r
,
since the map
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})\to\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{n}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
,
f\mapsto\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}
is
K
-equivariant,
and the restricted weights of
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
,
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{n}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
sit in
\left(\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{Z}_{\leq 0}\right)^{r}

under a suitable identification
(\mathfrak{a}_{\mathfrak{l}})^{\vee}\simeq\mathbb{R}^{r}
, where
\mathfrak{a}_{\mathfrak{l}}\subset\mathfrak{k}^{\mathbb{C}}
is as in Section
2.3
.
Moreover, we have
\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{k},\underline{0}_{r}}(x,y)=f(x)
,
\tilde{f}_{\underline{0}_{r},\mathbf{k}}(x,y)=f(y)
.
Therefore, by Corollary
2.4
, we easily get the following. Here, for
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
,
\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}\in(\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0})^{r}
,
let
(\lambda+\mathbf{m})_{\mathbf{n},d}:=\prod_{j=1}^{r}\left(\lambda+m_{j}-\frac{d}{2}(j-1)\right)_{n_{j}}
.

Theorem 8.1
.

Let
\operatorname{Re}\lambda,\operatorname{Re}\mu>p-1
,
\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}
, and let
f\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
. Then we have

\bigl\langle f(x-y),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}+(y|\overline{w})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda\otimes\mu,(x,y)}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}\\
m_{j}\leq k_{j}\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{n}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}\\
n_{j}\leq k_{j}\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{\mathbf{m},d}(\mu)_{\mathbf{n},d}}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(z,-w).

Especially, as a function of
(\lambda,\mu)
,

\displaystyle(\lambda)_{\mathbf{k},d}(\mu)_{\mathbf{k},d}\bigl\langle f(x-y),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}+(y|\overline{w})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda\otimes\mu,(x,y)}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}\\
m_{j}\leq k_{j}\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{n}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}\\
n_{j}\leq k_{j}\end{subarray}}(\lambda+\mathbf{m})_{\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{m},d}(\mu+\mathbf{n})_{\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{n},d}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(z,-w)

(8.1)

is holomorphically continued for all
\mathbb{C}^{2}
.

If
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
is of tube type, then for particular
\lambda,\mu\in\mathbb{C}
, the analytic continuation of (
8.1
) is factorized as follows.

Theorem 8.2
.

Suppose
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
is of tube type, and consider the meromorphic continuation of
\langle\cdot,\cdot\rangle_{\lambda\otimes\mu}
for
\lambda,\mu\in\mathbb{C}
.
Then for
\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}
,
a=1,2,\dots,k_{r}
and for
f\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
, we have

\displaystyle(\lambda)_{\mathbf{k},d}\bigl\langle f(x-y),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}+(y|\overline{w})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda\otimes\mu,(x,y)}\big|_{\lambda=\frac{n}{r}-a}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\left(\frac{n}{r}+a\right)_{\mathbf{k}-\underline{a}_{r},d}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{a}\bigl\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x-y)^{-a}f(x-y),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}+(y|\overline{w})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\left(\frac{n}{r}+a\right)\otimes\mu,(x,y)},

\displaystyle(\mu)_{\mathbf{k},d}\bigl\langle f(x-y),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}+(y|\overline{w})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda\otimes\mu,(x,y)}\big|_{\mu=\frac{n}{r}-a}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\left(\frac{n}{r}+a\right)_{\mathbf{k}-\underline{a}_{r},d}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(-w)^{a}\bigl\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x-y)^{-a}f(x-y),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}+(y|\overline{w})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda\otimes\left(\frac{n}{r}+a\right),(x,y)}.

Proof.

Put
g(x):=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x)^{-a}f(x)\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}-\underline{a}_{r}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})=\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}-(\smash{\overbrace{\scriptstyle a,\dots,a}^{r}})}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
. Then by
[
44
, Proposition 5.5]
, we have

\displaystyle\left(\frac{n}{r}-a+\mathbf{k}\right)_{\underline{a}_{r},d}\tilde{g}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(x,y)

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{n}{r}+\mathbf{m}\right)_{\underline{a}_{r},d}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x)^{-a}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m}+\underline{a}_{r},\mathbf{n}}(x,y)

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{n}{r}+\mathbf{n}\right)_{\underline{a}_{r},d}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(y)^{-a}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}+\underline{a}_{r}}(x,y).

Using this, we get

\displaystyle(\lambda)_{\mathbf{k},d}\bigl\langle f(x-y),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}+(y|\overline{w})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda\otimes\mu,(x,y)}\big|_{\lambda=\frac{n}{r}-a}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}\\
m_{j}\leq k_{j}\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{n}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}\\
n_{j}\leq k_{j}\end{subarray}}\frac{(\lambda+\mathbf{m})_{\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{m},d}}{(\mu)_{\mathbf{n},d}}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(z,-w)\biggr|_{\lambda=\frac{n}{r}-a}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}\\
a\leq m_{j}\leq k_{j}\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{n}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}\\
n_{j}\leq k_{j}\end{subarray}}\frac{\left(\frac{n}{r}-a+\mathbf{m}\right)_{\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{m},d}}{(\mu)_{\mathbf{n},d}}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(z,-w)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}\\
m_{j}\leq k_{j}-a\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{n}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}\\
n_{j}\leq k_{j}\end{subarray}}\frac{\left(\frac{n}{r}+\mathbf{m}\right)_{\mathbf{k}-\underline{a}_{r}-\mathbf{m},d}}{(\mu)_{\mathbf{n},d}}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m}+\underline{a}_{r},\mathbf{n}}(z,-w)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}\\
m_{j}\leq k_{j}-a\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{n}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}\\
n_{j}\leq k_{j}-a\end{subarray}}\frac{\left(\frac{n}{r}+\mathbf{m}\right)_{\mathbf{k}-\underline{a}_{r}-\mathbf{m},d}}{(\mu)_{\mathbf{n},d}}\frac{\left(\frac{n}{r}-a+\mathbf{k}\right)_{\underline{a}_{r},d}}{\left(\frac{n}{r}+\mathbf{m}\right)_{\underline{a}_{r},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{a}\tilde{g}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(z,-w)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}\\
m_{j}\leq k_{j}-a\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{n}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}\\
n_{j}\leq k_{j}-a\end{subarray}}\frac{\left(\frac{n}{r}+a+\mathbf{m}\right)_{\mathbf{k}-\underline{a}_{r}-\mathbf{m},d}}{(\mu)_{\mathbf{n},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{a}\tilde{g}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(z,-w)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\left(\frac{n}{r}+a\right)_{\mathbf{k}-\underline{a}_{r},d}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{a}\bigl\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x-y)^{-a}f(x-y),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}+(y|\overline{w})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\left(\frac{n}{r}+a\right)\otimes\mu,(x,y)}.

The 2nd formula is also proved similarly. We can also prove this by an argument similar to Proposition
2.8
(2) and Theorem
3.5

by using Theorem
3.4
.
∎

We note that (
8.1
) may vanish for some
(\lambda,\mu)\in\mathbb{C}^{2}
in general. For such
(\lambda,\mu)\in\mathbb{C}^{2}
the following holds. For rank 1 case see also
[
33
, Sections 8, 9]
.

Proposition 8.3
.

Suppose
f\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
is non-zero, and we consider the analytic continuation of (
8.1
).
If (
8.1
) vanishes at
(\lambda,\mu)=(\lambda_{0},\mu_{0})\in\mathbb{C}^{2}
,
then there exist
l_{1},l_{2}\in\mathbb{Z}_{>0}
such that

\displaystyle\lim_{\lambda\to\lambda_{0}}\frac{1}{(\lambda-\lambda_{0})^{l_{1}}}\Big((\lambda)_{\mathbf{k},d}(\mu)_{\mathbf{k},d}\bigl\langle f(x-y),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}+(y|\overline{w})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda\otimes\mu,(x,y)}\big|_{\mu=\mu_{0}}\Big),

\displaystyle\lim_{\mu\to\mu_{0}}\frac{1}{(\mu-\mu_{0})^{l_{2}}}\Big((\lambda)_{\mathbf{k},d}(\mu)_{\mathbf{k},d}\bigl\langle f(x-y),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}+(y|\overline{w})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda\otimes\mu,(x,y)}\big|_{\lambda=\lambda_{0}}\Big)

converge to non-zero polynomials. These are linearly independent in
\mathcal{P}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{+}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+}\bigr)
.

Proof.

Let
\Lambda(\mathbf{k}):=\big\{(\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n})\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}\times\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}\mid\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(x,y)\neq 0\big\}
. Then since

\displaystyle(\lambda)_{\mathbf{k},d}(\mu)_{\mathbf{k},d}\bigl\langle f(x-y),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}+(y|\overline{w})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda\otimes\mu,(x,y)}\big|_{\mu=\mu_{0}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n})\in\Lambda(\mathbf{k})\\
(\mu_{0}+\mathbf{n})_{\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{n},d}\neq 0\end{subarray}}(\lambda+\mathbf{m})_{\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{m},d}(\mu_{0}+\mathbf{n})_{\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{n},d}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(x,-y)

vanishes at
\lambda=\lambda_{0}
, this is divisible by
(\lambda-\lambda_{0})^{l_{1}}
for some
l_{1}\in\mathbb{Z}_{>0}
. Let
l_{1}
be the maximum integer satisfying this. Then

\displaystyle\lim_{\lambda\to\lambda_{0}}\frac{1}{(\lambda-\lambda_{0})^{l_{1}}}\Big((\lambda)_{\mathbf{k},d}(\mu)_{\mathbf{k},d}\bigl\langle f(x-y),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}+(y|\overline{w})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda\otimes\mu,(x,y)}\big|_{\mu=\mu_{0}}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\lim_{\lambda\to\lambda_{0}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n})\in\Lambda(\mathbf{k})\\
(\mu_{0}+\mathbf{n})_{\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{n},d}\neq 0\end{subarray}}\frac{(\lambda+\mathbf{m})_{\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{m},d}}{(\lambda-\lambda_{0})^{l_{1}}}(\mu_{0}+\mathbf{n})_{\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{n},d}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(x,-y)

is non-zero. Similarly,

\displaystyle\lim_{\mu\to\mu_{0}}\frac{1}{(\mu-\mu_{0})^{l_{2}}}\Big((\lambda)_{\mathbf{k},d}(\mu)_{\mathbf{k},d}\bigl\langle f(x-y),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}+(y|\overline{w})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda\otimes\mu,(x,y)}\big|_{\lambda=\lambda_{0}}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\lim_{\mu\to\mu_{0}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n})\in\Lambda(\mathbf{k})\\
(\lambda_{0}+\mathbf{m})_{\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{m},d}\neq 0\end{subarray}}(\lambda_{0}+\mathbf{m})_{\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{m},d}\frac{(\mu+\mathbf{n})_{\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{n},d}}{(\mu-\mu_{0})^{l_{2}}}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(x,-y)

is non-zero for some
l_{2}\in\mathbb{Z}_{>0}
. Now since
\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(x,-y)
are linearly independent for all
(\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n})\in\Lambda(\mathbf{k})
, and since

\displaystyle\{(\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n})\in\Lambda(\mathbf{k})\mid(\mu_{0}+\mathbf{n})_{\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{n},d}\neq 0\}\cap\{(\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n})\in\Lambda(\mathbf{k})\mid(\lambda_{0}+\mathbf{m})_{\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{m},d}\neq 0\}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\{(\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n})\in\Lambda(\mathbf{k})\mid(\lambda_{0}+\mathbf{m})_{\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{m},d}(\mu_{0}+\mathbf{n})_{\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{n},d}\neq 0\}=\varnothing

holds, the above two limits are linearly independent.
∎

Next we compute the value of the inner product at
w=-z
.

Theorem 8.4
.

Let
\operatorname{Re}\lambda,\operatorname{Re}\mu>p-1
,
\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}
, and let
f\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
. Then we have

\bigl\langle f(x-y),{\rm e}^{(x-y|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda\otimes\mu,(x,y)}=\frac{\tilde{C}^{d}(\lambda,\mu,\mathbf{k})}{(\lambda)_{\mathbf{k},d}(\mu)_{\mathbf{k},d}}f(z),

where

\displaystyle\tilde{C}^{d}(\lambda,\mu,\mathbf{k})

\displaystyle=\frac{\prod_{1\leq i\leq j\leq r}\left(\lambda+\mu-1-\frac{d}{2}(i+j-2)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{j}}}{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r+1}\left(\lambda+\mu-1-\frac{d}{2}(i+j-3)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{j}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\prod_{a=1}^{2r-1}\prod_{i=\max\{1,a+1-r\}}^{\lceil a/2\rceil}\left(\lambda+\mu-1-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{a+1-i}}}{\prod_{a=1}^{2r-1}\prod_{i=\max\{1,a+1-r\}}^{\lceil a/2\rceil}\left(\lambda+\mu-1-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{a+2-i}}}.

(8.2)

Here we put
k_{r+1}:=0
.

Proof.

The 2nd equality of (
8.2
) is easy. For the 1st equality,
by the last paragraph of Section
3.1
, we may assume
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
is of tube type, and by the
K
-equivariance, may assume
f(x)=\Delta^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}_{\mathbf{k}}(x)
.
We prove the theorem by induction on
r
. When
\mathbf{k}=(0,\dots,0)
(“
r=0
case”), this is clear. Next we assume the theorem for
r-1
, and prove for
r
.
Since we have defined
k_{r+1}=0
, by (
3.6
), we have

\displaystyle\bigl\langle\Delta^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}_{\mathbf{k}}(x-y),{\rm e}^{(x-y|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda\otimes\mu,(x,y)}

\displaystyle=\frac{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\hskip-1.0pt(z)^{-\lambda-\mu+\frac{2n}{r}}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k_{r}}_{r},d}(\mu)_{\underline{k_{r}}_{r},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\!\!\left(\hskip-1.0pt\frac{\partial}{\partial z}\hskip-1.0pt\right)\!\!{\vphantom{\biggr|}}^{k_{r}}\!\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\hskip-1.0pt(z)^{\lambda+\mu+2k_{r}-\frac{2n}{r}}\hskip-1.0pt\left\langle\hskip-1.0pt\Delta^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}_{\mathbf{k}-\underline{k_{r}}_{r}}\!(x\hskip-1.0pt-\hskip-1.0pty),{\rm e}^{(x-y|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\hskip-1.0pt\right\rangle_{\begin{subarray}{c}(\lambda+k_{r})\otimes\hskip 14.0pt\\
(\mu+k_{r}),(x,y)\end{subarray}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda-\mu+d(r-1)+2}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k_{r}}_{r},d}(\mu)_{\underline{k_{r}}_{r},d}(\lambda+k_{r})_{\mathbf{k}-\underline{k_{r}}_{r},d}(\mu+k_{r})_{\mathbf{k}-\underline{k_{r}},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial z}\right)^{k_{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{\lambda+\mu+2k_{r}-d(r-1)-2}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\frac{\prod_{1\leq i\leq j\leq r-1}\left((\lambda+k_{r})+(\mu+k_{r})-1-\frac{d}{2}(i+j-2)\right)_{(k_{i}-k_{r})+(k_{j}-k_{r})}}{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r}\left((\lambda+k_{r})+(\mu+k_{r})-1-\frac{d}{2}(i+j-3)\right)_{(k_{i}-k_{r})+(k_{j}-k_{r})}}\Delta^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}_{\mathbf{k}-\underline{k_{r}}_{r}}(z)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{\mathbf{k},d}(\mu)_{\mathbf{l},d}}\frac{\prod_{1\leq i\leq j\leq r-1}\left(\lambda+\mu+2k_{r}-1-\frac{d}{2}(i+j-2)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{j}-2k_{r}}}{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r}\left(\lambda+\mu+2k_{r}-1-\frac{d}{2}(i+j-3)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{j}-2k_{r}}}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\prod_{i=1}^{r}\left(\lambda+\mu+k_{i}-1-d(r-1)+\frac{d}{2}(r-i)\right)_{k_{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{k_{r}}\Delta^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}_{\mathbf{k}-\underline{k_{r}}_{r}}(z)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{\mathbf{k},d}(\mu)_{\mathbf{l},d}}\frac{\prod_{1\leq i\leq j\leq r-1}\left(\lambda+\mu-1-\frac{d}{2}(i+j-2)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{j}}}{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r}\left(\lambda+\mu-1-\frac{d}{2}(i+j-3)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{j}}}\Delta^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}_{\mathbf{k}}(z)

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\frac{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r}\left(\lambda+\mu-1-\frac{d}{2}(i+j-3)\right)_{2k_{r}}}{\prod_{1\leq i\leq j\leq r-1}\left(\lambda+\mu-1-\frac{d}{2}(i+j-2)\right)_{2k_{r}}}\frac{\prod_{i=1}^{r}\left(\lambda+\mu-1-\frac{d}{2}(i+r-2)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{r}}}{\prod_{i=1}^{r}\left(\lambda+\mu-1-\frac{d}{2}(i+(r+1)-3)\right)_{k_{i}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{\mathbf{k},d}(\mu)_{\mathbf{l},d}}\frac{\prod_{1\leq i\leq j\leq r}\left(\lambda+\mu-1-\frac{d}{2}(i+j-2)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{j}}}{\prod_{1\leq i<j\leq r+1}\left(\lambda+\mu-1-\frac{d}{2}(i+j-3)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{j}}}\Delta^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}_{\mathbf{k}}(z),

where we have used the induction hypothesis and Proposition
3.1
at the 2nd equality, and (
2.6
) at the 3rd equality.
Therefore, the theorem holds for all
r
.
∎

By Theorem
8.4
, we get the following.

Corollary 8.5
.

Let
\operatorname{Re}\lambda,\operatorname{Re}\mu>p-1
,
\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}
, and let
f\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
. Then we have

\|f(x-y)\|_{\lambda\otimes\mu,(x,y)}^{2}=\frac{\tilde{C}^{d}(\lambda,\mu,\mathbf{k})}{(\lambda)_{\mathbf{k},d}(\mu)_{\mathbf{k},d}}\|f\|_{F,\mathfrak{p}^{+}}^{2},

where
\tilde{C}^{d}(\lambda,\mu,\mathbf{k})
is as in (
8.2
).

Proof.

As in the proof of Proposition
2.6
(1), we have

\displaystyle\|f(x-y)\|_{\lambda\otimes\mu,(x,y)}^{2}

\displaystyle=\Big\langle\bigl\langle f(x-y),{\rm e}^{\frac{1}{2}(x+y|\overline{z+w})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}+\frac{1}{2}(x-y|\overline{z-w})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda\otimes\mu,(x,y)},f(z-w)\Big\rangle_{F,(z,w)}

\displaystyle=\Big\langle\bigl\langle f(x-y),{\rm e}^{\frac{1}{2}(x-y|\overline{z-w})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda\otimes\mu,(x,y)},f(z-w)\Big\rangle_{F,(z,w)}

\displaystyle=\frac{\tilde{C}^{d}(\lambda,\mu,\mathbf{k})}{(\lambda)_{\mathbf{k},d}(\mu)_{\mathbf{k},d}}\left\langle f\left(\frac{z-w}{2}\right),f(z-w)\right\rangle_{F,(z,w)}

\displaystyle=\frac{\tilde{C}^{d}(\lambda,\mu,\mathbf{k})}{(\lambda)_{\mathbf{k},d}(\mu)_{\mathbf{k},d}}\|f\|_{F,\mathfrak{p}^{+}}.

∎

Next we give a rough estimate of zeroes of
\tilde{C}^{d}(\lambda,\mu,\mathbf{k})
.

Proposition 8.6
.

For
\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}
, let
\tilde{C}^{d}(\lambda,\mu,\mathbf{k})
be as in (
8.2
).
For
1\leq m_{1}\leq m_{2}\leq r
, if
k_{1}=\cdots=k_{m_{1}}
and
k_{m_{2}+1}=0
, then

\displaystyle\big\{(\lambda,\mu)\in\mathbb{C}^{2}\mid\tilde{C}^{d}(\lambda,\mu,\mathbf{k})=0\big\}

\displaystyle\qquad{}\subset\begin{cases}\left\{(\lambda,\mu)\in\mathbb{C}^{2}\ \middle|\ \frac{d}{2}(m_{1}-1)-2k_{1}+2\leq\lambda+\mu\leq d(m_{2}-1)-k_{m_{2}}+1\right\},&k_{1}\geq 1,\\
\varnothing,&k_{1}=0.\end{cases}

Especially, for
m=0,1,\dots,r-1
, if
k_{m+1}=0
, then
\tilde{C}^{d}(\lambda,\mu,\mathbf{k})\neq 0
for
\lambda,\mu\geq\frac{d}{2}m
.

Proof.

By direct computation, we have

\displaystyle\tilde{C}^{d}(\lambda,\mu,\mathbf{k})

\displaystyle=\tilde{C}^{d}(\lambda,\mu,(k_{1},\dots,k_{m_{2}}))

\displaystyle=\prod_{a=m_{1}}^{2m_{2}-1}\prod_{i=\max\{1,a+1-m_{2}\}}^{\lceil a/2\rceil}\frac{\left(\lambda+\mu-1-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{a+1-i}}}{\left(\lambda+\mu-1-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{a+2-i}}}.

For
1\leq a\leq 2m_{2}-1
, let

\displaystyle\phi(\mathbf{k})_{a}:=\min\{k_{i}+k_{j}\mid 1\leq i<j\leq m_{2}+1,\,i+j=a+2\},

\displaystyle\psi(\mathbf{k})_{a}:=\max\{k_{i}+k_{j}\mid 1\leq i\leq j\leq m_{2},\,i+j=a+1\}.

Then we have

\displaystyle\big\{(\lambda,\mu)\in\mathbb{C}^{2}\mid\tilde{C}^{d}(\lambda,\mu,\mathbf{k})=0\big\}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\bigcup_{a=m_{1}}^{2m_{2}-1}\left\{(\lambda,\mu)\in\mathbb{C}^{2}\,\middle|\,\prod_{i=\max\{1,a+1-m_{2}\}}^{\lceil a/2\rceil}\frac{\left(\lambda+\mu-1-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{a+1-i}}}{\left(\lambda+\mu-1-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{k_{i}+k_{a+2-i}}}=0\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad{}\subset\bigcup_{a=m_{1}}^{2m_{2}-1}\left\{(\lambda,\mu)\in\mathbb{C}^{2}\,\middle|\,\frac{\left(\lambda+\mu-1-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{\psi(\mathbf{k})_{a}}}{\left(\lambda+\mu-1-\frac{d}{2}(a-1)\right)_{\phi(\mathbf{k})_{a}}}=0\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\bigcup_{a=m_{1}}^{2m_{2}-1}\left\{(\lambda,\mu)\in\mathbb{C}^{2}\,\middle|\,\lambda+\mu=\frac{d}{2}(a-1)-j+1,\,j\in\mathbb{Z},\,\phi(\mathbf{k})_{a}\leq j\leq\psi(\mathbf{k})_{a}-1\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad{}\subset\left\{(\lambda,\mu)\in\mathbb{C}^{2}\,\middle|\,\frac{d}{2}(m_{1}-1)-2k_{1}+2\leq\lambda+\mu\leq\frac{d}{2}(2m_{2}-2)-k_{m_{2}}+1\right\}.

When
k_{1}=0
, we have
\tilde{C}^{d}(\lambda,\mu,\mathbf{k})=1
, and this is non-zero everywhere.
The last statement is clear.
∎

8.2
Results on restriction of
\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D)\mathbin{\hat{\boxtimes}}\mathcal{H}_{\mu}(D)}
to subgroups

Next we consider the decomposition of the tensor product representation
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D)\mathbin{\hat{\boxtimes}}\mathcal{H}_{\mu}(D)|_{\Delta(\widetilde{G})}\allowbreak=\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D)\mathbin{\hat{\otimes}}\mathcal{H}_{\mu}(D)

under the diagonal subgroup
\Delta(\widetilde{G})\subset\widetilde{G}\times\widetilde{G}
.
By Theorem
2.5
, we have

\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D)\mathbin{\hat{\otimes}}\mathcal{H}_{\mu}(D)\simeq\sideset{}{{}^{\oplus}}{\sum}_{\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}}\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+\mu}\bigl(D,\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})\bigr).

For each
\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}
, let
V_{\mathbf{k}}
be the abstract
K
-module isomorphic to
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
,
let
\|\cdot\|_{\nu,\mathbf{k}}
be the
\widetilde{G}
-invariant norm on
\mathcal{H}_{\nu}(D,V_{\mathbf{k}})
normalized such that
\|v\|_{\nu,\mathbf{k}}=|v|_{V_{\mathbf{k}}}
holds for all constant functions

v\in V_{\mathbf{k}}
, and for
\lambda,\mu>p-1
, we consider the symmetry breaking operator

\displaystyle\hat{\mathcal{F}}_{\lambda,\mu,\mathbf{k}}^{\downarrow}\colon\ \mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D)\mathbin{\hat{\otimes}}\mathcal{H}_{\mu}(D)\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+\mu}(D,V_{\mathbf{k}}),

\displaystyle\bigl(\hat{\mathcal{F}}_{\lambda,\mu,\mathbf{k}}^{\downarrow}f\bigr)(x):=\hat{F}_{\lambda,\mu,\mathbf{k}}\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial x},\frac{\partial}{\partial y}\right)f(x,y)\biggr|_{y=x},

\displaystyle\hat{F}_{\lambda,\mu,\mathbf{k}}(z,w):=(\lambda)_{\mathbf{k},d}(\mu)_{\mathbf{k},d}\bigl\langle{\rm e}^{(x|z)_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}+(y|w)_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}},\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{k}}(x-y)\bigr\rangle_{\lambda\otimes\mu,(x,y)}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{-}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{-},V_{\mathbf{k}}),

where
\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{k}}(x)\in\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{+},\overline{V_{\mathbf{k}}})^{K}
is normalized such that

\big|\langle f(x),\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{k}}(x)\rangle_{F,\mathfrak{p}^{+}}\big|_{V_{\mathbf{k}}}^{2}=\|f\|_{F,\mathfrak{p}^{+}}^{2},\qquad f(x)\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}).

This operator coincides with the one given in
[
51
]
up to constant multiple.
In our normalization,
\hat{\mathcal{F}}_{\lambda,\mu,\mathbf{k}}^{\downarrow}\colon\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)\mathbin{\hat{\otimes}}\mathcal{O}_{\mu}(D)\rightarrow\mathcal{O}_{\lambda+\mu}(D,V_{\mathbf{k}})
is holomorphically continued for all
(\lambda,\mu)\in\mathbb{C}^{2}
.
We note that the normalization of
\hat{\mathcal{F}}_{\lambda,\mu,\mathbf{k}}^{\downarrow}
is different from (
2.18
) and (
2.19
), that is, we have

\hat{\mathcal{F}}_{\lambda,\mu,\mathbf{k}}^{\downarrow}(f(x-y))=\tilde{C}^{d}(\lambda,\mu,\mathbf{k})\left\langle f(x),\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{k}}(x)\right\rangle_{F,\mathfrak{p}^{+}}\in V_{\mathbf{k}},\qquad f(x)\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}).

Also, when
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
is of tube type, we fix
[K,K]
-isomorphisms
V_{\mathbf{k}+\underline{a}_{r}}\simeq V_{\mathbf{k}}
for each
a\in\mathbb{Z}_{>0}
.
Then as in Proposition
2.6
, by Theorems
8.1
,
8.2
, Propositions
8.3
,
8.6

and Corollary
8.5
, the following hold.

Corollary 8.7
.

For
\lambda,\mu>p-1
and for
f\in\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D)\mathbin{\hat{\otimes}}\mathcal{H}_{\mu}(D)
, we have

\|f\|_{\lambda\otimes\mu}^{2}=\sum_{\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}}\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{\mathbf{k},d}(\mu)_{\mathbf{k},d}\tilde{C}^{d}(\lambda,\mu,\mathbf{k})}\|\hat{\mathcal{F}}_{\lambda,\mu,\mathbf{k}}^{\downarrow}f\|_{\lambda+\mu,\mathbf{k}}^{2},

where
\tilde{C}^{d}(\lambda,\mu,\mathbf{k})
is as in (
8.2
).

Corollary 8.8
.

Let
\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}
.

(1)

For
a_{1},a_{2}=1,2,\dots,r
,

{\rm d}(\tau_{\lambda}\mathbin{\hat{\otimes}}\tau_{\mu})(\mathcal{U}(\mathfrak{g}))\big\{f(x-y)\mid f\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})\big\}\subset M_{a_{1}}^{\mathfrak{g}}(\lambda)\otimes M_{a_{2}}^{\mathfrak{g}}(\mu)

holds if and only if

(\lambda,\mu)\in\left(\frac{d}{2}(a_{1}-1)-k_{a_{1}}-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}\right)\times\left(\frac{d}{2}(a_{2}-1)-k_{a_{2}}-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}\right),

where
M_{a}^{\mathfrak{g}}(\lambda)\subset\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)_{\widetilde{K}}
is the
\bigl(\mathfrak{g},\widetilde{K}\bigr)
-submodule given in (
2.12
).

(2)

Let
\lambda,\mu\in\left\{0,\frac{d}{2},\dots,\frac{d}{2}(r-1)\right\}\cup\left(\frac{d}{2}(r-1),\infty\right)
. If
\min\{\lambda,\mu\}=\frac{d}{2}a
,
a\in\{0,1,\dots,r-1\}
,
then we have

\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D)\mathbin{\hat{\otimes}}\mathcal{H}_{\mu}(D)\simeq\sideset{}{{}^{\oplus}}{\sum}_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}\\
k_{a+1}=0\end{subarray}}\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+\mu}\bigl(D,\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})\bigr).

(3)

For
a=0,1,\dots,r-1
, if
k_{a+1}=0
, then for
\lambda,\mu\in\left\{\frac{d}{2}a,\frac{d}{2}(a+1),\dots,\frac{d}{2}(r-1)\right\}\cup\left(\frac{d}{2}(r-1),\infty\right)
,
the restriction of
\hat{\mathcal{F}}_{\lambda,\mu,\mathbf{k}}^{\downarrow}
gives the symmetry breaking operator

\hat{\mathcal{F}}_{\lambda,\mu,\mathbf{k}}^{\downarrow}\colon\ \mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D)\mathbin{\hat{\otimes}}\mathcal{H}_{\mu}(D)\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+\mu}(D,V_{\mathbf{k}}).

(4)

Suppose
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
is of tube type. For
a=1,2,\dots,k_{r}
, if

\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{k}}(x)=c\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x)^{a}\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{k}-\underline{a}_{r}}(x),

then we have

\displaystyle\hat{\mathcal{F}}_{\frac{n}{r}-a,\mu,\mathbf{k}}^{\downarrow}=c\frac{(\mu)_{\mathbf{k},d}}{(\mu)_{\mathbf{k}-\underline{a}_{r},d}}\hat{\mathcal{F}}_{\frac{n}{r}+a,\mu,\mathbf{k}-\underline{a}_{r}}^{\downarrow}\circ\biggl(\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\right)^{a}\otimes 1\biggr)\colon

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\frac{n}{r}-a}(D)\mathbin{\hat{\otimes}}\mathcal{O}_{\mu}(D)\longrightarrow\mathcal{O}_{\frac{n}{r}-a+\mu}(D,V_{\mathbf{k}})\simeq\mathcal{O}_{\frac{n}{r}+a+\mu}(D,V_{\mathbf{k}-\underline{a}_{r}}),

\displaystyle\hat{\mathcal{F}}_{\lambda,\frac{n}{r}-a,\mathbf{k}}^{\downarrow}=c\frac{(\lambda)_{\mathbf{k},d}}{(\lambda)_{\mathbf{k}-\underline{a}_{r},d}}\hat{\mathcal{F}}_{\lambda,\frac{n}{r}+a,\mathbf{k}-\underline{a}_{r}}^{\downarrow}\circ\biggl(1\otimes\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}\left(-\frac{\partial}{\partial y}\right)^{a}\biggr)\colon

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)\mathbin{\hat{\otimes}}\mathcal{O}_{\frac{n}{r}-a}(D)\longrightarrow\mathcal{O}_{\lambda+\frac{n}{r}-a}(D,V_{\mathbf{k}})\simeq\mathcal{O}_{\lambda+\frac{n}{r}+a}(D,V_{\mathbf{k}-\underline{a}_{r}}).

(5)

Suppose
\hat{\mathcal{F}}_{\lambda_{0},\mu_{0},\mathbf{k}}^{\downarrow}=0
. Then there exist
l_{1},l_{2}\in\mathbb{Z}_{>0}
such that

\lim_{\lambda\to\lambda_{0}}\frac{\hat{\mathcal{F}}_{\lambda,\mu_{0},\mathbf{k}}^{\downarrow}}{(\lambda-\lambda_{0})^{l_{1}}},\,\lim_{\mu\to\mu_{0}}\frac{\hat{\mathcal{F}}_{\lambda_{0},\mu,\mathbf{k}}^{\downarrow}}{(\mu-\mu_{0})^{l_{2}}}\colon\ \mathcal{O}_{\lambda_{0}}(D)\mathbin{\hat{\otimes}}\mathcal{O}_{\mu_{0}}(D)\longrightarrow\mathcal{O}_{\lambda_{0}+\mu_{0}}(D,V_{\mathbf{k}})

converge to non-zero operators. These are linearly independent in
\operatorname{Hom}_{\widetilde{G}}(\mathcal{O}_{\lambda_{0}}(D)\mathbin{\hat{\otimes}}\mathcal{O}_{\mu_{0}}(D),\allowbreak\mathcal{O}_{\lambda_{0}+\mu_{0}}(D,V_{\mathbf{k}}))
, and especially,
this space is at least
2
-dimensional.

Corollary
8.8
(2) and (3) are earlier given in
[
51
, Theorems 3.3 and 4.4]
.
As an example of (5), by (4) we easily get the following.

Corollary 8.9
.

Suppose
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
is of tube type, and let
\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}
. For
a_{1},a_{2}=1,2,\dots,k_{r}
with
a_{1}+a_{2}\geq k_{r}+1
, we have

\displaystyle\lim_{\mu\to\frac{n}{r}-a_{2}}\frac{\hat{\mathcal{F}}_{\frac{n}{r}-a_{1},\mu,\mathbf{k}}^{\downarrow}}{(\mu-a_{1}+\mathbf{k})_{\underline{a_{1}}_{r},d}}=c\hat{\mathcal{F}}_{\frac{n}{r}+a_{1},\frac{n}{r}-a_{2},\mathbf{k}-\underline{a_{1}}_{r}}^{\downarrow}\circ\biggl(\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\right)^{a_{1}}\otimes 1\biggr),

\displaystyle\lim_{\lambda\to\frac{n}{r}-a_{1}}\frac{\hat{\mathcal{F}}_{\lambda,\frac{n}{r}-a_{2},\mathbf{k}}^{\downarrow}}{(\lambda-a_{2}+\mathbf{k})_{\underline{a_{2}}_{r},d}}=c\hat{\mathcal{F}}_{\frac{n}{r}-a_{1},\frac{n}{r}+a_{2},\mathbf{k}-\underline{a_{2}}_{r}}^{\downarrow}\circ\biggl(1\otimes\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}\left(-\frac{\partial}{\partial y}\right)^{a_{2}}\biggr)\colon

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\frac{n}{r}-a_{1}}(D)\mathbin{\hat{\otimes}}\mathcal{O}_{\frac{n}{r}-a_{2}}(D)\longrightarrow\mathcal{O}_{2\frac{n}{r}-a_{1}-a_{2}}(D,V_{\mathbf{k}}),

and these are linearly independent.

8.3
Example:
\boldsymbol{\mathbf{k}=(k,\dots,k)}
case

In this subsection we assume
\mathfrak{p}^{+}=\mathfrak{n}^{+\mathbb{C}}
is of tube type, and consider the case
\mathbf{k}=\underline{k}_{r}=(\underbrace{k,\dots,k}_{r})
,
so that
\mathcal{P}_{\underline{k}_{r}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})=\mathbb{C}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x)^{k}
holds. For
\lambda,\mu\in\mathbb{C}
,
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
, we define the polynomial
RC_{\lambda,\mu,k}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z,w)\in\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+})
by

RC_{\lambda,\mu,k}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z,w):=(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r},d}(\mu)_{\underline{k}_{r},d}\bigl\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x-y)^{k},{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}+(y|\overline{w})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda\otimes\mu,(x,y)}.

This is originally defined for
\operatorname{Re}\lambda,\operatorname{Re}\mu>p-1=\frac{2n}{r}-1
, and holomorphically continued for all
\lambda,\mu\in\mathbb{C}
.
To describe this explicitly, for
\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}
let

\Phi_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x):=\int_{K_{L}}\Delta^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}_{\mathbf{m}}(kx)\,{\rm d}k\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})^{K_{L}},\qquad d_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}:=\dim\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}),

where
K_{L}\subset K\subset G
is the subgroup given in Section
2.2
, which acts on
\mathfrak{n}^{+}
as Jordan algebra automorphisms. Also, for
\alpha,\beta,\gamma\in\mathbb{C}
let

{}_{2}F_{1}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\left(\begin{matrix}\alpha,\beta\\
\gamma\end{matrix};z\right):=\sum_{\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}}\frac{(\alpha)_{\mathbf{m},d}(\beta)_{\mathbf{m},d}}{(\gamma)_{\mathbf{m},d}}\frac{d_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}{\left(\frac{n}{r}\right)_{\mathbf{m},d}}\Phi_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z).

Then the following holds.

Theorem 8.10
.

For
\lambda,\mu\in\mathbb{C}
,
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
z,w\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}
, we have

\displaystyle RC_{\lambda,\mu,k}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z,w)

\displaystyle=(\mu)_{\underline{k}_{r},d}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{k}{}_{2}F_{1}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\left(\begin{matrix}-k,-\lambda-k+\frac{n}{r}\\
\mu\end{matrix};-P\bigl(z^{\mathit{-1/2}}\bigr)w\right)

\displaystyle=(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r},d}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(-w)^{k}{}_{2}F_{1}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\left(\begin{matrix}-k,-\mu-k+\frac{n}{r}\\
\lambda\end{matrix};-P\bigl(w^{\mathit{-1/2}}\bigr)z\right).

Proof.

By
[
13
, Proposition XII.1.3 (ii)]
, we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x-y)^{k}

\displaystyle=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x)^{k}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}\\
m_{1}\leq k\end{subarray}}\frac{(-k)_{\mathbf{m},d}d_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}{\left(\frac{n}{r}\right)_{\mathbf{m},d}}\Phi_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\bigl(P\bigl(x^{\mathit{-1/2}}\bigr)y\bigr)

(8.3)

\displaystyle=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(-y)^{k}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}\\
m_{1}\leq k\end{subarray}}\frac{(-k)_{\mathbf{m},d}d_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}{\left(\frac{n}{r}\right)_{\mathbf{m},d}}\Phi_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\bigl(P\bigl(y^{\mathit{-1/2}}\bigr)x\bigr),

(8.4)

and by
[
13
, Lemma XIV.1.2]
, we have

\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x)^{k}\Phi_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\bigl(P\bigl(x^{\mathit{-1/2}}\bigr)y\bigr)=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(y)^{k}\Phi_{\underline{k}_{r}-\mathbf{m}^{\vee}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\bigl(P\bigl(y^{\mathit{-1/2}}\bigr)x\bigr)\in\mathcal{P}_{\underline{k}_{r}-\mathbf{m}^{\vee}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}),

where
\mathbf{m}^{\vee}:=(m_{r},\dots,m_{1})
. Then by Corollary
2.4
and (
8.3
), we get

\displaystyle RC_{\lambda,\mu,k}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z,w)

\displaystyle=(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r},d}(\mu)_{\underline{k}_{r},d}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}\\
m_{1}\leq k\end{subarray}}\frac{(-k)_{\mathbf{m},d}d_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}{\left(\frac{n}{r}\right)_{\mathbf{m},d}}\bigl\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x)^{k}\Phi_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\bigl(P\bigl(x^{\mathit{-1/2}}\bigr)y\bigr),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}+(y|\overline{w})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda\otimes\mu,(x,y)}

\displaystyle=(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r},d}(\mu)_{\underline{k}_{r},d}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}\\
m_{1}\leq k\end{subarray}}\frac{(-k)_{\mathbf{m},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r}-\mathbf{m}^{\vee},d}(\mu)_{\mathbf{m},d}}\frac{d_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}{\left(\frac{n}{r}\right)_{\mathbf{m},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{k}\Phi_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\bigl(P\bigl(z^{\mathit{-1/2}}\bigr)w\bigr)

\displaystyle=(\mu)_{\underline{k}_{r},d}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{k}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}\\
m_{1}\leq k\end{subarray}}\frac{(-k)_{\mathbf{m},d}\left(-\lambda-k+\frac{n}{r}\right)_{\mathbf{m},d}}{(\mu)_{\mathbf{m},d}}\frac{d_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}{\left(\frac{n}{r}\right)_{\mathbf{m},d}}(-1)^{|\mathbf{m}|}\Phi_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(P(z^{\mathit{-1/2}})w)

\displaystyle=(\mu)_{\underline{k}_{r},d}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{k}{}_{2}F_{1}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\left(\begin{matrix}-k,-\lambda-k+\frac{n}{r}\\
\mu\end{matrix};-P\bigl(z^{\mathit{-1/2}}\bigr)w\right).

The 2nd equality is also proved similarly by using (
8.4
).
∎

For special
\lambda,\mu\in\mathbb{C}
,
RC_{\lambda,\mu,k}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}
is factorized as follows.

Theorem 8.11
.

Let
a=1,2,\dots,k
.

(1)

RC_{\frac{n}{r}-a,\mu,k}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z,w)=(\mu+k-a)_{\underline{a}_{r},d}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{a}RC_{\frac{n}{r}+a,\mu,k-a}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z,w)
.

(2)

RC_{\lambda,\frac{n}{r}-a,k}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z,w)=(\lambda+k-a)_{\underline{a}_{r},d}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(-w)^{a}RC_{\lambda,\frac{n}{r}+a,k-a}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z,w)
.

(3)

Suppose
\lambda+\mu=\frac{n}{r}-2k+a
. Then

\displaystyle RC_{\lambda,\mu,k}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z,w)

\displaystyle=(\mu+k-a)_{\underline{a}_{r},d}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z+w)^{a}RC_{\lambda,\mu,k-a}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z,w)

\displaystyle=(-1)^{ar}(\lambda+k-a)_{\underline{a}_{r},d}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z+w)^{a}RC_{\lambda,\mu,k-a}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z,w).

Proof.

(1) and (2) follow from Theorem
8.2
.

For (3), by
[
13
, Proposition XV.3.4 (ii)]
, we have

\displaystyle RC_{\lambda,\mu,k}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z,w)=(\mu)_{\underline{k}_{r},d}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{k}{}_{2}F_{1}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\left(\begin{matrix}-k,-\lambda-k+\frac{n}{r}\\
\mu\end{matrix};-P\bigl(z^{\mathit{-1/2}}\bigr)w\right)

\displaystyle\quad\qquad{}=(\mu)_{\underline{k}_{r},d}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\bigl(e+P\bigl(z^{\mathit{-1/2}}\bigr)w\bigr)^{\lambda+\mu+2k-\frac{n}{r}}

\displaystyle\quad\qquad{}\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times{}_{2}F_{1}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\left(\begin{matrix}\lambda+\mu+k-\frac{n}{r},\mu+k\\
\mu\end{matrix};-P\bigl(z^{\mathit{-1/2}}\bigr)w\right)

\displaystyle\quad\qquad{}=(\mu)_{\underline{k}_{r},d}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\bigl(e+P\bigl(z^{\mathit{-1/2}}\bigr)w\bigr)^{a}{}_{2}F_{1}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\left(\begin{matrix}-k+a,\mu+k\\
\mu\end{matrix};-P\bigl(z^{\mathit{-1/2}}\bigr)w\right)

\displaystyle\quad\qquad{}=(\mu)_{\underline{k-a}_{r},d}(\mu+k-a)_{\underline{a}_{r},d}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{k-a}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z+w)^{a}

\displaystyle\quad\qquad{}\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times{}_{2}F_{1}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\left(\begin{matrix}-k+a,-\lambda-k+a+\frac{n}{r}\\
\mu\end{matrix};-P\bigl(z^{\mathit{-1/2}}\bigr)w\right)

\displaystyle\quad\qquad{}=(\mu+k-a)_{\underline{a}_{r},d}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z+w)^{a}RC_{\lambda,\mu,k-a}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z,w),

and we have
(\mu+k-a)_{\underline{a}_{r},d}=\left(-\lambda-k+\frac{n}{r}\right)_{\underline{a}_{r},d}=(-1)^{ar}(\lambda+k-a)_{\underline{a}_{r},d}
.
∎

The polynomial
RC_{\lambda,\mu,k}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z,w)
gives the symmetry breaking operator

\displaystyle\mathcal{RC}_{\lambda,\mu,k}^{\downarrow}\colon\ \mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)\mathbin{\hat{\otimes}}\mathcal{O}_{\mu}(D)\longrightarrow\mathcal{O}_{\lambda+\mu+2k}(D),

\displaystyle\bigl(\mathcal{RC}_{\lambda,\mu,k}^{\downarrow}f\bigr)(x):=RC_{\lambda,\mu,k}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial x},\frac{\partial}{\partial y}\right)f(x,y)\biggr|_{y=x},

where we normalize
\frac{\partial}{\partial x}
with respect to the bilinear form
(\cdot|\cdot)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}=(\cdot|Q(\overline{e})\cdot)_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}
on
\mathfrak{p}^{+}=\mathfrak{n}^{+\mathbb{C}}
.
We note that
\mathcal{O}_{\lambda+\mu}(D,\mathcal{P}_{\underline{k}_{r}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}))\simeq\mathcal{O}_{\lambda+\mu+2k}(D)
holds if
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
is of tube type.
When
\mathfrak{p}^{+}=\mathbb{C}
,
{G={\rm SL}(2,\mathbb{R})}
, this is proportional to the Rankin–Cohen bidifferential operator
(see, e.g.,
[
8
,
53
]
and
[
33
, Sections 8 and 9]
). For general
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
see also, e.g.,
[
6
,
7
,
49
,
50
]
.
In our normalization we have the following.

Proposition 8.12
.

\displaystyle\mathcal{RC}_{\lambda,\mu,k}^{\downarrow}\bigl(\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x-y)^{k}\bigr)=\left(\lambda+\mu+k-\frac{n}{r}\right)_{\underline{k}_{r},d}\left(\frac{n}{r}\right)_{\underline{k}_{r},d}
.

Proof.

As in Proposition
2.6
(2), by Corollary
8.5
we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{RC}_{\lambda,\mu,k}^{\downarrow}\bigl(\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x-y)^{k}\bigr)

\displaystyle=(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r},d}(\mu)_{\underline{k}_{r},d}\big\|\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x-y)^{k}\big\|_{\lambda\otimes\mu,(x,y)}^{2}

\displaystyle=\tilde{C}^{d}(\lambda,\mu,\underline{k}_{r})\big\|\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x)^{k}\big\|_{F,\mathfrak{p}^{+}}^{2},

and we have
