\tilde{C}^{d}(\lambda,\mu,\underline{k}_{r})=\left(\lambda+\mu+k-\frac{n}{r}\right)_{\underline{k}_{r},d},\qquad\big\|\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x)^{k}\big\|_{F,\mathfrak{p}^{+}}^{2}=\left(\frac{n}{r}\right)_{\underline{k}_{r},d}

(for the 2nd equality see
[
13
, Proposition XI.4.1 (ii)]
). Hence we get the proposition.
∎

Next we consider the zeroes of
RC_{\lambda,\mu,k}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}
. If this vanishes at
(\lambda,\mu)
, then by Corollary
8.8
(4),

\operatorname{Hom}_{\widetilde{G}}(\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)\mathbin{\hat{\otimes}}\mathcal{O}_{\mu}(D),\mathcal{O}_{\lambda+\mu+2k}(D))
is at least 2-dimensional. More precisely, the following holds.

Theorem 8.13
.

For
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 1}
,
1\leq i,j\leq r
, let

Z_{i,j}^{k,d}:=\left\{\left(\frac{d}{2}(i-1)+1-a_{1},\frac{d}{2}(j-1)+1-a_{2}\right)\ \middle|\ \begin{matrix}a_{1},a_{2}\in\mathbb{Z},\,1\leq a_{1},a_{2}\leq k,\\
a_{1}+a_{2}\geq k+1\end{matrix}\right\}.

(1)

RC_{\lambda,\mu,k}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}=0
holds if

(\lambda,\mu)\in\bigcup_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq i,j\leq r\\
i+j\geq r+1\end{subarray}}Z_{i,j}^{k,d}.

(2)

For
(\lambda,\mu)
in the above set, let

\displaystyle l:=\min\big\{i+j\,\big|\,1\leq i,j\leq r,\,i+j\geq r+1,\,(\lambda,\mu)\in Z_{i,j}^{k,d}\big\},

\displaystyle\alpha:=\#\big\{(i,j)\in\{1,\dots,r\}^{2}\,\big|\,i+j=l,\,(\lambda,\mu)\in Z_{i,j}^{k,d}\big\}.

Then we have

\dim\operatorname{Hom}_{\widetilde{G}}(\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)\mathbin{\hat{\otimes}}\mathcal{O}_{\mu}(D),\mathcal{O}_{\lambda+\mu+2k}(D))\geq\alpha+1.

Proof.

For
1\leq i,j\leq r
, let
Z_{i,j}^{\prime}:=Z_{r-i+1,j}^{k,d}
, so that we have

\bigcup_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq i,j\leq r\\
i+j\geq r+1\end{subarray}}Z_{i,j}^{k,d}=\bigcup_{1\leq i\leq j\leq r}Z_{i,j}^{\prime}.

(1) For
\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}
,
m_{1}\leq k
, the coefficient of
\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{k}\Phi_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\bigl(P\bigl(z^{\mathit{-1/2}}\bigr)w\bigr)
in
RC_{\lambda,\mu,k}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z,w)
is given by

\displaystyle F_{\mathbf{m}}(\lambda,\mu):=(-1)^{kr}(-k)_{\mathbf{m},d}\left(-\lambda-k+\frac{n}{r}\right)_{\mathbf{m},d}\left(-\mu-k+\frac{n}{r}\right)_{\underline{k}_{r}-\mathbf{m}^{\vee},d}\frac{d_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}{\left(\frac{n}{r}\right)_{\mathbf{m},d}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{(-k)_{\mathbf{m},d}\,d_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}{(-1)^{kr}\left(\frac{n}{r}\right)_{\mathbf{m},d}}\prod_{i=1}^{r}\left(-\lambda-k+1+\frac{d}{2}(r-i)\right)_{m_{i}}\prod_{j=1}^{r}\left(-\mu-k+1+\frac{d}{2}(j-1)\right)_{k-m_{j}}.

We fix
(\lambda_{0},\mu_{0})\in\mathbb{C}^{2}
. For
1\leq i,j\leq r
, let
\frac{d}{2}(r-i)+1-\lambda_{0}=:a_{i}^{(1)}
,
\frac{d}{2}(j-1)+1-\mu_{0}=:a_{j}^{(2)}
. If
a_{i}^{(1)}\in\{1,\dots,k\}
, then

\left(-\lambda_{0}-k+1+\frac{d}{2}(r-i)\right)_{m_{i}}=\bigl(-k+a_{i}^{(1)}\bigr)_{m_{i}}=0\quad\text{holds if}\quad k-a_{i}^{(1)}+1\leq m_{i}\leq k.

Similarly, if
a_{j}^{(2)}\in\{1,\dots,k\}
, then

\left(-\mu_{0}-k+1+\frac{d}{2}(j-1)\right)_{k-m_{j}}=\bigl(-k+a_{j}^{(2)}\bigr)_{k-m_{j}}=0\quad\text{holds if}\quad 0\leq m_{j}<a_{j}^{(2)}.

If
(\lambda_{0},\mu_{0})\in Z_{i,j}^{\prime}
holds for some
1\leq i\leq j\leq r
, then we have
k-a_{i}^{(1)}+1\leq a_{j}^{(2)}
,
and at least one of the two above formulas vanishes for
k\geq m_{i}\geq m_{j}\geq 0
.
Hence
F_{\mathbf{m}}(\lambda_{0},\mu_{0})
vanishes for all
\mathbf{m}
, and
RC_{\lambda,\mu,k}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}=0
holds.

(2) Again we fix
(\lambda_{0},\mu_{0})
, define
a_{i}^{(1)}
,
a_{j}^{(2)}
as above, and let

\displaystyle\{i\in\{1,\dots,r\}\mid a^{(1)}_{i}\in\{1,\dots,k\}\}=:\{i(1),\dots,i(r^{\prime})\},

\displaystyle i(1)<\cdots<i(r^{\prime}),

\displaystyle\{j\in\{1,\dots,r\}\mid a^{(2)}_{j}\in\{1,\dots,k\}\}=:\{j(1),\dots,j(r^{\prime\prime})\},\qquad

\displaystyle j(1)<\cdots<j(r^{\prime\prime}).

Then for
0\leq u\leq r^{\prime}
,
0\leq v\leq r^{\prime\prime}
,
F_{\mathbf{m}}(\lambda,\mu)
has a zero of order
u
at
\lambda=\lambda_{0}
and of order
r^{\prime\prime}-v
at
\mu=\mu_{0}
if

\mathbf{m}\in W(u,v):=\left\{\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}\,\middle|\,\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}0\leq m_{j}\leq k,\ j=1,\dots,r,\\
\begin{array}[]{@{}ll@{}}m_{i(u)}\geq k-a_{i(u)}^{(1)}+1,&m_{i(u+1)}\leq k-a_{i(u+1)}^{(1)},\\
m_{j(v)}\geq a_{j(v)}^{(2)},&m_{j(v+1)}\leq a_{j(v+1)}^{(2)}-1\end{array}\end{array}\right\},

where we ignore the conditions for
m_{i(0)}
,
m_{i(r^{\prime}+1)}
,
m_{j(0)}
,
m_{j(r^{\prime\prime}+1)}
.
For
\mathbf{m}\in W(u,v)
, let

\hat{F}_{\mathbf{m}}(\lambda,\mu):=(\lambda-\lambda_{0})^{-u}(\mu-\mu_{0})^{-r^{\prime\prime}+v}F_{\mathbf{m}}(\lambda,\mu),

so that
\hat{F}_{\mathbf{m}}(\lambda_{0},\mu_{0})\neq 0
holds. Then we have

\displaystyle RC_{\lambda,\mu,k}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z,w)=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}\\
m_{1}\leq k\end{subarray}}F_{\mathbf{m}}(\lambda,\mu)\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{k}\Phi_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\bigl(P\bigl(z^{\mathit{-1/2}}\bigr)w\bigr)

\displaystyle\hphantom{RC_{\lambda,\mu,k}^{\mathfrak{n}^+}(z,w)}{}=\!\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leq u\leq r^{\prime}\\
0\leq v\leq r^{\prime\prime}\end{subarray}}\sum_{\mathbf{m}\in W(u,v)}\!\!(\lambda-\lambda_{0})^{u}(\mu-\mu_{0})^{r^{\prime\prime}-v}\hat{F}_{\mathbf{m}}(\lambda,\mu)\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{k}\Phi_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\bigl(P\bigl(z^{\mathit{-1/2}}\bigr)w\bigr).

Now let

l^{\prime}:=\max\left\{u-v\,\middle|\,\begin{matrix}1\leq u\leq r^{\prime},\,1\leq v\leq r^{\prime\prime},\\
i(u)\leq j(v),\,a_{i(u)}^{(1)}+a_{j(v)}^{(2)}\geq k+1\end{matrix}\right\}.

If
u-v\leq l^{\prime}-1
, then since
i(u+1)\leq j(v)
and
k-a_{i(u+1)}^{(1)}+1\leq a_{j(v)}^{(2)}
we have
W(u,v)=\varnothing
.
Then for
(s,t)\in\mathbb{C}^{2}
, we can take the limit

\displaystyle\lim_{\nu\to 0}\frac{1}{\nu^{l^{\prime}+r^{\prime\prime}}}RC_{\lambda_{0}+\nu s,\mu_{0}+\nu t,k}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z,w)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\lim_{\nu\to 0}\sum_{u-v\geq l^{\prime}}\sum_{\mathbf{m}\in W(u,v)}\frac{(\nu s)^{u}(\nu t)^{r^{\prime\prime}-v}}{\nu^{l^{\prime}+r^{\prime\prime}}}\hat{F}_{\mathbf{m}}(\lambda_{0}+\nu s,\mu_{0}+\nu t)\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{k}\Phi_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(P(z^{\mathit{-1/2}})w)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\sum_{u-v=l^{\prime}}s^{u}t^{r^{\prime\prime}-v}\sum_{\mathbf{m}\in W(u,v)}\hat{F}_{\mathbf{m}}(\lambda_{0},\mu_{0})\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{k}\Phi_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(P(z^{\mathit{-1/2}})w).

By varying
(s,t)
, we get
\alpha+1
linearly independent polynomials. By substituting
(z,w)=\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial x},\frac{\partial}{\partial y}\right)
and restricting to
y=x
,
these polynomials give symmetry breaking operators in
\operatorname{Hom}_{\widetilde{G}}(\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)\mathbin{\hat{\otimes}}\mathcal{O}_{\mu}(D),\mathcal{O}_{\lambda+\mu+2k}(D))
, and hence this space is at least
(\alpha+1)
-dimensional.
∎

Example 8.14
.

(1)

Suppose
d\in 2\mathbb{Z}
,
k\geq\frac{n}{r}=\frac{d}{2}(r-1)+1
. If

(\lambda,\mu)\in\bigcap_{j=1}^{r}Z_{r-j+1,j}^{k,d}=\left\{(\lambda,\mu)\in\mathbb{Z}^{2}\ \middle|\ \begin{matrix}\frac{n}{r}-k\leq\lambda,\mu\leq 0,\\
\lambda+\mu\leq\frac{n}{r}-k\end{matrix}\right\},

then we have

\dim\operatorname{Hom}_{\widetilde{G}}(\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)\mathbin{\hat{\otimes}}\mathcal{O}_{\mu}(D),\mathcal{O}_{\lambda+\mu+2k}(D))\geq r+1.

(2)

Suppose
d=1
, i.e.,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}=\operatorname{Sym}(r,\mathbb{C})
, and let
\delta\in\{0,1\}
. For
k\geq\left\lfloor\frac{r+\delta}{2}\right\rfloor
, if

\displaystyle(\lambda,\mu)\in

\displaystyle\bigcap_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq j\leq r\\
j\in 2\mathbb{Z}+\delta\end{subarray}}Z_{r-j+1,j}^{k,1}

\displaystyle=\left\{(\lambda,\mu)\in\left(\mathbb{Z}+\frac{r+\delta}{2}\right)\times\left(\mathbb{Z}+\frac{1-\delta}{2}\right)\ \middle|\ \begin{matrix}\frac{1}{2}r-k\leq\lambda,\mu\leq\frac{1}{2},\\
\lambda+\mu\leq\frac{1}{2}(r+1)-k\end{matrix}\right\},

then we have

\dim\operatorname{Hom}_{\widetilde{G}}(\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)\mathbin{\hat{\otimes}}\mathcal{O}_{\mu}(D),\mathcal{O}_{\lambda+\mu+2k}(D))\geq\left\lfloor\frac{r+\delta}{2}\right\rfloor+1.

Similarly, for
k\geq\left\lfloor\frac{r-\delta}{2}\right\rfloor
, if

\displaystyle(\lambda,\mu)\in

\displaystyle\bigcap_{\begin{subarray}{c}2\leq j\leq r\\
j\in 2\mathbb{Z}+\delta\end{subarray}}Z_{r-j+2,j}^{k,1}

\displaystyle=\left\{(\lambda,\mu)\in\left(\mathbb{Z}+\frac{r+1+\delta}{2}\right)\times\left(\mathbb{Z}+\frac{1-\delta}{2}\right)\ \middle|\ \begin{matrix}\frac{1}{2}r-k\leq\lambda,\mu\leq 1,\\
\lambda+\mu\leq\frac{1}{2}r+1-k\end{matrix}\right\},

then we have

\dim\operatorname{Hom}_{\widetilde{G}}(\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)\mathbin{\hat{\otimes}}\mathcal{O}_{\mu}(D),\mathcal{O}_{\lambda+\mu+2k}(D))\geq\left\lfloor\frac{r-\delta}{2}\right\rfloor+1.

(3)

Suppose
d=2
, i.e.,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}={\rm M}(r,\mathbb{C})
. For
\alpha=1,2,\dots,\min\{k,r\}
, if

(\lambda,\mu)\in\left\{(\lambda,\mu)\in\mathbb{Z}^{2}\,\middle|\,\begin{matrix}\alpha-k\leq\lambda,\mu\leq r-\alpha,\\
r+\alpha-2k\leq\lambda+\mu\leq 2r-\alpha-k\end{matrix}\right\},

then we have

\dim\operatorname{Hom}_{\widetilde{G}}(\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)\mathbin{\hat{\otimes}}\mathcal{O}_{\mu}(D),\mathcal{O}_{\lambda+\mu+2k}(D))\geq\alpha+1.

When
\mathfrak{p}^{+}=\mathbb{C}
, it is proved in
[
33
, Theorem 9.1]
by the F-method that
\operatorname{Hom}_{\widetilde{G}}(\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)\mathbin{\hat{\otimes}}\mathcal{O}_{\mu}(D),\allowbreak\mathcal{O}_{\lambda+\mu+2k}(D))
is precisely 2-dimensional
if
(\lambda,\mu)\in Z_{1,1}^{k,d}
, and precisely 1-dimensional otherwise. For general
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
, we need further study to determine the precise dimension of this space.
Also, for
(\lambda,\mu)\in Z_{r,r}^{k,d}\cup Z_{r,1}^{k,d}\cup Z_{1,r}^{k,d}
, we can consider an analogue of
[
33
, Theorem 9.2]
as follows.
We note that the result on linear independence for
\frac{n}{r}\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 2}
case differs from
\mathfrak{p}^{+}=\mathbb{C}
case.

Theorem 8.15
.

(1)

If
\frac{n}{r}-\lambda_{0}=:a_{1}\in\{1,2,\dots,k\}
, then we have

\lim_{\mu\to\mu_{0}}\frac{\mathcal{RC}_{\frac{n}{r}-a_{1},\mu,k}^{\downarrow}}{(\mu+k-a_{1})_{\underline{a_{1}}_{r},d}}=\mathcal{RC}_{\frac{n}{r}+a_{1},\mu_{0},k-a_{1}}^{\downarrow}\circ\biggl(\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\right)^{a_{1}}\otimes 1\biggr).

(8.5)

(2)

If
\frac{n}{r}-\mu_{0}=:a_{2}\in\{1,2,\dots,k\}
, then we have

\lim_{\lambda\to\lambda_{0}}\frac{\mathcal{RC}_{\lambda,\frac{n}{r}-a_{2},k}^{\downarrow}}{(\lambda+k-a_{2})_{\underline{a_{2}}_{r},d}}=\mathcal{RC}_{\lambda_{0},\frac{n}{r}+a_{2},k-a_{2}}^{\downarrow}\circ\biggl(1\otimes\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\left(-\frac{\partial}{\partial y}\right)^{a_{2}}\biggr).

(8.6)

(3)

If
\lambda_{0}+\mu_{0}-\frac{n}{r}+2k=:a_{3}\in\{1,2,\dots,k\}
, then we have

\lim_{\nu\to 0}\frac{\mathcal{RC}_{\lambda_{0}-\nu,\mu_{0}+\nu,k}^{\downarrow}}{(\mu_{0}+\nu+k-a_{3})_{\underline{a_{3}}_{r},d}}=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\right)^{a_{3}}\circ\mathcal{RC}_{\lambda_{0},\mu_{0},k-a_{3}}^{\downarrow}.

(8.7)

(4)

If
(\lambda_{0},\mu_{0})\in Z_{r,r}^{k,d}
, then (
8.5
) and (
8.6
) are linearly independent.

(5)

If
(\lambda_{0},\mu_{0})\in Z_{r,1}^{k,d}
, then (
8.5
) and (
8.7
) are linearly independent.

(6)

If
(\lambda_{0},\mu_{0})\in Z_{1,r}^{k,d}
, then (
8.6
) and (
8.7
) are linearly independent.

(7)

Suppose
\frac{n}{r}\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 2}
. If
(\lambda_{0},\mu_{0})\in Z_{r,r}^{k,d}\cap\bigl(Z_{r,1}^{k,d}\cup Z_{1,r}^{k,d}\bigr)
,
then (
8.5
), (
8.6
) and (
8.7
) are linearly independent.

Proof.

(1), (2), (3) follow from Theorem
8.11
.
(4) follows from Corollary
8.9
.

For (5), we have

\displaystyle RC_{\lambda,\mu,k}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z,w)

\displaystyle=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{k}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}\\
m_{1}\leq k\end{subarray}}(-k)_{\mathbf{m},d}\left(-\lambda-k+\frac{n}{r}\right)_{\mathbf{m},d}(\mu+\mathbf{m})_{\underline{k}_{r}-\mathbf{m},d}\frac{d_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}{\left(\frac{n}{r}\right)_{\mathbf{m},d}}\Phi_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\bigl(-P\bigl(z^{\mathit{-1/2}}\bigr)w\bigr).

Put
\frac{n}{r}-\lambda_{0}=:a_{1}\in\{1,\dots,k\}
,
1-\mu_{0}=:a_{2}^{\prime}\in\{1,\dots,k\}
, so that
a_{3}=2k-a_{1}-a_{2}^{\prime}+1\in\{1,\dots,k\}
. Then we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-k}\lim_{\mu\to 1-a_{2}^{\prime}}\frac{RC_{\frac{n}{r}-a_{1},\mu,k}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z,w)}{(\mu+k-a_{1})_{\underline{a_{1}}_{r},d}}\\
&=\lim_{\mu\to 1-a_{2}^{\prime}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}\\
m_{1}\leq k-a_{1}\end{subarray}}(-k)_{\mathbf{m},d}(a_{1}-k)_{\mathbf{m},d}(\mu+\mathbf{m})_{\underline{k-a_{1}}_{r}-\mathbf{m},d}\frac{d_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}{\left(\frac{n}{r}\right)_{\mathbf{m},d}}\Phi_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\bigl(-P\bigl(z^{\mathit{-1/2}}\bigr)w\bigr)\\
&=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}\\
m_{1}\leq k-a_{1}\end{subarray}}(-k)_{\mathbf{m},d}(a_{1}-k)_{\mathbf{m},d}(1-a_{2}^{\prime}+\mathbf{m})_{\underline{k-a_{1}}_{r}-\mathbf{m},d}\frac{d_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}{\left(\frac{n}{r}\right)_{\mathbf{m},d}}\Phi_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\bigl(-P\bigl(z^{\mathit{-1/2}}\bigr)w\bigr),\end{split}

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-k}\lim_{\nu\to 0}\frac{RC_{\frac{n}{r}-a_{1}-\nu,1-a_{2}^{\prime}+\nu,k}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z,w)}{(\nu-k+a_{1})_{\underline{2k-a_{1}-a_{2}^{\prime}+1}_{r},d}}\\
&=\lim_{\nu\to 0}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}\\
m_{1}\leq k\end{subarray}}(-k)_{\mathbf{m},d}\frac{(\nu+a_{1}-k)_{\mathbf{m},d}(\nu+1-a_{2}^{\prime}+\mathbf{m})_{\underline{k}_{r}-\mathbf{m},d}}{(\nu-k+a_{1})_{\underline{2k-a_{1}-a_{2}^{\prime}+1}_{r},d}}\frac{d_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}{\left(\frac{n}{r}\right)_{\mathbf{m},d}}\Phi_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\bigl(-P\bigl(z^{\mathit{-1/2}}\bigr)w\bigr)\\
&=\lim_{\nu\to 0}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}\\
m_{1}\leq k\end{subarray}}(-k)_{\mathbf{m},d}(\nu+1-a_{2}^{\prime}+\mathbf{m})_{\underline{a_{1}+a_{2}^{\prime}-k-1}_{r},d}\frac{d_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}{\left(\frac{n}{r}\right)_{\mathbf{m},d}}\Phi_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\bigl(-P\bigl(z^{\mathit{-1/2}}\bigr)w\bigr)\\
&=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}\\
m_{1}\leq k\end{subarray}}(-k)_{\mathbf{m},d}(1-a_{2}^{\prime}+\mathbf{m})_{\underline{a_{1}+a_{2}^{\prime}-k-1}_{r},d}\frac{d_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}{\left(\frac{n}{r}\right)_{\mathbf{m},d}}\Phi_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\bigl(-P\bigl(z^{\mathit{-1/2}}\bigr)w\bigr).\end{split}

Then the coefficient for
\mathbf{m}=(k,\underline{0}_{r-1})
is zero for the former formula, and is non-zero for the latter one, and hence these are linearly independent.

(6) Proved similarly by comparing the coefficients for
\mathbf{m}=(\underline{k}_{r-1},0)
.

(7) By the assumption
\frac{n}{r}\geq 2
, we have
r\geq 2
, and thus
\underline{k}_{r}
,
(k,\underline{0}_{r-1})
,
\underline{0}_{r}

(for
(\lambda_{0},\mu_{0})\in Z_{r,r}^{k,d}\cap Z_{r,1}^{k,d}
case)
or
\underline{k}_{r}
,
(\underline{k}_{r-1},0)
,
\underline{0}_{r}
(for
(\lambda_{0},\mu_{0})\in Z_{r,r}^{k,d}\cap Z_{1,r}^{k,d}
case) are distinct.
Hence, comparing the coefficients for these
\mathbf{m}
, we get the linear independence of three formulas.
∎
